            className="bg-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-700 text-white font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-xs px-6 py-3.5 rounded-xl cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap shadow-sm transition-colors"
          >
            {isEn ? "Complete Now" : "Preencher Agora"} &rarr;
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* CABEÇALHO */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between items-start sm:items-end gap-4">
        <div>
          <span className="inline-block text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-[#0E3A68] bg-blue-50 border border-blue-200 px-3 py-1 rounded-full mb-2">
            {isEn ? "Family Area" : "Espaço da Família"}
          </span>
          <h1 className="text-3xl md:text-4xl font-black text-slate-900 m-0 tracking-tight">
            {isEn
              ? `Hello, ${perfilPai?.nome_completo?.split(" ")[0] || "Parent"}!`
              : `Olá, ${
                  perfilPai?.nome_completo?.split(" ")[0] || "Encarregado(a)"
                }!`}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 mt-1 text-sm md:text-base font-medium mb-0">
            {isEn
              ? "Manage your children's camp bookings, payments, brochures, and reviews."
              : "Gira as inscrições dos seus filhos, pagamentos, brochuras e avaliações."}
          </p>
        </div>

        <Link
          href={`/${lang}/pesquisa`}
          className="bg-[#0E3A68] hover:bg-[#0b2b4d] text-white font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-xs px-6 py-3.5 rounded-xl no-underline shadow-md transition-all"
        >
          + {isEn ? "Explore New Camps" : "Explorar Novos Campos"}
        </Link>
      </div>

      {/* SECÇÃO 1: AS MINHAS RESERVAS */}
      <section>
        <h2 className="text-lg font-black text-slate-900 uppercase tracking-widest mb-5">
          🎟️ {isEn ? "My Bookings & Enrollments" : "As Minhas Inscrições"} (
          {reservas.length})
        </h2>

        {reservas.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 p-12 text-center shadow-sm">
            <span className="text-5xl block mb-4">🏕️</span>
            <h3 className="text-xl font-black text-slate-900 m-0 mb-2">
              {isEn
                ? "You have no active bookings yet"
                : "Ainda não tem reservas registadas"}
            </h3>
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 max-w-md mx-auto mb-6">
              {isEn
                ? "Discover verified summer camps, sports clinics, and language programs for your children."
                : "Descubra centenas de campos de férias, cursos de surf e programas internacionais verificados."}
            </p>
            <Link
              href={`/${lang}/pesquisa`}
              className="inline-block bg-[#e2a41d] hover:bg-[#d4991b] text-slate-900 font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-xs px-8 py-4 rounded-xl no-underline shadow-md"
            >
              {isEn ? "Find a Camp" : "Procurar Campos de Férias"}
            </Link>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
            {reservas.map((res) => {
              const nomeCampo =
                isEn && res.campos?.nome_en
                  ? res.campos.nome_en
                  : res.campos?.nome || "Programa HelloCamp";
              const nomeCrianca =
                res.criancas?.nome ||
                res.respostas_customizadas?.nome_crianca ||
                "Participante";
              const imgUrl = res.campos?.imagem?.startsWith("http")
                ? res.campos.imagem
                : "/og-image.jpg";

              // Calcula se há valor em falta (2ª Tranche de 50% OU Extra lançado pelo Parceiro!)
              const valorFaltaCobrar =
                Number(res.valor_em_falta) > 0
                  ? Number(res.valor_em_falta)
                  : Number(res.valor_pendente_extra) > 0
                  ? Number(res.valor_pendente_extra)
                  : res.status_pagamento === "Sinal Pago"
                  ? Number(res.valor_total) - Number(res.valor_pago)
                  : 0;

              const temPedidoPendente =
                !!res.respostas_customizadas?.pedido_pai_pendente;
              const isReembolsado = res.status_pagamento === "Reembolsado";

              return (
                <div
                  key={res.id}
                  className={`bg-white rounded-3xl border overflow-hidden shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-all flex flex-col ${
                    valorFaltaCobrar > 0 && !isReembolsado
                      ? "border-amber-300"
                      : "border-slate-200"
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="h-44 bg-slate-100 relative overflow-hidden">
                    <img
                      src={imgUrl}
                      alt={nomeCampo}
                      className="w-full h-full object-cover"
                    />
                    <div className="absolute top-3 left-3 flex gap-2">
                      <span
                        className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest shadow-sm ${
                          isReembolsado
                            ? "bg-red-600 text-white"
                            : res.status_pagamento === "Pago"
                            ? "bg-emerald-600 text-white"
                            : "bg-amber-400 text-slate-900"
                        }`}
                      >
                        {res.status_pagamento}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <span className="absolute top-3 right-3 bg-slate-900/80 backdrop-blur-sm text-white text-[10px] font-black px-3 py-1 rounded-full">
                      Ref: #{res.id.split("-")[0]}
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <div className="p-6 flex-1 flex flex-col">
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400 mb-1">
                      📍 {res.campos?.local || res.campos?.Distrito || "Portugal"}
                    </span>
                    <h3 className="text-xl font-black text-slate-900 m-0 mb-2">
                      {nomeCampo}
                    </h3>

                    <div className="bg-slate-50 p-3.5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 mb-4 space-y-1 text-xs">
                      <p className="m-0 font-bold text-slate-800">
                        👦 {isEn ? "Participant:" : "Participante:"}{" "}
                        <span className="text-[#0E3A68] font-black">
                          {nomeCrianca}
                        </span>
                      </p>
                      <p className="m-0 font-bold text-slate-600">
                        🗓️ {isEn ? "Turn / Package:" : "Turno / Pacote:"}{" "}
                        {res.turno_nome}
                      </p>
                      <p className="m-0 font-bold text-slate-600">
                        💶 {isEn ? "Total Value:" : "Valor Total:"}{" "}
                        <span className="font-black text-slate-900">
                          {Number(res.valor_total || 0).toFixed(2)}€
                        </span>
                      </p>
                    </div>

                    {/* ALERTA DE PAGAMENTO PENDENTE (2ª TRANCHE OU EXTRA APROVADO) */}
                    {valorFaltaCobrar > 0 && !isReembolsado && (
                      <div className="bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 p-4 rounded-2xl mb-4 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                        <div>
                          <span className="block text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-amber-800">
                            {res.status_pagamento ===
                            "Aguardando Pagamento Extra"
                              ? isEn
                                ? "Extra Approved by Organizer"
                                : "Extra Aprovado pelo Organizador"
                              : isEn
                              ? "Remaining 50% Balance"
                              : "2.ª Tranche Pendente (50%)"}
                          </span>
                          <span className="text-lg font-black text-amber-950">
                            {valorFaltaCobrar.toFixed(2)}€
                          </span>
                        </div>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          disabled={loadingStripe === res.id}
                          onClick={() =>
                            handlePagarRestante(res, valorFaltaCobrar)
                          }
                          className="bg-[#167524] hover:bg-[#115a1b] text-white font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-[11px] px-5 py-3 rounded-xl cursor-pointer shadow-sm"
                        >
                          {loadingStripe === res.id
                            ? "..."
                            : isEn
                            ? "Pay Now"
                            : "Liquidar Agora"}
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    )}

                    {temPedidoPendente && (
                      <div className="bg-blue-50 border border-blue-200 p-3 rounded-xl mb-4 text-xs font-bold text-blue-900">
                        ⏳{" "}
                        {isEn
                          ? "Change request sent to organizer:"
                          : "Pedido de alteração em análise pelo campo:"}{" "}
                        "{res.respostas_customizadas.pedido_pai_pendente}"
                      </div>
                    )}

                    <div className="mt-auto pt-4 border-t border-slate-100 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setReservaModal(res)}
                        className="flex-1 bg-slate-900 hover:bg-[#0E3A68] text-white font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-[11px] py-3 px-4 rounded-xl cursor-pointer transition-colors"
                      >
                        📋{" "}
                        {isEn
                          ? "Manage / Details"
                          : "Gerir Inscrição / Detalhes"}
                      </button>

                      {!isReembolsado && (
                        <Link
                          href={`/${lang}/cliente/avaliar/${res.id}`}
                          className="bg-amber-100 hover:bg-amber-200 text-amber-900 font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-[11px] py-3 px-4 rounded-xl no-underline transition-colors flex items-center justify-center"
                        >
                          ★ {isEn ? "Review" : "Avaliar"}
                        </Link>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}
      </section>

      {/* SECÇÃO 2: LISTAS PARTILHÁVEIS (WISHLISTS) */}
      {wishlists.length > 0 && (
        <section className="pt-6 border-t border-slate-200">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-black text-slate-900 uppercase tracking-widest mb-4">
            ❤️ {isEn ? "My Shared Wishlists" : "As Minhas Listas de Favoritos"}
          </h2>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
            {wishlists.map((w) => (
              <div
                key={w.id}
                className="bg-white p-5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 shadow-sm flex items-center justify-between gap-3"
              >
                <div>
                  <h4 className="m-0 text-sm font-black text-slate-900">
                    {w.nome}
                  </h4>
                  <Link
                    href={`/${lang}/lista/${w.token_partilha}`}
                    target="_blank"
                    className="text-[11px] font-bold text-[#0E3A68] hover:underline"
                  >
                    {isEn ? "View Public List ↗" : "Ver Lista Pública ↗"}
                  </Link>
                </div>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => abrirModalPartilha(w.token_partilha, w.nome)}
                  className="bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-800 text-xs font-black px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl cursor-pointer"
                >
                  🔗 {isEn ? "Share" : "Partilhar"}
                </button>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </section>
      )}

      {/* SECÇÃO 3: SUGESTÕES PARA AS PRÓXIMAS FÉRIAS */}
      {sugestoesAzuis.length > 0 && (
        <section className="pt-6 border-t border-slate-200">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-black text-slate-900 uppercase tracking-widest mb-5">
            ✨ {isEn ? "Recommended Programs" : "Sugestões para as Próximas Férias"}
          </h2>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
            {sugestoesAzuis.map((c) => {
              const nome = isEn && c.nome_en ? c.nome_en : c.nome;
              return (
                <Link
                  key={c.id}
                  href={`/${lang}/campo/${c.id}`}
                  className="bg-white rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-all no-underline text-inherit flex flex-col group"
                >
                  <div className="h-40 bg-slate-100 overflow-hidden">
                    <img
                      src={c.imagem || "/og-image.jpg"}
                      alt={nome}
                      className="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform duration-500"
                    />
                  </div>
                  <div className="p-5 flex-1 flex flex-col justify-between">
                    <div>
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase text-slate-400">
                        📍 {c.local || "Portugal"}
                      </span>
                      <h4 className="m-0 mt-1 text-base font-black text-slate-900">
                        {nome}
                      </h4>
                    </div>
                    <div className="mt-4 pt-3 border-t border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between">
                      <span className="text-lg font-black text-[#0E3A68]">
                        {c.preco > 0 ? `${c.preco}€` : "--"}
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-xs font-black text-[#e2a41d]">
                        {isEn ? "Explore" : "Ver Datas"} &rarr;
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </Link>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </section>
      )}

      {/* =================================================================== */}
      {/* MODAL DE DETALHES DA RESERVA, BROCHURAS PDF, EXTRAS E CANCELAMENTO  */}
      {/* =================================================================== */}
      {reservaModal && (
        <div
          className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4"
          onClick={fecharReservaModal}
        >
          <div
            className="bg-white rounded-3xl w-full max-w-2xl max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto p-6 md:p-8 shadow-2xl border border-slate-200"
            onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
          >
            <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4 pb-5 border-b border-slate-100">
              <div>
                <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-[#0E3A68] bg-blue-50 px-3 py-1 rounded-full">
                  Reserva #{reservaModal.id.split("-")[0]}
                </span>
                <h2 className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-900 m-0 mt-2">
                  {isEn && reservaModal.campos?.nome_en
                    ? reservaModal.campos.nome_en
                    : reservaModal.campos?.nome}
                </h2>
                <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 m-0 mt-1">
                  👦 {reservaModal.criancas?.nome || "Participante"} • 🗓️{" "}
                  {reservaModal.turno_nome}
                </p>
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={fecharReservaModal}
                className="w-9 h-9 rounded-full bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 font-black cursor-pointer"
              >
                ✕
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="py-6 space-y-6">
              {/* BROCHURAS / PDF DO CAMPO (SE EXISTIREM) */}
              {Array.isArray(reservaModal.campos?.programas_pdf) &&
                reservaModal.campos.programas_pdf.length > 0 && (
                  <div className="bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 p-4 rounded-2xl">
                    <h4 className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-[#167524] m-0 mb-2">
                      📄{" "}
                      {isEn
                        ? "Camp Documents & Schedule (PDF)"
                        : "Documentos e Horário do Campo (PDF)"}
                    </h4>
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                      {reservaModal.campos.programas_pdf.map(
                        (doc: any, i: number) => (
                          <a
                            key={i}
                            href={doc.url}
                            target="_blank"
                            rel="noopener noreferrer"
                            className="bg-white border border-emerald-300 text-[#167524] text-xs font-black px-4 py-2 rounded-xl no-underline shadow-sm"
                          >
                            📥 {doc.nome || "Descarregar PDF"}
                          </a>
                        )
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}

              {/* POLÍTICA DE CANCELAMENTO ACORDADA */}
              <div className="bg-slate-50 p-4 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 text-xs">
                <strong className="block text-slate-900 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
                  🛡️{" "}
                  {isEn
                    ? "Agreed Cancellation Policy:"
                    : "Política de Cancelamento deste Campo:"}
                </strong>
                <span className="text-slate-600 font-medium">
                  {reservaModal.campos?.politica_cancelamento ||
                    "Consulte os termos na página do programa."}
                </span>
              </div>

              {/* PEDIR ALTERAÇÃO OU ADICIONAR EXTRA */}
              {reservaModal.status_pagamento !== "Reembolsado" && (
                <div className="bg-blue-50/60 border border-blue-200 p-5 rounded-2xl">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                    <div>
                      <h4 className="m-0 text-sm font-black text-[#0E3A68]">
                        📝{" "}
                        {isEn
                          ? "Need to add an extra or change dates?"
                          : "Precisa de pedir uma alteração ou adicionar um extra?"}
                      </h4>
                      <p className="m-0 text-xs text-slate-600 mt-0.5">
                        {isEn
                          ? "Send a direct request to the camp organizer."
                          : "Envie o pedido diretamente ao organizador (ex: transporte, prolongamento, troca de semana)."}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                    {!changeRequest.active && (
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() =>
                          setChangeRequest({
                            active: true,
                            text: "",
                            loading: false,
                          })
                        }
                        className="bg-[#0E3A68] text-white font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-[10px] px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap"
                      >
                        {isEn ? "Request Change" : "Pedir Alteração"}
                      </button>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  {changeRequest.active && (
                    <div className="mt-4 space-y-3">
                      <textarea
                        rows={3}
                        value={changeRequest.text}
                        onChange={(e) =>
                          setChangeRequest({
                            ...changeRequest,
                            text: e.target.value,
                          })
                        }
                        placeholder={
                          isEn
                            ? "Describe what you would like to add or change..."
                            : "Descreva aqui o extra que pretende adicionar ou a alteração necessária..."
                        }
                        className="w-full p-3.5 bg-white border border-blue-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-medium outline-none focus:border-[#0E3A68]"
                      />
                      <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() =>
                            setChangeRequest({
                              active: false,
                              text: "",
                              loading: false,
                            })
                          }
                          className="px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 cursor-pointer"
                        >
                          {isEn ? "Cancel" : "Cancelar"}
                        </button>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          disabled={changeRequest.loading}
                          onClick={() =>
                            handleEnviarPedidoAlteracao(reservaModal)
                          }
                          className="bg-[#0E3A68] text-white font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-xs px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl cursor-pointer"
                        >
                          {changeRequest.loading
                            ? "..."
                            : isEn
                            ? "Send Request"
                            : "Enviar Pedido ao Campo"}
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}

              {/* CANCELAMENTO E REEMBOLSO AUTOMÁTICO */}
              {reservaModal.status_pagamento !== "Reembolsado" && (
                <div className="border-t border-slate-100 pt-5">
                  {!cancelState.confirm ? (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() =>
                        setCancelState({ loading: false, confirm: true })
                      }
                      className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-red-600 hover:underline cursor-pointer bg-transparent border-0 p-0"
                    >
                      ✕{" "}
                      {isEn
                        ? "Cancel Booking & Request Refund"
                        : "Cancelar Inscrição / Solicitar Reembolso"}
                    </button>
                  ) : (
                    <div className="bg-red-50 border border-red-200 p-4 rounded-2xl">
                      <p className="text-xs font-bold text-red-900 m-0 mb-3">
                        ⚠️{" "}
                        {isEn
                          ? "Are you sure you want to cancel this booking? Any refund will be automatically calculated according to the camp's cancellation policy."
                          : "Tem a certeza que pretende cancelar esta inscrição? O estorno será processado automaticamente de acordo com a política de cancelamento do campo."}
                      </p>
                      <div className="flex gap-3">
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() =>
                            setCancelState({ loading: false, confirm: false })
                          }
                          className="px-4 py-2 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold cursor-pointer"
                        >
                          {isEn ? "Keep Booking" : "Manter Inscrição"}
                        </button>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          disabled={cancelState.loading}
                          onClick={() => handleCancelarReserva(reservaModal)}
                          className="px-5 py-2 bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest cursor-pointer"
                        >
                          {cancelState.loading
                            ? "..."
                            : isEn
                            ? "Confirm Cancellation"
                            : "Confirmar Cancelamento"}
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* MODAL PREENCHIMENTO RÁPIDO DE PERFIL */}
      {isPerfilModalOpen && (
        <div
          className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4"
          onClick={() => setIsPerfilModalOpen(false)}
        >
          <div
            className="bg-white rounded-3xl w-full max-w-lg p-6 md:p-8 shadow-2xl border border-slate-200"
            onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
          >
            <h3 className="text-xl font-black text-slate-900 m-0 mb-4">
              🛡️{" "}
              {isEn
                ? "Family Security & Billing Details"
                : "Dados de Segurança e Faturação"}
            </h3>
            <form onSubmit={handleSavePerfilRapido} className="space-y-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-[10px] font-black uppercase text-slate-400 mb-1">
                  Nome Completo *
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required
                  value={perfilForm.nome_completo}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setPerfilForm({
                      ...perfilForm,
                      nome_completo: e.target.value,
                    })
                  }
                  className="w-full p-3.5 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold"
                />
              </div>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[10px] font-black uppercase text-slate-400 mb-1">
                    Telemóvel *
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="tel"
                    required
                    value={perfilForm.telefone}
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      setPerfilForm({ ...perfilForm, telefone: e.target.value })
                    }
                    className="w-full p-3.5 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[10px] font-black uppercase text-slate-400 mb-1">
                    NIF (Faturação) *
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    value={perfilForm.nif}
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      setPerfilForm({ ...perfilForm, nif: e.target.value })
                    }
                    className="w-full p-3.5 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="block text-[10px] font-black uppercase text-red-700 mb-1">
                  Contacto de Emergência Alternativo (Nome e Telefone) *
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required
                  placeholder="Ex: Avó Maria - 912 345 678"
                  value={perfilForm.contacto_emergencia}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setPerfilForm({
                      ...perfilForm,
                      contacto_emergencia: e.target.value,
                    })
                  }
                  className="w-full p-3.5 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold"
                />
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 pt-3">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setIsPerfilModalOpen(false)}
                  className="px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 text-xs font-bold cursor-pointer"
                >
                  Cancelar
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={savingPerfil}
                  className="bg-[#0E3A68] text-white font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-xs px-6 py-2.5 rounded-xl cursor-pointer"
                >
                  {savingPerfil ? "..." : "Guardar Dados"}
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* MODAL PARTILHA DE LISTA */}
      {isShareModalOpen && (
        <div
          className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4"
          onClick={() => setIsShareModalOpen(false)}
        >
          <div
            className="bg-white rounded-3xl max-w-md w-full p-6 text-center shadow-2xl"
            onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
          >
            <h3 className="text-lg font-black text-slate-900 m-0 mb-2">
              🔗 {shareTitle}
            </h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mb-4">
              {isEn
                ? "Share this link with family or friends so they can see your saved camps:"
                : "Partilhe este link com outros pais ou familiares para verem os campos que guardou:"}
            </p>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <input
                type="text"
                readOnly
                value={shareUrl}
                className="flex-1 p-3 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold"
              />
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleCopyLink}
                className="bg-[#0E3A68] text-white font-black text-xs px-4 rounded-xl cursor-pointer"
              >
                {copied ? "✓" : isEn ? "Copy" : "Copiar"}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="app/[lang]/(public)/cliente/perfil/page.tsx">
"use client";

import React, { useEffect, useState, use } from "react";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";

export default function PerfilPaiPage({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ lang: string }>;
}) {
  const { lang } = use(params);
  const isEn = lang === "en";

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [savingPass, setSavingPass] = useState(false);
  const [novaPassword, setNovaPassword] = useState("");
  const [confirmarPassword, setConfirmarPassword] = useState("");

  const [perfil, setPerfil] = useState<any>({
    id: "",
    nome_completo: "",
    email: "",
    telefone: "",
    nif: "",
    contacto_emergencia: "",
    pessoas_autorizadas_recolha: "",
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    const carregarPerfil = async () => {
      const {
        data: { session },
      } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
      if (!session) {
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      const { data } = await supabase
        .from("perfis")
        .select("*")
        .eq("id", session.user.id)
        .maybeSingle();

      setPerfil({
        id: session.user.id,
        nome_completo:
          data?.nome_completo ||
          session.user.user_metadata?.nome_completo ||
          "",
        email: data?.email || session.user.email || "",
        telefone: data?.telefone || session.user.user_metadata?.telefone || "",
        nif: data?.nif || "",
        contacto_emergencia: data?.contacto_emergencia || "",
        pessoas_autorizadas_recolha: data?.pessoas_autorizadas_recolha || "",
      });
      setLoading(false);
    };

    carregarPerfil();
  }, []);

  const handleSave = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!perfil.id) return;
    setSaving(true);

    try {
      const { error } = await supabase.from("perfis").upsert({
        id: perfil.id,
        email: perfil.email,
        nome_completo: perfil.nome_completo,
        telefone: perfil.telefone,
        nif: perfil.nif,
        contacto_emergencia: perfil.contacto_emergencia,
        pessoas_autorizadas_recolha: perfil.pessoas_autorizadas_recolha,
        role: "cliente",
      });

      if (error) throw error;

      alert(
        isEn
          ? "Profile and security details updated successfully!"
          : "Dados pessoais e de segurança guardados com sucesso!"
      );
    } catch (err: any) {
      alert(err.message);
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  const handleUpdatePassword = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (novaPassword.length < 6) {
      alert(
        isEn
          ? "Password must be at least 6 characters long."
          : "A palavra-passe deve ter pelo menos 6 caracteres."
      );
      return;
    }
    if (novaPassword !== confirmarPassword) {
      alert(
        isEn
          ? "Passwords do not match."
          : "As palavras-passe introduzidas não coincidem."
      );
      return;
    }

    setSavingPass(true);
    try {
      const { error } = await supabase.auth.updateUser({
        password: novaPassword,
      });
      if (error) throw error;
      setNovaPassword("");
      setConfirmarPassword("");
      alert(
        isEn
          ? "Password updated successfully!"
          : "Palavra-passe atualizada com sucesso!"
      );
    } catch (err: any) {
      alert(err.message);
    } finally {
      setSavingPass(false);
    }
  };

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="p-20 text-center font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest text-xs animate-pulse">
        {isEn
          ? "Loading security profile..."
          : "A carregar perfil e segurança..."}
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <main className="max-w-4xl mx-auto pb-20 font-sans">
      <div className="mb-10">
        <h1 className="text-3xl md:text-4xl font-black text-slate-900 m-0 tracking-tight">
          {isEn ? "Profile & Security" : "O Meu Perfil e Segurança"}
        </h1>
        <p className="text-slate-500 mt-2 text-sm md:text-base font-medium mb-0">
          {isEn
            ? "Manage your contact details and authorized pickup persons."
            : "Gira os seus contactos e defina quem está autorizado a recolher os seus educandos no final do dia."}
        </p>
      </div>

      <form onSubmit={handleSave} className="flex flex-col gap-8">
        {/* DADOS PESSOAIS */}
        <div className="bg-white p-6 md:p-8 rounded-[2rem] border border-slate-200 shadow-sm">
          <h2 className="text-base font-black text-slate-900 mb-6 pb-4 border-b border-slate-100 flex items-center gap-3 uppercase tracking-widest m-0">
            <span className="text-2xl">👤</span>{" "}
            {isEn ? "Personal Details" : "Os Seus Dados"}
          </h2>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
            <div className="sm:col-span-2">
              <label className="block text-[10px] font-black text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest mb-2 ml-1">
                {isEn ? "Full Name *" : "Nome Completo *"}
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={perfil.nome_completo}
                onChange={(e) =>
                  setPerfil({ ...perfil, nome_completo: e.target.value })
                }
                required
                className="w-full p-4 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 outline-none focus:border-[#0E3A68] focus:bg-white transition-colors"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="sm:col-span-2">
              <label className="block text-[10px] font-black text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest mb-2 ml-1">
                {isEn ? "Email (Login)" : "E-mail (Login e Notificações)"}
              </label>
              <input
                type="email"
                value={perfil.email}
                disabled
                className="w-full p-4 bg-slate-100 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-slate-400 cursor-not-allowed outline-none"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-[10px] font-black text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest mb-2 ml-1">
                {isEn ? "Phone Number *" : "Telefone / Telemóvel *"}
              </label>
              <input
                type="tel"
                value={perfil.telefone}
                onChange={(e) =>
                  setPerfil({ ...perfil, telefone: e.target.value })
                }
                required
                className="w-full p-4 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 outline-none focus:border-[#0E3A68] focus:bg-white transition-colors"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-[10px] font-black text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest mb-2 ml-1">
                {isEn ? "Tax ID (Billing)" : "NIF (Faturação IRS)"}
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={perfil.nif}
                onChange={(e) => setPerfil({ ...perfil, nif: e.target.value })}
                placeholder={
                  isEn ? "For receipt issuance" : "Para emissão de faturas"
                }
                className="w-full p-4 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 outline-none focus:border-[#0E3A68] focus:bg-white transition-colors"
              />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* SEGURANÇA E RECOLHA */}
        <div className="bg-red-50 p-6 md:p-8 rounded-[2rem] border border-red-100 shadow-sm">
          <h2 className="text-base font-black text-red-800 mb-6 pb-4 border-b border-red-200/50 flex items-center gap-3 uppercase tracking-widest m-0">
            <span className="text-2xl bg-white rounded-full w-10 h-10 flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm">
              🛡️
            </span>
            {isEn ? "Security & Emergency" : "Segurança e Recolha"}
          </h2>

          <div className="flex flex-col gap-6">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-[11px] font-black text-red-800 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1.5 ml-1">
                {isEn
                  ? "Emergency Contact (Name & Phone) *"
                  : "Contacto de Emergência Alternativo *"}
              </label>
              <p className="text-xs font-bold text-red-600 mb-3 ml-1 mt-0">
                {isEn
                  ? "Who should we call if we cannot reach you?"
                  : "A quem deve o campo ligar num cenário de SOS caso não consigam contactar o encarregado de educação principal?"}
              </p>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={perfil.contacto_emergencia}
                onChange={(e) =>
                  setPerfil({
                    ...perfil,
                    contacto_emergencia: e.target.value,
                  })
                }
                required
                placeholder={
                  isEn
                    ? "E.g.: Grandma Mary - 912 345 678"
                    : "Ex: Avó Maria - 912 345 678"
                }
                className="w-full p-4 bg-white border border-red-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 outline-none focus:border-red-500 transition-colors shadow-sm"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="border-t border-red-200/50 pt-6">
              <label className="block text-[11px] font-black text-red-800 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1.5 ml-1">
                {isEn
                  ? "Authorized Pickup Persons *"
                  : "Pessoas Autorizadas na Recolha *"}
              </label>
              <p className="text-xs font-bold text-red-600 mb-3 ml-1 leading-relaxed mt-0">
                {isEn
                  ? "List names and ID numbers. For security reasons, camps may require identification on site."
                  : "Liste os Nomes e números de Cartão de Cidadão das pessoas autorizadas. Por motivos de segurança, os campos podem exigir identificação no local e recusar a entrega da criança a pessoas não listadas nesta caixa."}
              </p>
              <textarea
                rows={4}
                value={perfil.pessoas_autorizadas_recolha}
                onChange={(e) =>
                  setPerfil({
                    ...perfil,
                    pessoas_autorizadas_recolha: e.target.value,
                  })
                }
                required
                placeholder={
                  isEn
                    ? "E.g.: John Doe (Father) - ID: 12345678"
                    : "Ex: João Silva (Pai) - CC: 12345678, Avó Margarida - CC: 87654321"
                }
                className="w-full p-4 bg-white border border-red-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 outline-none focus:border-red-500 transition-colors shadow-sm resize-none"
              />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <button
          type="submit"
          disabled={saving}
          className="p-5 bg-[#0E3A68] hover:bg-[#0b2b4d] text-white font-black uppercase tracking-widest rounded-2xl border-none cursor-pointer text-sm shadow-xl transition-all disabled:opacity-50"
        >
          {saving
            ? isEn
              ? "Saving data..."
              : "A guardar..."
            : isEn
            ? "Save Profile & Security"
            : "Gravar Alterações e Segurança"}
        </button>
      </form>

      {/* SECÇÃO EXTRA: ALTERAR PALAVRA-PASSE */}
      <form
        onSubmit={handleUpdatePassword}
        className="mt-12 bg-white p-6 md:p-8 rounded-[2rem] border border-slate-200 shadow-sm space-y-5"
      >
        <h2 className="text-base font-black text-slate-900 pb-4 border-b border-slate-100 flex items-center gap-3 uppercase tracking-widest m-0">
          <span className="text-2xl">🔑</span>{" "}
          {isEn ? "Change Password" : "Alterar Palavra-Passe de Acesso"}
        </h2>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
          <div>
            <label className="block text-[10px] font-black text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest mb-2 ml-1">
              {isEn ? "New Password" : "Nova Palavra-Passe"}
            </label>
            <input
              type="password"
              required
              minLength={6}
              value={novaPassword}
              onChange={(e) => setNovaPassword(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Mínimo 6 caracteres"
              className="w-full p-4 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 outline-none focus:border-[#0E3A68] focus:bg-white"
            />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="block text-[10px] font-black text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest mb-2 ml-1">
              {isEn ? "Confirm Password" : "Confirmar Nova Palavra-Passe"}
            </label>
            <input
              type="password"
              required
              minLength={6}
              value={confirmarPassword}
              onChange={(e) => setConfirmarPassword(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Repita a palavra-passe"
              className="w-full p-4 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 outline-none focus:border-[#0E3A68] focus:bg-white"
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex justify-end">
          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={savingPass || !novaPassword}
            className="px-6 py-3.5 bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 disabled:bg-slate-200 text-white font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-xs rounded-xl cursor-pointer transition-colors"
          >
            {savingPass
              ? "..."
              : isEn
              ? "Update Password"
              : "Atualizar Palavra-Passe"}
          </button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </main>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="app/[lang]/(public)/cliente/layout.tsx">
"use client";

import React, { useEffect, useState, use } from "react";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";
import { useRouter, usePathname } from "next/navigation";
import Link from "next/link";

export default function ClienteLayout({
  children,
  params,
}: {
  children: React.ReactNode;
  params: Promise<{ lang: string }>;
}) {
  const { lang } = use(params);
  const router = useRouter();
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const isEn = lang === "en";

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [user, setUser] = useState<any>(null);
  const [unreadMsgs, setUnreadMsgs] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    const checkSession = async () => {
      const {
        data: { session },
      } = await supabase.auth.getSession();

      if (!session) {
        router.push(`/${lang}/login`);
        return;
      }

      setUser(session.user);

      const { count } = await supabase
        .from("mensagens")
        .select("*", { count: "exact", head: true })
        .eq("receiver_id", session.user.id)
        .eq("lida", false);

      setUnreadMsgs(count || 0);
      setLoading(false);
    };

    checkSession();
  }, [lang, router, pathname]);

  const handleLogout = async () => {
    await supabase.auth.signOut();
    router.push(`/${lang}`);
  };

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-[70vh] bg-slate-50 flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-slate-400 text-xs uppercase tracking-widest animate-pulse">
        {isEn ? "Loading Family Portal..." : "A carregar Portal da Família..."}
      </div>
    );
  }

  const navItems = [
    {
      href: `/${lang}/cliente/dashboard`,
      active: pathname.includes("/dashboard"),
      icon: "🏕️",
      label: isEn ? "My Bookings" : "As Minhas Reservas",
    },
    {
      href: `/${lang}/cliente/criancas`,
      active: pathname.includes("/criancas"),
      icon: "👦",
      label: isEn ? "My Children" : "Os Meus Filhos",
    },
    {
      href: `/${lang}/cliente/mensagens`,
      active: pathname.includes("/mensagens"),
      icon: "💬",
      label: isEn ? "Messages" : "Mensagens",
      badge: unreadMsgs,
    },
    {
      href: `/${lang}/cliente/favoritos`,
      active: pathname.includes("/favoritos"),
      icon: "❤️",
      label: isEn ? "Wishlists" : "Favoritos",
    },
    {
      href: `/${lang}/cliente/perfil`,
      active: pathname.includes("/perfil"),
      icon: "🛡️",
      label: isEn ? "Profile & Security" : "Perfil e Segurança",
    },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 font-sans">
      {/* SUB-NAVEGAÇÃO DO PORTAL DOS PAIS */}
      <div className="bg-white border-b border-slate-200 sticky top-16 md:top-20 z-30 shadow-sm">
        <div className="max-w-[1200px] mx-auto px-4 md:px-6 flex items-center justify-between gap-4 overflow-x-auto no-scrollbar py-3">
          <nav className="flex items-center gap-2">
            {navItems.map((item) => (
              <Link
                key={item.href}
                href={item.href}
                className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider whitespace-nowrap no-underline transition-all ${
                  item.active
                    ? "bg-[#0E3A68] text-white shadow-sm"
                    : "text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100"
                }`}
              >
                <span>{item.icon}</span>
                <span>{item.label}</span>
                {item.badge !== undefined && item.badge > 0 && (
                  <span className="bg-[#e2a41d] text-slate-900 text-[10px] font-black px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                    {item.badge}
                  </span>
                )}
              </Link>
            ))}
          </nav>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleLogout}
            className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-red-600 hover:bg-red-50 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-red-100 cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap transition-colors"
          >
            {isEn ? "Logout" : "Sair"}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* CONTEÚDO DA PÁGINA */}
      <div className="max-w-[1200px] mx-auto px-4 md:px-6 pt-8 pb-20">
        {children}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
</file>

import Link from "next/link";
import { Metadata } from "next";

export async function generateMetadata({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ lang: string }>;
}): Promise<Metadata> {
  const { lang } = await params;
  const isEn = lang === "en";

  return {
    title: isEn
      ? "Parents Guide | How to Book & Prepare | HelloCamp"
      : "Guia de Pais | Como Reservar e Preparar as Férias | HelloCamp",
    description: isEn
      ? "Everything parents need to know about booking summer camps, flexible payments, safety standards, and packing checklists."
      : "Tudo o que os pais precisam de saber sobre como escolher e reservar campos de férias, pagamentos faseados, segurança e lista do que levar.",
  };
}

              >
                <span className="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-[#16A34A] text-white flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-black shrink-0">
                  ✓
                </span>
                {item}
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-[#0F172A] text-white p-8 md:p-10 rounded-md shadow-xl flex flex-col justify-between">
          <div>
            <span className="inline-block bg-[#F59E0B] text-white text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest px-3 py-1 rounded-md mb-4">
              🛡️ {isEn ? "Family Portal" : "Portal da Família"}
            </span>
            <h3 className="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-black text-white mb-4">
              {isEn
                ? "Manage all your children's bookings in one place."
                : "Gira todas as inscrições dos seus filhos num único painel."}
            </h3>

              {isEn
                ? "In your Customer Area you can download payment receipts, update allergy notes, request extra days, or pay the second installment with 1 click."
                : "Na sua Área de Cliente pode consultar faturas, atualizar dados médicos dos seus filhos, pedir dias extra ao organizador ou liquidar a 2.ª prestação com 1 clique."}

              className="bg-[#2563EB] hover:bg-blue-700 text-white font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-xs px-6 py-4 rounded-md no-underline"

            </Link>
            <Link
              href={`/${lang}/pesquisa`}
              className="bg-[#F59E0B] hover:bg-amber-600 text-white font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-xs px-6 py-4 rounded-md no-underline"
            >
              {isEn ? "Find a Camp" : "Procurar Campo"}
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}

<file path="app/[lang]/(public)/monitores/login/page.tsx">
"use client";

import React, { useState, use } from "react";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import Link from "next/link";

export default function LoginMonitorPage({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ lang: string }>;
}) {
  const { lang } = use(params);
  const isEn = lang === "en";
  const router = useRouter();

  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState("");

  const handleLogin = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    setErrorMsg("");

    try {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({
        email: email.trim(),
        password,
      });

      if (error || !data.user) {
        throw new Error(
          isEn
            ? "Invalid email or password."
            : "E-mail ou palavra-passe incorretos."
        );
      }

      router.push(`/${lang}/monitores/portal/perfil`);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setErrorMsg(err.message);
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const inputClass =
    "w-full p-4 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 outline-none focus:border-[#2563eb] focus:bg-white transition-colors";

  return (
    <main className="min-h-[calc(100vh-80px)] flex flex-col bg-slate-50 font-sans">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md mx-auto pt-8 px-4 relative z-10 text-left">
        <Link
          href={`/${lang}/monitores`}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-900 transition-colors bg-white px-5 py-2.5 rounded-full border border-slate-200 shadow-sm no-underline"
        >
          &larr; {isEn ? "Back" : "Voltar Atrás"}
        </Link>
      </div>

      <div className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center p-4 pb-20">
        <div className="w-full max-w-md">
          <div className="text-center mb-8">
            <h1 className="text-3xl md:text-4xl font-black text-slate-900 tracking-tight mb-2">
              {isEn ? "Monitor Portal" : "Portal do Monitor"}
            </h1>
            <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-500">
              {isEn
                ? "Log in to manage your staff profile and job offers."
                : "Inicie sessão para gerir a sua disponibilidade e propostas."}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-white p-8 md:p-10 rounded-[2rem] border border-slate-200 shadow-2xl shadow-slate-200/50">
            {errorMsg && (
              <div className="mb-6 p-4 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-700 text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest rounded-xl text-center">
                {errorMsg}
              </div>
            )}

            <form onSubmit={handleLogin} className="space-y-6">
              <div>
                <label className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400 mb-2 block ml-1">
                  Email
                </label>
                <input
                  type="email"
                  required
                  className={inputClass}
                  value={email}
                  onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="nome@email.com"
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-2">
                  <label className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400 ml-1">
                    {isEn ? "Password" : "Palavra-Passe"}
                  </label>
                  <Link
                    href={`/${lang}/recuperar-password`}
                    className="text-[10px] font-black text-[#2563eb] hover:underline"
                  >
                    {isEn ? "Forgot password?" : "Recuperar password"}
                  </Link>
                </div>
                <input
                  type="password"
                  required
                  className={inputClass}
                  value={password}
                  onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="••••••••"
                />
              </div>

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={loading}
                className="w-full bg-[#2563eb] text-white font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-xs py-4 md:py-5 rounded-xl shadow-lg hover:bg-blue-700 transition-all mt-4 disabled:opacity-50 cursor-pointer"
              >
                {loading
                  ? isEn
                    ? "Entering..."
                    : "A entrar..."
                  : isEn
                  ? "Sign In"
                  : "Entrar no Portal"}
              </button>
            </form>
          </div>

          <div className="text-center mt-10 space-y-5">
            <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-500">
              {isEn
                ? "Want to work in summer camps?"
                : "Queres trabalhar em campos de férias?"}{" "}
              <br />
              <Link
                href={`/${lang}/monitores/registo`}
                className="text-[#2563eb] font-black hover:underline mt-1 inline-block"
              >
                {isEn
                  ? "Create your profile here"
                  : "Cria o teu perfil de monitor aqui"}
              </Link>
            </p>
            <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-bold">
              Ao entrar, aceitas os{" "}
              <Link
                href={`/${lang}/monitores/termos`}
                className="underline hover:text-slate-600"
              >
                Termos e Condições
              </Link>
              .
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

<file path="app/[lang]/(public)/monitores/portal/layout.tsx">
"use client";

import React, { useEffect, useState, use } from "react";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";
import { useRouter, usePathname } from "next/navigation";
import Link from "next/link";

function NavLink({
  href,
  active,
  icon,
  text,
  badge,
}: {
  href: string;
  active: boolean;
  icon: string;
  text: string;
  badge?: number;
}) {
  return (
    <Link
      href={href}
      className={`flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3.5 rounded-xl font-black text-xs uppercase tracking-widest no-underline transition-all whitespace-nowrap ${
        active
          ? "bg-[#2563eb] text-white shadow-md"
          : "text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100"
      }`}
    >
      <span className="flex items-center gap-3">
        <span className="text-base">{icon}</span>
        <span>{text}</span>
      </span>
      {badge !== undefined && badge > 0 && (
        <span className="bg-[#e2a41d] text-slate-900 text-[10px] font-black px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full ml-2">
          {badge}
        </span>
      )}
    </Link>
  );
}

export default function PortalMonitorLayout({
  children,
  params,
}: {
  children: React.ReactNode;
  params: Promise<{ lang: string }>;
}) {
  const { lang } = use(params);
  const router = useRouter();
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const isEn = lang === "en";

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [user, setUser] = useState<any>(null);
  const [unreadCount, setUnreadCount] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    const verifyMonitor = async () => {
      const {
        data: { session },
      } = await supabase.auth.getSession();

      if (!session) {
        router.push(`/${lang}/monitores/login`);
        return;
      }

      setUser(session.user);

      const { count } = await supabase
        .from("mensagens_recrutamento")
        .select("*", { count: "exact", head: true })
        .eq("monitor_id", session.user.id)
        .eq("remetente", "parceiro")
        .eq("lida", false);

      setUnreadCount(count || 0);
      setLoading(false);
    };

    verifyMonitor();
  }, [router, lang, pathname]);

  const handleLogout = async () => {
    await supabase.auth.signOut();
    router.push(`/${lang}`);
  };

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="flex-1 bg-slate-50 flex items-center justify-center min-h-screen">
        <div className="w-10 h-10 border-4 border-blue-200 border-t-[#2563eb] rounded-full animate-spin"></div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col md:flex-row w-full bg-slate-50 font-sans min-h-screen">
      <aside className="w-full md:w-[280px] bg-white border-b md:border-b-0 md:border-r border-slate-200 flex flex-col flex-shrink-0 z-20 shadow-sm md:shadow-none">
        <div className="p-6 md:p-8 border-b border-slate-100 hidden md:block">
          <h2 className="text-xl font-black m-0 text-slate-900 tracking-tight">
            Portal do <span className="text-[#2563eb]">Monitor</span>
          </h2>
          <p className="text-[10px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mt-1.5 mb-0">
            Bolsa de Talentos
          </p>
        </div>

        <nav className="flex md:flex-col overflow-x-auto md:overflow-visible gap-2 p-3 md:p-6 no-scrollbar items-center md:items-stretch">
          <NavLink
            href={`/${lang}/monitores/portal/perfil`}
            active={pathname.includes("/perfil")}
            icon="🧑‍🏫"
            text={isEn ? "My Profile" : "O Meu Perfil"}
          />
          <NavLink
            href={`/${lang}/monitores/portal/mensagens`}
            active={pathname.includes("/mensagens")}
            icon="💬"
            text={isEn ? "Job Offers" : "Propostas"}
            badge={unreadCount}
          />
          <NavLink
            href={`/${lang}/pesquisa`}
            active={false}
            icon="🏕️"
            text={isEn ? "Explore Camps" : "Explorar Campos"}
          />

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleLogout}
            className="md:hidden flex-shrink-0 px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-red-500 bg-red-50 border border-red-100 ml-2 cursor-pointer"
          >
            {isEn ? "Logout" : "Sair"}
          </button>
        </nav>

        <div className="p-6 border-t border-slate-100 hidden md:block mt-auto bg-slate-50/50">
          <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-4 truncate">
            {user?.email}
          </p>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleLogout}
            className="w-full p-3.5 bg-white border border-slate-200 text-slate-700 rounded-xl font-black text-xs uppercase tracking-widest hover:bg-red-50 hover:text-red-600 hover:border-red-200 transition-colors shadow-sm cursor-pointer"
          >
            {isEn ? "Logout" : "Terminar Sessão"}
          </button>
        </div>
      </aside>

      <main className="flex-1 p-4 md:p-8 w-full box-border overflow-y-auto">
        {children}
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}
</file>

Furthermore, we may use global service providers located outside the European Economic Area (EEA), always ensuring the implementation of Standard Contractual Clauses (SCCs) to protect your data internationally.' 
              : 'Utilizamos cookies (técnicos e analíticos) para assegurar a persistência de contas e pesquisas, o processamento seguro de pagamentos e a análise de tráfego. Adicionalmente, podemos utilizar prestadores de serviços globais localizados fora do Espaço Económico Europeu (EEE), assegurando sempre a implementação de Cláusulas Contratuais-Tipo (CCT) para proteger os seus dados a nível internacional.'}
          </p>

          {/* CAIXA DE CONTACTO FINAL */}
          <div className="bg-slate-50 p-6 md:p-8 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 mt-12">
            <p className="text-[15px] m-0 text-slate-700 leading-relaxed text-center sm:text-left">
              <strong className="text-slate-900 block mb-2">{!isPt ? 'Contact and Complaints:' : 'Contacto e Reclamações:'}</strong>
              {!isPt 
                ? 'To exercise your privacy rights, please contact our Data Protection Office at ' 
                : 'Para exercer os seus direitos de privacidade, contacte a nossa equipa de Proteção de Dados através de '
              }
              <a href="mailto:info@hellocamp.pt" className="text-[#059669] font-black hover:underline">info@hellocamp.pt</a>. 
              {!isPt 
                ? ' You also have the right to lodge a complaint with your national supervisory authority (e.g., CNPD in Portugal).' 
                : ' Tem ainda o direito de apresentar uma reclamação junto da autoridade de controlo competente (em Portugal, a Comissão Nacional de Proteção de Dados - CNPD).'
              }
            </p>
          </div>

        </div>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}
