<file path="app/[lang]/(public)/layout.tsx">
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { getDictionary } from "@/lib/getDictionary";
import Header from "./components/header";
import Footer from "./components/Footer";
import { Analytics } from "@vercel/analytics/react";
import { SpeedInsights } from "@vercel/speed-insights/next";

export async function generateMetadata({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ lang: string }>;
}): Promise<Metadata> {
  const { lang } = await params;
  const isEn = lang === "en";

  return {
    metadataBase: new URL("https://www.hellocamp.pt"),
    title: {
      template: "%s | HelloCamp",
      default: isEn
        ? "HelloCamp | Find the Best Summer Camps in Portugal & Europe"
        : "HelloCamp | Os Melhores Campos de Férias em Portugal e na Europa",
    },
    description: isEn
      ? "Discover and book the best holiday camps, sports programs, and creative workshops for children and teens in Portugal and Europe."
      : "Descubra e reserve de forma segura os melhores campos de férias, colónias desportivas e workshops criativos para crianças e jovens em Portugal e na Europa.",
    alternates: {
      canonical: `https://www.hellocamp.pt/${lang}`,
      languages: {
        "pt-PT": "https://www.hellocamp.pt/pt",
        "en-US": "https://www.hellocamp.pt/en",
      },
    },
    openGraph: {
      title: "HelloCamp",
      description: isEn
        ? "The easiest way to find and book holiday camps."
        : "A forma mais fácil de procurar e reservar campos de férias.",
      url: `https://www.hellocamp.pt/${lang}`,
      siteName: "HelloCamp",
      images: [
        {
          url: "/og-image.jpg",
          width: 1200,
          height: 630,
        },
      ],
      locale: isEn ? "en_US" : "pt_PT",
      type: "website",
    },
    robots: {
      index: true,
      follow: true,
    },
  };
}

export default async function PublicLayout({
  children,
  params,
}: {
  children: React.ReactNode;
  params: Promise<{ lang: string }>;
}) {
  const { lang } = await params;
  const dict = await getDictionary(lang as "pt" | "en");

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col min-h-screen">
      <Header dict={dict} lang={lang} />

      <main className="flex-grow flex flex-col w-full">{children}</main>

      <Footer dict={dict} lang={lang} />

      <Analytics />
      <SpeedInsights />
    </div>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="app/[lang]/[categoria]/[localizacao]/page.tsx">
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";
import { unstable_cache } from "next/cache";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Metadata } from "next";
import BotaoFavorito from "../../(public)/components/BotaoFavorito";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

const getCamposCacheados = unstable_cache(
  async () => {
    const { data } = await supabase
      .from("campos")
      .select("*")
      .eq("ativo", true)
      .not("contrato_parceiro_url", "is", null)
      .order("nome");
    return data || [];
  },
  ["cache-pesquisa-campos"],
  { revalidate: 86400 }
);

// Função de normalização que remove acentos e caracteres especiais para comparação infalível
function normalizeStr(str?: string | null): string {
  if (!str) return "";
  return str
    .normalize("NFD")
    .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "")
    .toLowerCase()
    .trim();
}

// Filtra campos com inteligência semântica (ex: "ingles" -> "linguas"/"ingles", "algarve" -> "faro"/"algarve")
function filtrarCamposPorRotaProgramatica(todosOsCampos: any[], categoriaSlug: string, localizacaoSlug: string) {
  const catNorm = normalizeStr(decodeURIComponent(categoriaSlug).replace(/-/g, " "));
  const locNorm = normalizeStr(decodeURIComponent(localizacaoSlug).replace(/-/g, " "));

  // Sinónimos de categorias para garantir que "ingles", "surf", "futebol" encontram resultados
  const termosCat = [catNorm];
  if (catNorm === "ingles" || catNorm === "english" || catNorm === "linguas") {
    termosCat.push("linguas", "languages", "ingles", "english", "idiomas");
  }
  if (catNorm === "desportos" || catNorm === "sports") {
    termosCat.push("desporto", "sports");
  }

  // Sinónimos geográficos (ex: Algarve -> Distrito de Faro)
  const termosLoc = [locNorm];
  if (locNorm === "algarve") termosLoc.push("faro", "algarve", "portimao", "lagos", "albufeira", "loulé", "loule", "tavira");
  if (locNorm === "reino unido" || locNorm === "uk" || locNorm === "united kingdom") {
    termosLoc.push("reino unido", "united kingdom", "uk", "inglaterra", "londres", "london");
  }
  if (locNorm === "espanha" || locNorm === "spain") {
    termosLoc.push("espanha", "spain", "madrid", "barcelona", "valencia");
  }

  return todosOsCampos.filter((c) => {
    const textoCategoriaECampo = normalizeStr(
      `${c.categoria || ""} ${c.categoria_en || ""} ${c.nome || ""} ${c.nome_en || ""} ${c.descricao || ""} ${c.descricao_en || ""}`
    );
    const textoLocalizacao = normalizeStr(
      `${c.Distrito || ""} ${c.distrito || ""} ${c.local || ""} ${c.local_en || ""} ${c.pais || ""} ${c.pais_en || ""}`
    );

    const matchCat = termosCat.some((t) => textoCategoriaECampo.includes(t));
    const matchLoc = termosLoc.some((t) => textoLocalizacao.includes(t));

    return matchCat && matchLoc;
  });
}

export async function generateMetadata({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ lang: string; categoria: string; localizacao: string }>;
}): Promise<Metadata> {
  const { lang, categoria, localizacao } = await params;
  const isEn = lang === "en";

  const catFormatada = decodeURIComponent(categoria)
    .replace(/-/g, " ")
    .replace(/\b\w/g, (c) => c.toUpperCase());
  const localFormatado = decodeURIComponent(localizacao)
    .replace(/-/g, " ")
    .replace(/\b\w/g, (c) => c.toUpperCase());

  const todosOsCampos = await getCamposCacheados();
  const camposFiltrados = filtrarCamposPorRotaProgramatica(todosOsCampos, categoria, localizacao);
  const count = camposFiltrados.length;

  const metaTitle = isEn
    ? `Best ${catFormatada} Camps in ${localFormatado} (${new Date().getFullYear()}) | HelloCamp`
    : `Campos de Férias de ${catFormatada} em ${localFormatado} (${new Date().getFullYear()}) | HelloCamp`;

  const metaDescription = isEn
    ? `Compare and book ${count > 0 ? count : "the best"} verified ${catFormatada.toLowerCase()} camps and youth programs in ${localFormatado}. Direct booking, verified organizers, and parent reviews on HelloCamp.`
    : `Compare e reserve ${count > 0 ? count : "os melhores"} campos de férias e programas de ${catFormatada.toLowerCase()} em ${localFormatado}. Inscrições seguras, parceiros verificados e avaliações reais na HelloCamp.`;

  return {
    title: metaTitle,
    description: metaDescription,
    alternates: {
      canonical: `https://www.hellocamp.pt/${lang}/${categoria}/${localizacao}`,
      languages: {
        "pt-PT": `https://www.hellocamp.pt/pt/${categoria}/${localizacao}`,
        "en-US": `https://www.hellocamp.pt/en/${categoria}/${localizacao}`,
      },
    },
    openGraph: {
      title: metaTitle,
      description: metaDescription,
      url: `https://www.hellocamp.pt/${lang}/${categoria}/${localizacao}`,
      siteName: "HelloCamp",
      type: "website",
    },
  };
}

export default async function CategoriaLocalizacaoPage({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ lang: string; categoria: string; localizacao: string }>;
}) {
  const { lang, categoria, localizacao } = await params;
  const isEn = lang === "en";

  const catFormatada = decodeURIComponent(categoria)
    .replace(/-/g, " ")
    .replace(/\b\w/g, (c) => c.toUpperCase());
  const localFormatado = decodeURIComponent(localizacao)
    .replace(/-/g, " ")
    .replace(/\b\w/g, (c) => c.toUpperCase());

  const todosOsCampos = await getCamposCacheados();
  const camposFiltrados = filtrarCamposPorRotaProgramatica(todosOsCampos, categoria, localizacao);

  const baseUrl = "https://www.hellocamp.pt";
  const breadcrumbSchema = {
    "@context": "https://schema.org",
    "@type": "BreadcrumbList",
    itemListElement: [
      {
        "@type": "ListItem",
        position: 1,
        name: isEn ? "Home" : "Início",
        item: `${baseUrl}/${lang}`,
      },
      {
        "@type": "ListItem",
        position: 2,
        name: catFormatada,
        item: `${baseUrl}/${lang}/pesquisa?q=${encodeURIComponent(catFormatada)}`,
      },
      {
        "@type": "ListItem",
        position: 3,
        name: `${catFormatada} - ${localFormatado}`,
        item: `${baseUrl}/${lang}/${categoria}/${localizacao}`,
      },
    ],
  };

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 font-sans text-slate-900 pb-24">
      <script
        type="application/ld+json"
        dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: JSON.stringify(breadcrumbSchema) }}
      />

      {/* HERO SEO PROGRAMÁTICO */}
      <section className="bg-white border-b border-slate-200 pt-10 pb-12 px-4 md:px-6 shadow-sm">
        <div className="max-w-[1100px] mx-auto">
          <nav className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[10px] font-black text-slate-400 mb-6 tracking-widest uppercase">
            <Link href={`/${lang}`} className="hover:text-[#0E3A68] transition-colors no-underline">
              {isEn ? "Home" : "Início"}
            </Link>
            <span>/</span>
            <Link href={`/${lang}/pesquisa`} className="hover:text-[#0E3A68] transition-colors no-underline">
              {isEn ? "Camps" : "Campos"}
            </Link>
            <span>/</span>
            <span className="text-slate-900">
              {catFormatada} • {localFormatado}
            </span>
          </nav>

          <span className="inline-block text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-[#167524] bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg mb-4">
            {isEn ? "Curated Selection" : "Seleção Especializada HelloCamp"}
          </span>

          <h1 className="text-3xl md:text-5xl font-black text-slate-900 tracking-tight mb-4 capitalize">
            {isEn
              ? `${catFormatada} Camps in ${localFormatado}`
              : `Campos de Férias de ${catFormatada} em ${localFormatado}`}
          </h1>

          <p className="text-base md:text-lg text-slate-500 font-medium max-w-3xl leading-relaxed">
            {isEn
              ? `Discover verified ${catFormatada.toLowerCase()} programs and summer camps in ${localFormatado}. Compare dates, age groups, and book directly with complete peace of mind.`
              : `Descubra os melhores programas e campos de férias de ${catFormatada.toLowerCase()} em ${localFormatado}. Compare datas, idades e reserve diretamente com garantia de segurança.`}
          </p>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* LISTAGEM DE RESULTADOS */}
      <section className="max-w-[1100px] mx-auto px-4 py-12 md:py-16">
        {camposFiltrados.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="text-center p-12 bg-white rounded-[2rem] border border-slate-200 shadow-sm max-w-2xl mx-auto flex flex-col items-center gap-4">
            <div className="w-20 h-20 bg-slate-50 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-4xl border border-slate-100 mb-2">
              🏕️
            </div>
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-900">
              {isEn
                ? `No exact matches for ${catFormatada} in ${localFormatado} right now`
                : `Ainda não temos campos exatos de ${catFormatada} em ${localFormatado}`}
            </h2>
            <p className="text-slate-500 font-medium text-sm md:text-base">
              {isEn
                ? "We are constantly onboarding new partners. Explore all available programs in our main directory below."
                : "Estamos a integrar novos parceiros todas as semanas. Explore abaixo todos os programas ativos no nosso diretório."}
            </p>
            <Link
              href={`/${lang}/pesquisa`}
              className="mt-4 bg-[#0E3A68] text-white font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-xs px-8 py-4 rounded-xl hover:bg-[#0b2b4d] transition-colors shadow-lg no-underline"
            >
              {isEn ? "Explore All Camps" : "Explorar Todos os Campos"}
            </Link>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <>
            <div className="mb-8 flex items-center justify-between">
              <span className="text-xs font-black text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest bg-white border border-slate-200 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl shadow-sm">
                {camposFiltrados.length} {isEn ? "programs available" : "programas disponíveis"}
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
              {camposFiltrados.map((campo: any) => {
                const nomeCampo = isEn && campo.nome_en ? campo.nome_en : campo.nome;
                const catCampo = isEn && campo.categoria_en ? campo.categoria_en : campo.categoria;
                const localCampo = isEn && campo.local_en ? campo.local_en : campo.Distrito || campo.local;

                let precoVisivel = campo.preco || 0;
                if (!precoVisivel && campo.pacotes?.length > 0) {
                  const todosPrecos = campo.pacotes.flatMap(
                    (p: any) => p.variantes?.map((v: any) => v.preco) || []
                  );
                  if (todosPrecos.length > 0) precoVisivel = Math.min(...todosPrecos);
                }

                const imgUrl = campo.imagem?.startsWith("http") ? campo.imagem : "/og-image.jpg";

                return (
                  <div
                    key={campo.id}
                    className="group flex flex-col bg-white rounded-[2rem] overflow-hidden border border-slate-200 relative shadow-sm hover:shadow-[0_20px_60px_rgb(0,0,0,0.08)] transition-all duration-300 hover:-translate-y-1"
                  >
                    <Link href={`/${lang}/campo/${campo.id}`} className="absolute inset-0 z-10">
                      <span className="sr-only">Explorar {nomeCampo}</span>
                    </Link>
                    <div className="absolute top-4 right-4 z-20">
                      <BotaoFavorito campoId={campo.id} />
                    </div>

                    <div className="relative h-60 w-full overflow-hidden bg-slate-100">
                      <div
                        className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full bg-cover bg-center transition-transform duration-700 group-hover:scale-105"
                        style={{ backgroundImage: `url('${imgUrl}')` }}
                      />
                      <div className="absolute top-4 left-4 bg-white/90 backdrop-blur-sm px-3 py-1.5 text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-[#0E3A68] rounded-lg z-0 shadow-sm">
                        {catCampo}
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex flex-col p-6 flex-1 pointer-events-none">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[10px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-3">
                        <svg className="w-3 h-3 text-[#e2a41d]" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="3">
                          <path d="M21 10c0 7-9 13-9 13s-9-6-9-13a9 9 0 0 1 18 0z"></path>
                          <circle cx="12" cy="10" r="3"></circle>
                        </svg>
                        {localCampo}
                      </div>
                      <h3 className="text-xl font-black text-slate-900 leading-tight mb-2 line-clamp-2">
                        {nomeCampo}
                      </h3>
                      <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 font-medium mb-6">
                        {isEn ? "Ages:" : "Idades:"}{" "}
                        <span className="text-slate-900 font-bold">{campo.idade}</span>
                      </p>

                      <div className="mt-auto pt-6 flex items-center justify-between border-t border-slate-100">
                        <div>
                          <p className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400 mb-0.5">
                            {isEn ? "From" : "A partir de"}
                          </p>
                          <p className="text-2xl font-black text-[#0E3A68] m-0">
                            {precoVisivel > 0 ? `${precoVisivel}€` : "--"}
                          </p>
                        </div>
                        <span className="bg-slate-100 text-[#0E3A68] px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest transition-colors group-hover:bg-[#0E3A68] group-hover:text-white shadow-sm">
                          {isEn ? "View Dates" : "Ver Datas"}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </>
        )}
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}

export default function BlogIndexPage({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ lang: string }>;
}) {
  const { lang } = use(params);
  const isEn = lang === "en";

  const [posts, setPosts] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [audiencia, setAudiencia] = useState<"pais" | "parceiros">("pais");
  const [categoriaAtiva, setCategoriaAtiva] = useState("Tudo");

  useEffect(() => {
    async function loadPosts() {
      setLoading(true);
      const { data } = await supabase
        .from("blog_posts")
        .select("*")
        .eq("publicado", true)
        .order("created_at", { ascending: false });

      const bdPosts = data || [];
      const temParceirosNaBD = bdPosts.some((p) => p.audiencia === "parceiros");

      if (!temParceirosNaBD) {
        setPosts([...bdPosts, ...ARTIGOS_B2B_GARANTIDOS]);
      } else {
        setPosts(bdPosts);
      }
      setLoading(false);
    }
    loadPosts();
  }, []);

  const postsDaAudiencia = posts.filter((p) =>
    audiencia === "parceiros"
      ? p.audiencia === "parceiros"
      : p.audiencia !== "parceiros"
  );

  const categorias = [
    "Tudo",
    ...Array.from(
      new Set(
        postsDaAudiencia
          .map((p) => (isEn && p.categoria_en ? p.categoria_en : p.categoria))
          .filter(Boolean)
      )
    ),
  ];

  const postsFiltrados = postsDaAudiencia.filter((p) => {
    if (categoriaAtiva === "Tudo") return true;
    const cat = isEn && p.categoria_en ? p.categoria_en : p.categoria;
    return cat === categoriaAtiva;
  });

  const formatarData = (iso: string) => {
    if (!iso) return "";
    return new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString(isEn ? "en-GB" : "pt-PT", {
      day: "2-digit",
      month: "short",
      year: "numeric",
    });
  };

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-[#F8FAFC] font-sans text-[#0F172A] pb-24">
      {/* HERO VIBRANTE COM O MESMO DESIGN DA PÁGINA PARCEIROS */}
      <section className="relative overflow-hidden bg-[#2563EB] text-white pt-20 pb-28 md:pt-24 md:pb-32 px-4 md:px-8 border-b-8 border-[#F59E0B]">
        <div className="absolute -top-24 -right-24 w-96 h-96 rounded-full bg-[#F59E0B] opacity-90 pointer-events-none hidden lg:block" />
        <div className="absolute bottom-[-60px] left-[-60px] w-72 h-72 rounded-full bg-[#16A34A] opacity-90 pointer-events-none hidden lg:block" />
        <div className="absolute top-1/3 left-10 w-20 h-20 rounded-2xl bg-[#F43F5E] rotate-12 opacity-90 pointer-events-none hidden xl:block" />

        <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto text-center relative z-10">
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-white/15 border border-white/30 rounded-md text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-white mb-6 backdrop-blur-md shadow-sm">
            <span className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-[#F59E0B]" />
            {isEn ? "HelloCamp Editorial & Guides" : "Revista & Guias HelloCamp"}
          </div>

          <h1 className="text-4xl md:text-6xl font-black tracking-tight mb-5 leading-tight text-white">
            {isEn
              ? "Stories, Tips & Camp Guides"
              : "Dicas, Guias e Histórias de Verão"}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-blue-100 text-lg md:text-xl font-medium max-w-2xl mx-auto leading-relaxed">
            {isEn
              ? "Practical articles for families choosing the best summer camp and strategies for partner organizers."
              : "Artigos práticos para famílias escolherem o campo ideal e estratégias de gestão para entidades organizadoras."}
          </p>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* SELETOR DE AUDIÊNCIA E CATEGORIAS */}
      <section className="max-w-6xl mx-auto px-4 md:px-8 py-10 -mt-14 relative z-20">
        <div className="flex justify-center mb-10">
          <div className="flex bg-white p-2 rounded-md shadow-xl border border-slate-200 gap-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setAudiencia("pais");
                setCategoriaAtiva("Tudo");
              }}
              className={`px-7 py-4 rounded-md text-xs font-black tracking-widest uppercase transition-all cursor-pointer border-0 ${
                audiencia === "pais"
                  ? "bg-[#F59E0B] text-white shadow-md"
                  : "bg-transparent text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100"
              }`}
            >
              👨‍👩‍👧 {isEn ? "For Families" : "Para Famílias"}
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setAudiencia("parceiros");
                setCategoriaAtiva("Tudo");
              }}
              className={`px-7 py-4 rounded-md text-xs font-black tracking-widest uppercase transition-all cursor-pointer border-0 ${
                audiencia === "parceiros"
                  ? "bg-[#16A34A] text-white shadow-md"
                  : "bg-transparent text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100"
              }`}
            >
              🏢 {isEn ? "For Organizers" : "Para Organizadores"}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* FILTRO DE CATEGORIAS */}
        {!loading && categorias.length > 1 && (
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3 mb-12 justify-center">
            {categorias.map((cat) => (
              <button
                type="button"
                key={cat}
                onClick={() => setCategoriaAtiva(cat)}
                className={`px-5 py-2.5 rounded-md text-xs font-black tracking-wider uppercase transition-all border shadow-sm cursor-pointer ${
                  categoriaAtiva === cat
                    ? audiencia === "pais"
                      ? "bg-[#2563EB] border-[#2563EB] text-white"
                      : "bg-[#16A34A] border-[#16A34A] text-white"
                    : "bg-white border-slate-200 text-slate-700 hover:border-[#2563EB]"
                }`}
              >
                {cat === "Tudo"
                  ? isEn
                    ? "All Articles"
                    : "Todos os Artigos"
                  : cat}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* GRELHA DE ARTIGOS */}
        {loading ? (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
            {[1, 2, 3].map((i) => (
              <div
                key={i}
                className="bg-white rounded-md border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden animate-pulse"
              >
                <div className="h-60 bg-slate-200" />
                <div className="p-8 space-y-4">
                  <div className="h-4 bg-slate-200 rounded w-1/3" />
                  <div className="h-8 bg-slate-200 rounded w-full" />
                  <div className="h-4 bg-slate-200 rounded w-2/3" />
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
            {postsFiltrados.map((post) => {
              const titulo =
                isEn && post.titulo_en ? post.titulo_en : post.titulo;
              const resumo =
                isEn && post.resumo_en ? post.resumo_en : post.resumo;
              const categoria =
                isEn && post.categoria_en ? post.categoria_en : post.categoria;
              const isPartner = audiencia === "parceiros";
              const destinoHref = post.link_direto
                ? `/${lang}${post.link_direto}`
                : `/${lang}/blog/${post.slug}`;

              return (
                <Link
                  key={post.id}
                  href={destinoHref}
                  className="group bg-white rounded-md border border-slate-200 shadow-sm hover:shadow-xl hover:border-[#2563EB] transition-all duration-300 overflow-hidden flex flex-col hover:-translate-y-1 no-underline"
                >
                  <div className="relative w-full h-60 bg-slate-100 overflow-hidden">
                    <img
                      src={post.imagem_url || FALLBACK_BLOG}
                      onError={(e) => {
                        e.currentTarget.src = FALLBACK_BLOG;
                      }}
                      alt={titulo}
                      loading="lazy"
                      className="w-full h-full object-cover transition-transform duration-700 group-hover:scale-105"
                    />
                    {categoria && (
                      <div
                        className={`absolute top-4 left-4 px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-md text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-white shadow-sm ${
                          isPartner ? "bg-[#16A34A]" : "bg-[#2563EB]"
                        }`}
                      >
                        {categoria}
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <div className="p-7 flex flex-col flex-1">
                    <p className="text-[10px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-2">
                      {formatarData(post.created_at)}
                    </p>
                    <h3 className="text-xl font-black text-[#0F172A] leading-tight mb-3 line-clamp-3 group-hover:text-[#2563EB] transition-colors">
                      {titulo}
                    </h3>
                    <p className="text-slate-600 text-sm font-medium leading-relaxed line-clamp-3 mb-6">
                      {resumo}
                    </p>
                    <div className="mt-auto pt-4 border-t border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between">
                      <span
                        className={`px-5 py-2.5 rounded-md text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-white shadow-sm ${
                          isPartner ? "bg-[#16A34A]" : "bg-[#F59E0B]"
                        }`}
                      >
                        {isEn ? "Read Guide →" : "Ler Guia →"}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </Link>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="app/[lang]/(public)/cliente/favoritos/page.tsx">
"use client";

import React, { useEffect, useState, use } from "react";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";
import Link from "next/link";
import BotaoFavorito from "../../components/BotaoFavorito";

export default function FavoritosCliente({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ lang: string }>;
}) {
  const { lang } = use(params);
  const isEn = lang === "en";

  const [wishlists, setWishlists] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [listaAtivaId, setListaAtivaId] = useState<string>("todas");
  const [camposFavoritos, setCamposFavoritos] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [copiedToken, setCopiedToken] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const carregarFavoritos = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    const {
      data: { session },
    } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
    if (!session) {
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    // 1. Buscar todas as wishlists do utilizador
    const { data: wishData } = await supabase
      .from("wishlists")
      .select("id, nome, token_partilha")
      .eq("user_id", session.user.id)
      .order("created_at", { ascending: false });

    const listas = wishData || [];
    setWishlists(listas);

    if (listas.length === 0) {
      setCamposFavoritos([]);
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    const listasIds = listas.map((l) => l.id);

    // 2. Buscar as ligações wishlist_campos
    const { data: relacoes } = await supabase
      .from("wishlist_campos")
      .select("wishlist_id, campo_id")
      .in("wishlist_id", listasIds);

    if (!relacoes || relacoes.length === 0) {
      setCamposFavoritos([]);
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    const camposIds = Array.from(
      new Set(relacoes.map((r) => r.campo_id).filter(Boolean))
    );

    // 3. Buscar os dados completos dos campos
    const { data: camposData } = await supabase
      .from("campos")
      .select("*")
      .in("id", camposIds);

    const camposComListas = (camposData || []).map((c) => {
      const listasOndeEsta = relacoes
        .filter((r) => r.campo_id === c.id)
        .map((r) => r.wishlist_id);

      let precoMin = Number(c.preco) || 0;
      if (!precoMin && Array.isArray(c.pacotes) && c.pacotes.length > 0) {
        const precos = c.pacotes.flatMap((p: any) =>
          Array.isArray(p.variantes)
            ? p.variantes
                .map((v: any) => Number(v.preco) || 0)
                .filter((v: number) => v > 0)
            : []
        );
        if (precos.length > 0) precoMin = Math.min(...precos);
      }

      return {
        ...c,
        precoCalc: precoMin,
        listasIds: listasOndeEsta,
      };
    });

    setCamposFavoritos(camposComListas);
    setLoading(false);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    carregarFavoritos();
  }, []);

  const partilharLista = async (token: string) => {
    const url = `${window.location.origin}/${lang}/lista/${token}`;
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(url);
      setCopiedToken(token);
      setTimeout(() => setCopiedToken(null), 2500);
    } catch {}
  };

  const camposExibidos =
    listaAtivaId === "todas"
      ? camposFavoritos
      : camposFavoritos.filter((c) => c.listasIds?.includes(listaAtivaId));

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="p-20 text-center font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest text-xs animate-pulse">
        {isEn
          ? "Loading your wishlist..."
          : "A carregar os seus favoritos..."}
      </div>
    );
  }

  const listaSelecionadaObj = wishlists.find((w) => w.id === listaAtivaId);

  return (
    <div className="max-w-[1200px] mx-auto pb-20 font-sans">
      <div className="mb-8 flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between items-start sm:items-end gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl md:text-4xl font-black text-slate-900 m-0 tracking-tight">
            {isEn ? "My Wishlists" : "Os Meus Favoritos"}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 mt-2 text-sm md:text-base font-medium mb-0">
            {isEn
              ? "Holiday programs saved to review and share with family and friends."
              : "Programas de férias guardados para analisar e partilhar em família."}
          </p>
        </div>

        {listaSelecionadaObj?.token_partilha && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => partilharLista(listaSelecionadaObj.token_partilha)}
            className="bg-[#0E3A68] hover:bg-[#0b2b4d] text-white font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-xs px-5 py-3 rounded-xl cursor-pointer shadow-sm transition-colors"
          >
            {copiedToken === listaSelecionadaObj.token_partilha
              ? isEn
                ? "✓ Link Copied!"
                : "✓ Link de Partilha Copiado!"
              : isEn
              ? "🔗 Share This List"
              : "🔗 Partilhar Esta Lista"}
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* BARRA DE LISTAS (ABAS) */}
      {wishlists.length > 0 && (
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 mb-8">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setListaAtivaId("todas")}
            className={`px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest cursor-pointer transition-all ${
              listaAtivaId === "todas"
                ? "bg-[#0E3A68] text-white shadow-sm"
                : "bg-white border border-slate-200 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50"
            }`}
          >
            {isEn ? "All Saved" : "Todos os Guardados"} ({camposFavoritos.length})
          </button>

          {wishlists.map((w) => (
            <div key={w.id} className="flex items-center gap-1">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setListaAtivaId(w.id)}
                className={`px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest cursor-pointer transition-all ${
                  listaAtivaId === w.id
                    ? "bg-[#0E3A68] text-white shadow-sm"
                    : "bg-white border border-slate-200 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50"
                }`}
              >
                ❤️ {w.nome}
              </button>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {camposExibidos.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="text-center p-16 md:p-24 bg-white border border-dashed border-slate-300 rounded-[2rem] shadow-sm">
          <span className="text-5xl block mb-6 opacity-50">❤️</span>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-base md:text-lg mb-8 font-medium">
            {isEn
              ? "Your wishlist is currently empty."
              : "Ainda não adicionou nenhum programa aos seus favoritos."}
          </p>
          <Link
            href={`/${lang}/pesquisa`}
            className="inline-block bg-[#0E3A68] text-white px-8 py-4 rounded-xl font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-xs shadow-md hover:bg-[#0b2b4d] transition-all no-underline"
          >
            {isEn ? "Explore Camps" : "Explorar Programas"}
          </Link>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
          {camposExibidos.map((campo) => {
            const nomeVisivel =
              isEn && campo.nome_en ? campo.nome_en : campo.nome;
            const localVisivel =
              isEn && campo.local_en
                ? campo.local_en
                : campo.Distrito || campo.local;

            return (
              <div
                key={campo.id}
                className="bg-white rounded-[1.5rem] border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden flex flex-col relative shadow-sm hover:shadow-lg hover:border-[#0E3A68]/30 transition-all duration-300 group"
              >
                <div className="absolute top-4 right-4 z-10 bg-white/90 backdrop-blur-md rounded-full shadow-sm">
                  <BotaoFavorito campoId={campo.id} />
                </div>

                <Link
                  href={`/${lang}/campo/${campo.id}`}
                  className="no-underline text-inherit flex flex-col flex-1"
                >
                  <div className="h-48 w-full overflow-hidden bg-slate-100">
                    <img
                      src={campo.imagem || "/og-image.jpg"}
                      alt={nomeVisivel}
                      className="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform duration-700"
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div className="p-6 flex flex-col flex-1">
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-black text-[#e2a41d] uppercase tracking-widest bg-[#e2a41d]/10 px-3 py-1 rounded-md self-start">
                      {campo.categoria || "Geral"}
                    </span>
                    <h3 className="mt-3 mb-2 text-lg font-black text-slate-900 leading-tight group-hover:text-[#0E3A68] transition-colors line-clamp-2">
                      {nomeVisivel}
                    </h3>
                    <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 m-0">
                      📍 {localVisivel}
                    </p>

                    <div className="mt-auto pt-5 border-t border-slate-100 flex justify-between items-center">
                      <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 bg-slate-50 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md">
                        {campo.idade}
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-xl font-black text-[#0E3A68]">
                        {campo.precoCalc > 0
                          ? `${campo.precoCalc}€`
                          : isEn
                          ? "Upon Request"
                          : "Sob Consulta"}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </Link>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="app/[lang]/(public)/cliente/mensagens/page.tsx">
"use client";

import React, { useEffect, useState, use, useRef } from "react";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";

export default function MensagensClientePage({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ lang: string }>;
}) {
  const { lang } = use(params);
  const isEn = lang === "en";

  const [userId, setUserId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [nomePai, setNomePai] = useState<string>("Encarregado de Educação");
  const [emailPai, setEmailPai] = useState<string>("");
  const [threads, setThreads] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [threadAtiva, setThreadAtiva] = useState<any | null>(null);
  const [novaMensagem, setNovaMensagem] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const mensagensEndRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchCaixaDeEntrada();
    const channel = supabase
      .channel("chat_cliente_geral")
      .on(
        "postgres_changes",
        { event: "INSERT", schema: "public", table: "mensagens" },
        () => fetchCaixaDeEntrada()
      )
      .subscribe();

    return () => {
      supabase.removeChannel(channel);
    };
  }, []);

  const fetchCaixaDeEntrada = async () => {
    const {
      data: { session },
    } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
    if (!session) return;
    const uid = session.user.id;
    setUserId(uid);
    setEmailPai(session.user.email || "");

    const { data: meuPerfil } = await supabase
      .from("perfis")
      .select("nome_completo")
      .eq("id", uid)
      .maybeSingle();
    if (meuPerfil?.nome_completo) setNomePai(meuPerfil.nome_completo);

    const [{ data: reservasData }, { data: msgs }] = await Promise.all([
      supabase
        .from("reservas")
        .select("*, criancas(*)")
        .eq("cliente_id", uid),
      supabase
        .from("mensagens")
        .select(
          "*, campos(id, nome, nome_en, organizador_id, local, imagem, preco)"
        )
        .or(`sender_id.eq.${uid},receiver_id.eq.${uid}`)
        .order("created_at", { ascending: true }),
    ]);

    if (!msgs) {
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    const threadsMap: Record<string, any> = {};

    msgs.forEach((m: any) => {
      const campId = m.campo_id || "geral";
      const campoObj = m.campos || {
        id: campId,
        nome: isEn ? "Camp Organizer" : "Organizador HelloCamp",
        organizador_id: m.sender_id === uid ? m.receiver_id : m.sender_id,
        local: "Portugal",
      };

      if (!threadsMap[campId]) {
        const reservasDoCampo =
          reservasData?.filter((r) => r.campo_id === campId) || [];

        threadsMap[campId] = {
          campo: campoObj,
          partner_id:
            campoObj.organizador_id ||
            (m.sender_id === uid ? m.receiver_id : m.sender_id),
          mensagens: [],
          reservas: reservasDoCampo,
          unread: 0,
          lastMsg: null,
        };
      }

      threadsMap[campId].mensagens.push(m);
      threadsMap[campId].lastMsg = m;
      if (!m.lida && m.receiver_id === uid) {
        threadsMap[campId].unread++;
      }
    });

    const threadsArray = Object.values(threadsMap).sort(
      (a: any, b: any) =>
        new Date(b.lastMsg.created_at).getTime() -
        new Date(a.lastMsg.created_at).getTime()
    );

    setThreads(threadsArray);
    setThreadAtiva((prev: any) =>
      prev ? threadsMap[prev.campo.id] || null : threadsArray[0] || null
    );
    setLoading(false);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    mensagensEndRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth" });
  }, [threadAtiva?.mensagens?.length]);

  const abrirConversa = async (thread: any) => {
    setThreadAtiva(thread);
    if (thread.unread > 0 && userId) {
      await supabase
        .from("mensagens")
        .update({ lida: true })
        .eq("campo_id", thread.campo.id)
        .eq("receiver_id", userId)
        .eq("lida", false);
      fetchCaixaDeEntrada();
    }
  };

  const enviarMensagem = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!novaMensagem.trim() || !threadAtiva || !userId) return;

    const msgTexto = novaMensagem.trim();
    setNovaMensagem("");

    const novaMsgVisual = {
      id: Date.now(),
      sender_id: userId,
      texto: msgTexto,
      created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    };

    setThreadAtiva((prev: any) => ({
      ...prev,
      mensagens: [...prev.mensagens, novaMsgVisual],
    }));

    await supabase.from("mensagens").insert([
      {
        campo_id:
          threadAtiva.campo.id === "geral" ? null : threadAtiva.campo.id,
        sender_id: userId,
        receiver_id: threadAtiva.partner_id,
        texto: msgTexto,
        lida: false,
      },
    ]);

    // Notifica também o parceiro por e-mail!
    if (threadAtiva.partner_id) {
      const { data: orgPerfil } = await supabase
        .from("perfis")
        .select("email")
        .eq("id", threadAtiva.partner_id)
        .maybeSingle();

      if (orgPerfil?.email) {
        fetch("/api/send-contact", {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            nome: nomePai,
            emailCliente: emailPai || "info@hellocamp.pt",
            emailCampo: orgPerfil.email,
            nomeCampo: threadAtiva.campo.nome || "HelloCamp",
            mensagem: msgTexto,
          }),
        }).catch(() => {});
      }
    }

    fetchCaixaDeEntrada();
  };

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="p-16 text-center font-bold text-slate-400 text-xs uppercase tracking-widest animate-pulse">
        {isEn ? "Loading Messages..." : "A carregar as suas conversas..."}
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 shadow-sm overflow-hidden flex h-[calc(100vh-200px)] min-h-[550px]">
      {/* BARRA LATERAL ESQUERDA: LISTA DE CHATS */}
      <div
        className={`w-full md:w-[340px] border-r border-slate-200 flex-col bg-slate-50/50 ${
          threadAtiva ? "hidden md:flex" : "flex"
        }`}
      >
        <div className="p-6 border-b border-slate-200 bg-white">
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-900 m-0 tracking-tight">
            {isEn ? "Messages" : "Mensagens"}
          </h2>
        </div>
        <div className="overflow-y-auto flex-1">
          {threads.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="p-8 text-center text-slate-400 font-bold text-sm h-full flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
              <span className="text-5xl mb-4">📭</span>
              {isEn
                ? "No conversations started yet."
                : "Ainda não iniciou nenhuma conversa com organizadores."}
            </div>
          ) : (
            threads.map((t) => {
              const nomeCampo =
                isEn && t.campo.nome_en ? t.campo.nome_en : t.campo.nome;
              const isActive = threadAtiva?.campo.id === t.campo.id;
              return (
                <div
                  key={t.campo.id}
                  onClick={() => abrirConversa(t)}
                  className={`p-5 border-b border-slate-100 cursor-pointer transition-colors relative flex gap-4 ${
                    isActive
                      ? "bg-[#0E3A68] text-white"
                      : "hover:bg-slate-100/70"
                  }`}
                >
                  <div
                    className={`w-12 h-12 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-xl flex-shrink-0 shadow-sm ${
                      isActive ? "bg-white/20" : "bg-slate-100"
                    }`}
                  >
                    ⛺
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 flex flex-col justify-center">
                    <h4
                      className={`m-0 text-sm font-bold truncate mb-1 pr-4 ${
                        isActive ? "text-white" : "text-slate-900"
                      }`}
                    >
                      {nomeCampo}
                    </h4>
                    <p
                      className={`m-0 text-xs truncate ${
                        isActive
                          ? "text-white/70"
                          : "text-slate-500 font-medium"
                      }`}
                    >
                      {t.lastMsg?.texto}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                  {t.unread > 0 && !isActive && (
                    <div className="absolute right-5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-3 h-3 bg-[#e2a41d] rounded-full shadow-sm ring-2 ring-white"></div>
                  )}
                </div>
              );
            })
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* JANELA CENTRAL: CHAT */}
      <div
        className={`flex-1 flex-col bg-slate-50/50 ${
          !threadAtiva ? "hidden md:flex" : "flex"
        }`}
      >
        {!threadAtiva ? (
          <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-slate-400 font-bold text-sm">
            <div className="w-20 h-20 bg-slate-200 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-3xl mb-4">
              💬
            </div>
            {isEn
              ? "Select a conversation"
              : "Selecione uma conversa ao lado para responder."}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <>
            <div className="p-4 md:p-6 border-b border-slate-200 bg-white flex items-center gap-4 shadow-sm z-10">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setThreadAtiva(null)}
                className="md:hidden w-10 h-10 flex items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 font-black cursor-pointer"
              >
                &larr;
              </button>
              <h3 className="m-0 text-base md:text-lg font-black text-slate-900 truncate">
                {isEn && threadAtiva.campo.nome_en
                  ? threadAtiva.campo.nome_en
                  : threadAtiva.campo.nome}
              </h3>
            </div>

            <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 md:p-6 flex flex-col gap-4">
              {threadAtiva.mensagens.map((msg: any, i: number) => {
                const isMine = msg.sender_id === userId;
                return (
                  <div
                    key={msg.id || i}
                    className={`flex ${
                      isMine ? "justify-end" : "justify-start"
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div
                      className={`max-w-[85%] md:max-w-[70%] p-4 md:p-5 rounded-[1.5rem] text-sm font-medium leading-relaxed shadow-sm whitespace-pre-wrap ${
                        isMine
                          ? "bg-[#0E3A68] text-white rounded-br-sm"
                          : "bg-white text-slate-800 border border-slate-200 rounded-bl-sm"
                      }`}
                    >
                      {msg.texto}
                      <div
                        className={`text-[10px] mt-2 text-right font-black uppercase tracking-wider ${
                          isMine ? "text-white/60" : "text-slate-400"
                        }`}
                      >
                        {new Date(msg.created_at).toLocaleTimeString([], {
                          hour: "2-digit",
                          minute: "2-digit",
                        })}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
              <div ref={mensagensEndRef} />
            </div>

            <form
              onSubmit={enviarMensagem}
              className="p-4 md:p-6 bg-white border-t border-slate-200 flex gap-3"
            >
              <input
                type="text"
                value={novaMensagem}
                onChange={(e) => setNovaMensagem(e.target.value)}
                placeholder={
                  isEn
                    ? "Type a message to the organizer..."
                    : "Escreva a sua mensagem para o organizador..."
                }
                className="flex-1 px-4 py-3.5 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl outline-none text-sm font-bold text-slate-800 focus:border-[#0E3A68] focus:bg-white"
              />
              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={!novaMensagem.trim()}
                className="px-6 py-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-[#0E3A68] disabled:bg-slate-200 text-white font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-xs cursor-pointer transition-colors"
              >
                {isEn ? "Send" : "Enviar"}
              </button>
            </form>
          </>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="app/[lang]/(public)/components/FormContactoCampo.tsx">
"use client";

import React, { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";
import Link from "next/link";

export default function FormContactoCampo({
  campoId,
  organizadorId,
  nomeCampo,
  dict,
  isEn,
  lang,
}: any) {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [sucesso, setSucesso] = useState(false);
  const [isLogged, setIsLogged] = useState(false);

  const [form, setForm] = useState({
    nome: "",
    apelido: "",
    email: "",
    telefone: "",
    idade: "",
    mensagem: "",
  });

  // Se o pai já estiver logado, pré-preenche os dados para facilitar!
  useEffect(() => {
    const prefillUser = async () => {
      const {
        data: { session },
      } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
      if (!session?.user) return;
      setIsLogged(true);

      const { data: perfil } = await supabase
        .from("perfis")
        .select("nome_completo, email, telefone")
        .eq("id", session.user.id)
        .maybeSingle();

      const partesNome = (
        perfil?.nome_completo ||
        session.user.user_metadata?.nome_completo ||
        ""
      ).split(" ");
      setForm((prev) => ({
        ...prev,
        nome: partesNome[0] || "",
        apelido: partesNome.slice(1).join(" ") || "",
        email: perfil?.email || session.user.email || "",
        telefone: perfil?.telefone || "",
      }));
    };
    prefillUser();
  }, []);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      const formData = new FormData();
      formData.append("First_Name", form.nome);
      formData.append("Last_Name", form.apelido);
      formData.append("Email", form.email);
      formData.append("Phone", form.telefone);
      formData.append("Age", form.idade);
      formData.append("Message", form.mensagem);
      formData.append(
        "_subject",
        isEn
          ? `New Question regarding ${nomeCampo} - HelloCamp`
          : `Nova Dúvida sobre ${nomeCampo} - HelloCamp`
      );
      formData.append("campo_id", String(campoId || ""));
      formData.append("organizador_id", String(organizadorId || ""));
      formData.append("lang", lang || "pt");

      const res = await fetch("/api/enviar-duvida", {
        method: "POST",
        body: formData,
      });

      if (!res.ok) {
        const errData = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
        throw new Error(errData.error || "Erro ao enviar mensagem.");
      }

      setSucesso(true);
      setForm((prev) => ({ ...prev, mensagem: "" }));
    } catch (err: any) {
      alert(err.message);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  if (sucesso) {
    return (
      <div className="bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 p-6 rounded-2xl text-center">
        <span className="text-4xl block mb-3">✅</span>
        <h4 className="text-base font-black text-[#167524] m-0 mb-1">
          {isEn
            ? "Message Sent to Organizer!"
            : "Mensagem Enviada ao Organizador!"}
        </h4>
        <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 m-0 mb-4 leading-relaxed">
          {isEn
            ? "The camp organizer received your question and will reply to your email and in your HelloCamp Inbox."
            : "O organizador já recebeu a sua questão na Inbox e responderá para o seu e-mail e no seu Portal de Cliente."}
        </p>
        <div className="flex justify-center gap-3">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setSucesso(false)}
            className="px-4 py-2.5 bg-white border border-emerald-200 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-[#167524] cursor-pointer"
          >
            {isEn ? "Send another question" : "Enviar outra questão"}
          </button>
          {isLogged && (
            <Link
              href={`/${lang}/cliente/mensagens`}
              className="px-5 py-2.5 bg-[#0E3A68] text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest no-underline"
            >
              {isEn ? "Open My Inbox" : "Ver nas Minhas Mensagens"}
            </Link>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
        <div>
          <label className="block text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400 mb-1.5 ml-1">
            {isEn ? "First Name *" : "Primeiro Nome *"}
          </label>
          <input
            type="text"
            required
            value={form.nome}
            onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, nome: e.target.value })}
            className="w-full p-3.5 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 outline-none focus:border-[#0E3A68] focus:bg-white"
          />
        </div>
        <div>
          <label className="block text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400 mb-1.5 ml-1">
            {isEn ? "Last Name" : "Apelido"}
          </label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={form.apelido}
            onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, apelido: e.target.value })}
            className="w-full p-3.5 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 outline-none focus:border-[#0E3A68] focus:bg-white"
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
        <div>
          <label className="block text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400 mb-1.5 ml-1">
            E-mail *
          </label>
          <input
            type="email"
            required
            value={form.email}
            onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, email: e.target.value })}
            className="w-full p-3.5 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 outline-none focus:border-[#0E3A68] focus:bg-white"
          />
        </div>
        <div>
          <label className="block text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400 mb-1.5 ml-1">
            {isEn ? "Phone *" : "Telemóvel *"}
          </label>
          <input
            type="tel"
            required
            value={form.telefone}
            onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, telefone: e.target.value })}
            className="w-full p-3.5 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 outline-none focus:border-[#0E3A68] focus:bg-white"
          />
        </div>
        <div>
          <label className="block text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400 mb-1.5 ml-1">
            {isEn ? "Child's Age" : "Idade do Participante"}
          </label>
          <input
            type="number"
            min="3"
            max="22"
            value={form.idade}
            onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, idade: e.target.value })}
            placeholder={isEn ? "e.g., 10" : "Ex: 10"}
            className="w-full p-3.5 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 outline-none focus:border-[#0E3A68] focus:bg-white"
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div>
        <label className="block text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400 mb-1.5 ml-1">
          {isEn ? "Your Question *" : "A sua Mensagem / Dúvida *"}
        </label>
        <textarea
          rows={4}
          required
          value={form.mensagem}
          onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, mensagem: e.target.value })}
          placeholder={
            isEn
              ? "Write your question to the camp organizer..."
              : "Escreva aqui a sua dúvida sobre horários, vagas, alimentação ou atividades..."
          }
          className="w-full p-4 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-medium text-slate-900 outline-none focus:border-[#0E3A68] focus:bg-white resize-none"
        />
      </div>

      <button
        type="submit"
        disabled={loading}
        className="w-full sm:w-auto px-8 py-4 bg-[#0E3A68] hover:bg-[#0b2b4d] disabled:bg-slate-300 text-white font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-xs rounded-xl shadow-md cursor-pointer transition-all"
      >
        {loading
          ? isEn
            ? "Sending..."
            : "A enviar..."
          : isEn
          ? "Send Question to Organizer"
          : "Enviar Dúvida ao Organizador"}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="app/[lang]/(public)/institucional/distrito/[slug]/page.tsx">
"use client";

import React, { useEffect, useState, use } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";

const FALLBACK_IMG =
  "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1540479859555-17af45c78602?auto=format&fit=crop&q=80&w=800";

export default function InstitucionalDistritoPage({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ lang: string; slug: string }>;
}) {
  const { lang, slug } = use(params);
  const isEn = lang === "en";

  const preposicoes = ["de", "da", "do", "das", "dos"];
  const nomeDistrito = decodeURIComponent(slug)
    .replace(/-/g, " ")
    .split(" ")
    .map((w, idx) => {
      const lower = w.toLowerCase();
      if (idx > 0 && preposicoes.includes(lower)) return lower;
      return lower.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + lower.slice(1);
    })
    .join(" ");

  const [iniciativas, setIniciativas] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchPorDistrito = async () => {
      setLoading(true);
      try {
        const res = await fetch(
          `/api/institucional?distrito=${encodeURIComponent(nomeDistrito)}`,
          { cache: "no-store" }
        );
        if (res.ok) {
          const json = await res.json();
          setIniciativas(json.data || []);
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.error(e);
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    };
    fetchPorDistrito();
  }, [nomeDistrito]);

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 font-sans pb-24">
      <div className="bg-[#0E3A68] pt-14 pb-14 px-6 text-center border-b border-slate-800">
        <span className="inline-block bg-white/10 text-[#e2a41d] text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest px-3 py-1 rounded-sm mb-3">
          🏛️ {isEn ? "Municipal Directory" : "Diretório Autárquico"}
        </span>
        <h1 className="text-3xl md:text-5xl font-black text-white mb-3">
          {isEn
            ? `Municipal Holiday Camps in ${nomeDistrito}`
            : `Campos de Férias Municipais em ${nomeDistrito}`}
        </h1>
        <p className="text-blue-100 font-medium max-w-2xl mx-auto m-0 text-sm md:text-base">
          {isEn
            ? "Explore all municipal and parish programs available in your region."
            : "Explore todos os programas de Câmaras Municipais e Juntas de Freguesia disponíveis na sua região."}
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 md:px-6 py-10">
        <Link
          href={`/${lang}/institucional`}
          className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-600 bg-white border border-slate-300 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-md hover:text-[#0E3A68] mb-8 inline-flex items-center gap-2 no-underline"
        >
          &larr;{" "}
          {isEn ? "Back to all municipalities" : "Voltar a todas as Autarquias"}
        </Link>

        {loading ? (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
            {[1, 2, 3].map((i) => (
              <div
                key={i}
                className="bg-white rounded-md border border-slate-200 h-72 animate-pulse"
              />
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : iniciativas.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="text-center py-16 bg-white rounded-md border border-slate-200">
            <span className="text-4xl block mb-3">🏛️</span>
            <p className="text-slate-600 font-bold text-sm">
              {isEn
                ? `No municipal programs found for ${nomeDistrito}.`
                : `Nenhum programa autárquico encontrado para o distrito de ${nomeDistrito}.`}
            </p>
            <Link
              href={`/${lang}/institucional`}
              className="mt-4 inline-block bg-[#0E3A68] text-white text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest px-6 py-3 rounded-md no-underline"
            >
              {isEn ? "See all programs" : "Ver todas as Autarquias"}
            </Link>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
            {iniciativas.map((item) => (
              <div
                key={item.id}
                className="flex flex-col bg-white rounded-md shadow-sm border border-slate-200 hover:border-[#0E3A68] transition-all overflow-hidden"
              >
                <div className="relative h-44 w-full bg-slate-100 overflow-hidden">
                  <img
                    loading="lazy"
                    src={item.imagem_capa_url || FALLBACK_IMG}
                    onError={(e) => {
                      e.currentTarget.src = FALLBACK_IMG;
                    }}
                    alt={item.titulo}
                    className="w-full h-full object-cover"
                  />
                  <span className="absolute top-2.5 left-2.5 bg-slate-950/90 text-white text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest px-2.5 py-1 rounded-sm">
                    📍 {item.concelho || item.distrito}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex flex-col p-5 flex-1">
                  <p className="text-[11px] font-black uppercase text-[#0E3A68] m-0 mb-1">
                    🏛️ {item.entidade_organizadora}
                  </p>
                  <h3 className="text-base font-black text-slate-900 leading-snug mb-4 line-clamp-2">
                    {item.titulo}
                  </h3>
                  <div className="mt-auto grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                    <Link
                      href={`/${lang}/institucional/${item.id}`}
                      className="text-center bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 text-white font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-[10px] py-3 rounded-md no-underline"
                    >
                      {isEn ? "Details" : "Ver Detalhes"}
                    </Link>
                    {item.link_oficial && (
                      <a
                        href={item.link_oficial}
                        target="_blank"
                        rel="noopener noreferrer"
                        className="text-center bg-[#167524] hover:bg-[#115a1b] text-white font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-[10px] py-3 rounded-md no-underline"
                      >
                        Site Oficial ↗
                      </a>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

import dynamic from "next/dynamic";

const MapaMapLibre = dynamic(() => import("./MapaMapLibre"), {

<file path="app/[lang]/(public)/monitores/portal/mensagens/page.tsx">
"use client";

import React, { useEffect, useState, use } from "react";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import Link from "next/link";

export default function MonitorMensagensRecrutamentoPage({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ lang: string }>;
}) {
  const { lang } = use(params);
  const isEn = lang === "en";
  const router = useRouter();

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [monitorId, setMonitorId] = useState<string>("");
  const [monitorPerfil, setMonitorPerfil] = useState<any>(null);
  const [conversas, setConversas] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [conversaAtiva, setConversaAtiva] = useState<any>(null);
  const [textoResposta, setTextoResposta] = useState("");
  const [sending, setSending] = useState(false);

  const carregarMensagensMonitor = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    const {
      data: { session },
    } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
    if (!session) {
      router.push(`/${lang}/login`);
      return;
    }

    const mId = session.user.id;
    setMonitorId(mId);

    const [{ data: profile }, { data: msgData }] = await Promise.all([
      supabase
        .from("monitores")
        .select("nome_completo, fotografia_url")
        .eq("id", mId)
        .maybeSingle(),
      supabase
        .from("mensagens_recrutamento")
        .select("*, perfis:parceiro_id(id, empresa_nome, nome_empresa, nome, email, logotipo_url)")
        .eq("monitor_id", mId)
        .order("criado_at", { ascending: true }),
    ]);

    setMonitorPerfil(profile);

    if (msgData) {
      const threadsMap: Record<string, any> = {};

      msgData.forEach((msg: any) => {
        const pId = msg.parceiro_id;
        const nomeOrg =
          msg.perfis?.empresa_nome ||
          msg.perfis?.nome_empresa ||
          msg.perfis?.nome ||
          (isEn ? "Camp Organizer" : "Organizador de Campo");

        if (!threadsMap[pId]) {
          threadsMap[pId] = {
            parceiro_id: pId,
            parceiro_nome: nomeOrg,
            parceiro_email: msg.perfis?.email || "",
            parceiro_logo: msg.perfis?.logotipo_url || "",
            vaga: isEn ? "Camp Staff Opportunity" : "Proposta de Recrutamento",
            mensagens: [],
            ultima_mensagem: "",
            data: "",
            lida: true,
          };
        }
        threadsMap[pId].mensagens.push(msg);
        threadsMap[pId].ultima_mensagem = msg.mensagem;
        threadsMap[pId].data = new Date(msg.criado_at).toLocaleDateString(
          "pt-PT",
          { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" }
        );
        if (msg.remetente === "parceiro" && !msg.lida) {
          threadsMap[pId].lida = false;
        }
      });

      const lista = Object.values(threadsMap);
      setConversas(lista);
      if (conversaAtiva) {
        setConversaAtiva(threadsMap[conversaAtiva.parceiro_id] || null);
      } else if (lista.length > 0) {
        selecionarConversa(lista[0], mId);
      }
    }

    setLoading(false);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    carregarMensagensMonitor();
  }, [lang]);

  const marcarComoLidas = async (pId: string, uidRef?: string) => {
    const targetId = uidRef || monitorId;
    if (!targetId) return;
    await supabase
      .from("mensagens_recrutamento")
      .update({ lida: true })
      .eq("monitor_id", targetId)
      .eq("parceiro_id", pId)
      .eq("remetente", "parceiro");
  };

  const selecionarConversa = (conv: any, uidRef?: string) => {
    setConversaAtiva({ ...conv, lida: true });
    setConversas((prev) =>
      prev.map((c) =>
        c.parceiro_id === conv.parceiro_id ? { ...c, lida: true } : c
      )
    );
    marcarComoLidas(conv.parceiro_id, uidRef);
  };

  const handleEnviarResposta = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!textoResposta.trim() || !conversaAtiva || !monitorId) return;

    setSending(true);
    try {
      const msgTxt = textoResposta.trim();
      const { data: nova, error } = await supabase
        .from("mensagens_recrutamento")
        .insert([
          {
            parceiro_id: conversaAtiva.parceiro_id,
            monitor_id: monitorId,
            remetente: "monitor",
            mensagem: msgTxt,
            lida: false,
          },
        ])
        .select()
        .single();

      if (error) throw error;

      const atualizada = {
        ...conversaAtiva,
        mensagens: [...conversaAtiva.mensagens, nova],
        ultima_mensagem: msgTxt,
      };
      setConversaAtiva(atualizada);
      setConversas((prev) =>
        prev.map((c) =>
          c.parceiro_id === conversaAtiva.parceiro_id ? atualizada : c
        )
      );
      setTextoResposta("");

      // Dispara e-mail de notificação ao Parceiro
      if (conversaAtiva.parceiro_email) {
        await fetch("/api/notificacoes/nova-mensagem-recrutamento", {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            destinatarioEmail: conversaAtiva.parceiro_email,
            destinatarioNome: conversaAtiva.parceiro_nome,
            remetenteNome: monitorPerfil?.nome_completo || "Monitor HelloCamp",
            mensagemTexto: msgTxt,
            tipoDestinatario: "parceiro",
            lang,
          }),
        }).catch(() => {});
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      alert("Erro ao enviar resposta: " + err.message);
    } finally {
      setSending(false);
    }
  };

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="p-20 text-center font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest text-xs animate-pulse">
        {isEn
          ? "Loading your job invitations..."
          : "A carregar os teus convites de emprego..."}
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-[#F8FAFC] py-10 px-4 font-sans text-[#0F172A] pb-24">
      <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto space-y-6">
        {/* TOPO */}
        <div className="bg-[#0F172A] text-white p-6 md:p-8 rounded-md border-b-4 border-[#F59E0B] flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
          <div>
            <span className="bg-[#F59E0B] text-white text-[9px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md inline-block mb-1.5">
              💬 {isEn ? "Recruitment Inbox" : "Caixa de Convites de Trabalho"}
            </span>
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-black m-0">
              {isEn
                ? "Job Offers & Organizer Messages"
                : "Propostas e Mensagens de Organizadores"}
            </h1>
          </div>

          <Link
            href={`/${lang}/monitores/portal/perfil`}
            className="bg-[#2563EB] hover:bg-blue-700 text-white font-black text-xs uppercase tracking-widest px-5 py-3 rounded-md no-underline shrink-0"
          >
            &larr; {isEn ? "Back to My Profile" : "Voltar ao Meu Perfil"}
          </Link>
        </div>

        {/* JANELA DE CHAT */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-md border border-slate-200 shadow-sm grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 min-h-[540px] overflow-hidden">
          <div className="lg:col-span-4 border-r border-slate-200 bg-[#F8FAFC] overflow-y-auto max-h-[600px]">
            {conversas.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="p-10 text-center text-xs font-bold text-slate-400">
                {isEn
                  ? "No job invitations received yet. Keep your availability calendar updated!"
                  : "Ainda não recebeste propostas. Mantém o teu calendário e certificações atualizados no teu perfil!"}
              </div>
            ) : (
              conversas.map((conv: any) => {
                const isSel =
                  conversaAtiva?.parceiro_id === conv.parceiro_id;
                return (
                  <div
                    key={conv.parceiro_id}
                    onClick={() => selecionarConversa(conv)}
                    className={`p-4 border-b border-slate-200 cursor-pointer transition-colors ${
                      isSel
                        ? "bg-[#2563EB] text-white"
                        : "bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 text-[#0F172A]"
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-1">
                      <strong className="text-xs font-black truncate">
                        🏢 {conv.parceiro_nome}
                      </strong>
                      {!conv.lida && (
                        <span className="bg-[#F43F5E] text-white text-[9px] font-black px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md">
                          NOVA
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <p
                      className={`text-xs m-0 truncate ${
                        isSel ? "text-blue-100" : "text-slate-500"
                      }`}
                    >
                      {conv.ultima_mensagem}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                );
              })
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="lg:col-span-8 flex flex-col justify-between bg-white">
            {!conversaAtiva ? (
              <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-12 text-center">
                <span className="text-4xl mb-3">🏕️</span>
                <h3 className="text-base font-black text-[#0F172A] m-0 mb-1">
                  Seleciona uma proposta à esquerda
                </h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-bold m-0">
                  Responde diretamente aos diretores dos campos de férias.
                </p>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <>
                <div className="p-5 bg-[#0F172A] text-white flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-800">
                  <div>
                    <span className="bg-[#16A34A] text-white text-[9px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md inline-block mb-1">
                      Organizador Verificado HelloCamp
                    </span>
                    <h3 className="text-base font-black m-0">
                      🏢 {conversaAtiva.parceiro_nome}
                    </h3>
                  </div>
                  <Link
                    href={`/${lang}/parceiro/${conversaAtiva.parceiro_id}`}
                    target="_blank"
                    className="bg-white/10 hover:bg-white/20 text-white text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest px-3 py-2 rounded-md no-underline"
                  >
                    Ver Campos do Organizador ↗
                  </Link>
                </div>

                <div className="flex-1 p-6 overflow-y-auto space-y-4 max-h-[420px] bg-[#F8FAFC]">
                  {conversaAtiva.mensagens.map((m: any, i: number) => {
                    const souEu = m.remetente === "monitor";
                    return (
                      <div
                        key={m.id || i}
                        className={`p-4 rounded-md max-w-[82%] shadow-sm ${
                          souEu
                            ? "ml-auto bg-[#16A34A] text-white"
                            : "mr-auto bg-white border border-slate-200 text-[#0F172A]"
                        }`}
                      >
                        <div className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-wider mb-1 opacity-80">
                          {souEu
                            ? monitorPerfil?.nome_completo || "Eu"
                            : conversaAtiva.parceiro_nome}
                        </div>
                        <p className="text-xs md:text-sm font-medium whitespace-pre-wrap leading-relaxed m-0">
                          {m.mensagem}
                        </p>
                        <span
                          className={`block text-[9px] mt-2 ${
                            souEu ? "text-emerald-100" : "text-slate-400"
                          }`}
                        >
                          {new Date(m.criado_at).toLocaleString("pt-PT")}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>

                <form
                  onSubmit={handleEnviarResposta}
                  className="p-4 bg-white border-t border-slate-200 flex gap-3"
                >
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    value={textoResposta}
                    onChange={(e) => setTextoResposta(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Escreve a tua resposta ao organizador..."
                    className="flex-1 py-3.5 px-4 bg-[#F8FAFC] border border-slate-300 rounded-md text-xs font-bold text-[#0F172A] outline-none focus:border-[#2563EB]"
                  />
                  <button
                    type="submit"
                    disabled={sending}
                    className="bg-[#16A34A] hover:bg-green-700 text-white font-black text-xs uppercase tracking-widest px-6 py-3.5 rounded-md cursor-pointer border-0 shrink-0"
                  >
                    {sending ? "..." : "Responder →"}
                  </button>
                </form>
              </>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}
