<file path="app/[lang]/(public)/pesquisa/pais/[paisNome]/page.tsx">
import { Metadata } from "next";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";
import Link from "next/link";
import BotaoFavorito from "../../../../(public)/components/BotaoFavorito";
import { unstable_cache } from "next/cache";

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

const escapeOr = (str: string) => str.replace(/[,()]/g, ' ').trim();

// ==========================================
// CACHE DE DADOS (24 HORAS) - ZERO EGRESS
// ==========================================
const getPaisCache = unstable_cache(
  async (nomePais: string) => {
    const { data } = await supabase.from('paises').select('*').ilike('nome', nomePais).single();
    return data;
  },
  ['cache-pais-detalhe'],
  { revalidate: 86400 }
);

const getCamposPorPaisCache = unstable_cache(
  async (nomePais: string) => {
    const { data } = await supabase
      .from("campos")
      .select("*")
      .not('contrato_parceiro_url', 'is', null)
      .or(`pais.ilike.%${nomePais}%,pais_en.ilike.%${nomePais}%`)
      .order('id', { ascending: false });
    return data || [];
  },
  ['cache-campos-pais'],
  { revalidate: 86400 }
);

// ==========================================
// 1. SEO METADATA (Com Tradução Segura)
// ==========================================
export async function generateMetadata({
  params
}: {
  params: Promise<{ lang: string; paisNome: string }>
}): Promise<Metadata> {
  const { lang, paisNome } = await params;
  const isEn = lang === 'en';
  const nomeLimpo = decodeURIComponent(paisNome).replace(/[%_]/g, '');

  const pais = await getPaisCache(nomeLimpo);

  // Fallback de Tradução Seguro
  const fallbackEN: Record<string, string> = { "Reino Unido": "United Kingdom", "Espanha": "Spain", "França": "France", "Estados Unidos": "United States", "Portugal": "Portugal" };
  const nomeIngles = fallbackEN[nomeLimpo] || nomeLimpo;
  const nomeFinalApresentar = isEn ? (pais?.nome_en || nomeIngles) : (pais?.nome || nomeLimpo);

  if (!pais) {
    return { title: isEn ? `Camps in ${nomeFinalApresentar} | HelloCamp` : `Campos em ${nomeFinalApresentar} | HelloCamp` };
  }

  const title = isEn
    ? (pais.seo_titulo_en || `Summer Camps in ${nomeFinalApresentar} | HelloCamp`)
    : (pais.seo_titulo || `Campos de Férias em ${nomeFinalApresentar} | HelloCamp`);

  const description = isEn ? pais.seo_descricao_en : pais.seo_descricao;

  return {
    title: title,
    description: description,
    openGraph: {
      title: title,
      description: description || '',
    }
  };
}

// ==========================================
// 2. COMPONENTE PRINCIPAL
// ==========================================
export default async function PesquisaPorPais({
  params,
  searchParams
}: {
  params: Promise<{ lang: string; paisNome: string }>;
  searchParams?: Promise<{ [key: string]: string | undefined }>
}) {
  const { lang, paisNome } = await params;
  const sp = await searchParams;
  const isEn = lang === 'en';

  const nomePaisInicial = decodeURIComponent(paisNome).replace(/[%_]/g, '');

  const categoriaParam = sp?.categoria || "";
  const distritoParam = sp?.distrito || "";
  const idadeParam = sp?.idade || "";
  const dataParam = sp?.data || "";

  const mostrarDistritos = nomePaisInicial.toLowerCase() === "portugal";

  const dadosPais = await getPaisCache(nomePaisInicial);
  const todosOsCamposDoPais = await getCamposPorPaisCache(nomePaisInicial);

  let resultados = todosOsCamposDoPais;

  if (categoriaParam) {
    resultados = resultados.filter(c => c.categoria === categoriaParam);
  }
  if (distritoParam && mostrarDistritos) {
    resultados = resultados.filter(c => (c.Distrito || '').toLowerCase().includes(escapeOr(distritoParam).toLowerCase()));
  }
  if (idadeParam) {
    resultados = resultados.filter(c => c.idade === idadeParam);
  }
  if (dataParam) {
    resultados = resultados.filter(c => {
      if (!c.data_inicio) return false;
      return new Date(c.data_inicio) >= new Date(dataParam);
    });
  }

  // Fallback de Tradução Seguro para a Interface
  const fallbackEN: Record<string, string> = { "Reino Unido": "United Kingdom", "Espanha": "Spain", "França": "France", "Estados Unidos": "United States", "Portugal": "Portugal" };
  const nomeIngles = fallbackEN[nomePaisInicial] || nomePaisInicial;
  const nomePaisApresentar = isEn ? (dadosPais?.nome_en || nomeIngles) : (dadosPais?.nome || nomePaisInicial);

  const descricaoPadrao = isEn
    ? `Explore premium holiday camps in ${nomePaisApresentar}. From thrilling outdoor adventures and intensive sports clubs to creative arts and language classes, discover handpicked programs.`
    : `Explore os melhores campos de férias em ${nomePaisApresentar}. Desde aventuras ao ar livre e atividades desportivas até programas de artes e línguas, descubra opções premium desenhadas para experiências inesquecíveis.`;

  const descPaisFinal = isEn
    ? (dadosPais?.seo_descricao_en || descricaoPadrao)
    : (dadosPais?.seo_descricao || descricaoPadrao);

  const distritosPT = ["Aveiro", "Beja", "Braga", "Bragança", "Castelo Branco", "Coimbra", "Évora", "Faro", "Guarda", "Leiria", "Lisboa", "Portalegre", "Porto", "Santarém", "Setúbal", "Viana do Castelo", "Vila Real", "Viseu"];

  const baseUrl = "https://www.hellocamp.pt";
  const breadcrumbSchema = {
    "@context": "https://schema.org",
    "@type": "BreadcrumbList",
    "itemListElement": [
      { "@type": "ListItem", "position": 1, "name": isEn ? "Home" : "Início", "item": `${baseUrl}/${lang}` },
      { "@type": "ListItem", "position": 2, "name": isEn ? "Countries" : "Países", "item": `${baseUrl}/${lang}/pesquisa` },
      { "@type": "ListItem", "position": 3, "name": nomePaisApresentar, "item": `${baseUrl}/${lang}/pesquisa/pais/${encodeURIComponent(nomePaisInicial)}` }
    ]
  };

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 text-slate-900 font-sans pb-20">
      <script type="application/ld+json" dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: JSON.stringify(breadcrumbSchema) }} />

      <section className="bg-white pt-10 pb-8 px-4 md:px-6 border-b border-slate-200 shadow-sm relative z-20">
        <div className="max-w-[1100px] mx-auto">
          <nav className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-black text-slate-400 mb-6 tracking-widest uppercase">
            <Link href={`/${lang}`} className="hover:text-slate-900 transition-colors">{isEn ? 'Home' : 'Início'}</Link>
            <span>/</span>
            <Link href={`/${lang}/pesquisa`} className="hover:text-slate-900 transition-colors">{isEn ? 'Countries' : 'Países'}</Link>
            <span>/</span>
            <span className="text-slate-900">{nomePaisApresentar}</span>
          </nav>

          <div className="max-w-3xl">
            <span className="block text-[10px] font-black text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest mb-3 bg-slate-100 w-max px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200">
              {isEn ? 'Destination Guide' : 'Guia de Destino'}
            </span>
            <h1 className="text-4xl md:text-5xl font-black text-slate-900 mb-4 tracking-tight capitalize">
              {isEn ? `Camps in ${nomePaisApresentar}` : `Campos em ${nomePaisApresentar}`}
            </h1>
            <p className="text-base md:text-lg text-slate-500 leading-relaxed font-medium">
              {descPaisFinal}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="bg-white border-b border-slate-200 py-4 px-4 md:px-6 sticky top-[72px] md:top-[80px] z-30 shadow-[0_8px_30px_rgb(0,0,0,0.04)]">
        <div className="max-w-[1100px] mx-auto">
          <form action={`/${lang}/pesquisa/pais/${encodeURIComponent(nomePaisInicial)}`} method="GET" className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-5 gap-3 items-center">

            <select name="categoria" defaultValue={categoriaParam} className="w-full p-3 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 outline-none cursor-pointer focus:border-slate-900 focus:bg-white transition-colors">
              <option value="">{isEn ? 'All Categories' : 'Todas as Categorias'}</option>
              <option value="Desporto">{isEn ? 'Sports' : 'Desporto'}</option>
              <option value="Aventura & Natureza">{isEn ? 'Adventure' : 'Aventura & Natureza'}</option>
              <option value="Tecnologia & Ciência">{isEn ? 'Tech' : 'Tecnologia & Ciência'}</option>
              <option value="Artes & Criatividade">{isEn ? 'Arts' : 'Artes & Criatividade'}</option>
              <option value="Línguas">{isEn ? 'Languages' : 'Línguas'}</option>
            </select>

            {mostrarDistritos && (
              <select name="distrito" defaultValue={distritoParam} className="w-full p-3 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 outline-none cursor-pointer focus:border-slate-900 focus:bg-white transition-colors">
                <option value="">{isEn ? 'All Districts' : 'Todos os Distritos'}</option>
                {distritosPT.map(d => <option key={d} value={d}>{d}</option>)}
              </select>
            )}

            <select name="idade" defaultValue={idadeParam} className="w-full p-3 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 outline-none cursor-pointer focus:border-slate-900 focus:bg-white transition-colors">
              <option value="">{isEn ? 'All Ages' : 'Todas as Idades'}</option>
              <option value="6-9 anos">6-9 {isEn ? 'years' : 'anos'}</option>
              <option value="10-13 anos">10-13 {isEn ? 'years' : 'anos'}</option>
              <option value="14-17 anos">14-17 {isEn ? 'years' : 'anos'}</option>
            </select>

            <input
              type="date"
              name="data"
              defaultValue={dataParam}
              min={new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}
              className="w-full p-3 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 outline-none cursor-pointer focus:border-slate-900 focus:bg-white transition-colors"
            />

            <button type="submit" className="w-full p-3 bg-[#e2a41d] hover:bg-[#d4991b] text-white rounded-xl text-sm font-black transition-colors shadow-sm uppercase tracking-widest">
              {isEn ? 'Update' : 'Atualizar'}
            </button>

            {(categoriaParam || distritoParam || idadeParam || dataParam) && (
              <Link href={`/${lang}/pesquisa/pais/${encodeURIComponent(nomePaisInicial)}`} className="text-xs font-bold text-red-500 hover:text-red-600 text-center sm:text-left transition-colors lg:col-span-5 uppercase tracking-widest bg-red-50 w-max px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-red-100">
                ✕ {isEn ? 'Clear filters' : 'Limpar filtros'}
              </Link>
            )}
          </form>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="max-w-[1100px] mx-auto px-4 py-12 md:py-16">
        {!resultados || resultados.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="text-center p-12 bg-white rounded-[2rem] border border-slate-200 shadow-sm max-w-2xl mx-auto flex flex-col items-center gap-4">
            <div className="w-20 h-20 bg-slate-50 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-4xl border border-slate-100 mb-2">🏕️</div>
            <h3 className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-900">{isEn ? 'No camps found' : 'Nenhum programa encontrado'}</h3>
            <p className="text-base text-slate-500 font-medium">
              {isEn ? 'Try adjusting your filters or dates.' : 'Não encontrámos resultados exatos. Tente remover o filtro da data ou pesquisar noutra categoria.'}
            </p>
            <Link href={`/${lang}/pesquisa/pais/${encodeURIComponent(nomePaisInicial)}`} className="mt-4 bg-slate-900 text-white font-bold px-6 py-3 rounded-xl hover:bg-slate-800 transition-colors shadow-lg">
              {isEn ? 'Clear all filters' : 'Ver todos os programas'}
            </Link>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-8">
              <span className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest bg-white border border-slate-200 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl shadow-sm">
                {resultados.length} {isEn ? 'experiences found' : 'experiências encontradas'}
              </span>
            </div>
            
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
              {resultados.map((campo) => {
                const nomeVisivel = isEn && campo.nome_en ? campo.nome_en : campo.nome;
                const localVisivel = isEn && campo.local_en ? campo.local_en : (campo.Distrito || campo.local);

                let precoVisivel = campo.preco || 0;
                if (!precoVisivel && campo.pacotes && campo.pacotes.length > 0) {
                  const todosPrecos = campo.pacotes.flatMap((p: any) =>
                    p.variantes ? p.variantes.map((v: any) => v.preco) : []
                  );
                  if (todosPrecos.length > 0) precoVisivel = Math.min(...todosPrecos);
                }

                return (
                  <div key={campo.id} className="group flex flex-col bg-white rounded-[2rem] overflow-hidden border border-slate-200 relative shadow-sm hover:shadow-[0_20px_60px_rgb(0,0,0,0.08)] transition-all duration-300 hover:-translate-y-1">
                    <Link href={`/${lang}/campo/${campo.id}`} className="absolute inset-0 z-10"><span className="sr-only">Explorar {nomeVisivel}</span></Link>
                    <div className="absolute top-4 right-4 z-20"><BotaoFavorito campoId={campo.id} /></div>

                    <div className="relative h-60 w-full overflow-hidden bg-slate-100">
                      <img 
                        src={campo.imagem || '/og-image.jpg'} 
                        alt={nomeVisivel} 
                        loading="lazy" 
                        className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full object-cover transition-transform duration-700 group-hover:scale-105" 
                      />
                      <div className="absolute top-4 left-4 bg-white/90 backdrop-blur-sm px-3 py-1.5 text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-900 rounded-lg z-0 shadow-sm">
                        {campo.categoria}
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex flex-col p-6 flex-1 pointer-events-none">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-3">
                        <svg className="w-3 h-3" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="3"><path d="M21 10c0 7-9 13-9 13s-9-6-9-13a9 9 0 0 1 18 0z"></path><circle cx="12" cy="10" r="3"></circle></svg>
                        {localVisivel}
                      </div>
                      <h3 className="text-xl font-black text-slate-900 leading-tight mb-2 line-clamp-2">{nomeVisivel}</h3>
                      <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 font-medium mb-6">
                        {isEn ? 'Ages:' : 'Idades:'} <span className="text-slate-900 font-bold">{campo.idade}</span>
                      </p>

                      <div className="mt-auto pt-6 flex items-center justify-between border-t border-slate-100">
                        <div>
                          <p className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400 mb-0.5">{isEn ? 'From' : 'A partir de'}</p>
                          <p className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-900 m-0">{precoVisivel > 0 ? `${precoVisivel}€` : '--'}</p>
                        </div>
                        <span className="bg-slate-100 text-slate-900 px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest transition-colors group-hover:bg-[#e2a41d] group-hover:text-white shadow-sm">
                          {isEn ? 'View Dates' : 'Ver Datas'}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </>
        )}
      </section>

      <section className="bg-white border-t border-slate-200 py-20 px-4 md:px-6">
        <div className="max-w-[1100px] mx-auto text-center md:text-left">
          <h3 className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-900 mb-8">{isEn ? `Explore more in ${nomePaisApresentar}` : `Explore mais em ${nomePaisApresentar}`}</h3>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
            <Link href={`/${lang}/pesquisa/pais/${encodeURIComponent(nomePaisInicial)}?categoria=Desporto`} className="p-6 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl hover:border-slate-900 transition-colors text-base font-black text-slate-800 no-underline flex items-center justify-between group shadow-sm hover:shadow-lg">
              <span className="flex items-center gap-3">⚽ {isEn ? `Sports Camps` : `Campos de Desporto`}</span>
              <span className="text-slate-400 group-hover:text-slate-900 transition-colors">&rarr;</span>
            </Link>
            <Link href={`/${lang}/pesquisa/pais/${encodeURIComponent(nomePaisInicial)}?categoria=Línguas`} className="p-6 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl hover:border-slate-900 transition-colors text-base font-black text-slate-800 no-underline flex items-center justify-between group shadow-sm hover:shadow-lg">
              <span className="flex items-center gap-3">🗣️ {isEn ? `Language Camps` : `Campos de Línguas`}</span>
              <span className="text-slate-400 group-hover:text-slate-900 transition-colors">&rarr;</span>
            </Link>
            <Link href={`/${lang}/pesquisa/pais/${encodeURIComponent(nomePaisInicial)}?categoria=Aventura & Natureza`} className="p-6 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl hover:border-slate-900 transition-colors text-base font-black text-slate-800 no-underline flex items-center justify-between group shadow-sm hover:shadow-lg">
              <span className="flex items-center gap-3">🏕️ {isEn ? `Adventure Camps` : `Campos de Aventura`}</span>
              <span className="text-slate-400 group-hover:text-slate-900 transition-colors">&rarr;</span>
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}

<file path="app/[lang]/(public)/pesquisa/page.tsx">
"use client";

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef, use, Suspense } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";
import BotaoFavorito from "../components/BotaoFavorito";

const normalize = (str: string) =>
  (str || "")
    .normalize("NFD")
    .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "")
    .toLowerCase()
    .trim();

interface OptionItem {
  value: string;
  label: string;
}

function CustomDropdown({
  label,
  value,
  options,
  onChange,
  placeholder,
}: {
  label: string;
  value: string;
  options: OptionItem[];
  onChange: (val: string) => void;
  placeholder: string;
}) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleClickOutside = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (ref.current && !ref.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
        setOpen(false);
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
  }, []);

  const selectedLabel =
    options.find((o) => o.value === value)?.label || placeholder;

  return (
    <div ref={ref} className="relative w-full">
      <label className="block text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400 mb-1">
        {label}
      </label>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => setOpen((prev) => !prev)}
        className={`w-full bg-[#F8FAFC] border ${
          open ? "border-[#2563EB] bg-white" : "border-slate-200"
        } rounded-md px-3.5 py-3 text-left text-sm font-bold text-[#0F172A] flex items-center justify-between transition-all cursor-pointer shadow-sm hover:border-[#2563EB]`}
      >
        <span className="truncate">{selectedLabel}</span>
        <svg
          className={`w-4 h-4 text-slate-500 transition-transform flex-shrink-0 ml-2 ${
            open ? "rotate-180 text-[#2563EB]" : ""
          }`}
          fill="none"
          viewBox="0 0 24 24"
          stroke="currentColor"
          strokeWidth="2.5"
        >
          <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M19 9l-7 7-7-7" />
        </svg>
      </button>

      {open && (
        <div className="absolute z-50 left-0 right-0 mt-1.5 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md shadow-2xl max-h-64 overflow-y-auto py-1">
          {options.map((opt) => {
            const active = opt.value === value;
            return (
              <button
                key={opt.value}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  onChange(opt.value);
                  setOpen(false);
                }}
                className={`w-full text-left px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-between cursor-pointer border-0 transition-colors ${
                  active
                    ? "bg-[#2563EB] text-white font-black"
                    : "bg-white text-[#0F172A] hover:bg-slate-100"
                }`}
              >
                <span className="truncate">{opt.label}</span>
                {active && <span>✓</span>}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function PesquisaConteudo({ lang }: { lang: string }) {
  const isEn = lang === "en";
  const router = useRouter();
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();

  const [todosCampos, setTodosCampos] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const [q, setQ] = useState(searchParams.get("q") || "");
  const [distrito, setDistrito] = useState(searchParams.get("distrito") || "");
  const [categoria, setCategoria] = useState(searchParams.get("categoria") || "");
  const [idade, setIdade] = useState(searchParams.get("idade") || "");
  const [dataInicio, setDataInicio] = useState(searchParams.get("data") || "");
  const [ordem, setOrdem] = useState(searchParams.get("ordem") || "relevancia");

  useEffect(() => {
    setQ(searchParams.get("q") || "");
    setDistrito(searchParams.get("distrito") || "");
    setCategoria(searchParams.get("categoria") || "");
    setIdade(searchParams.get("idade") || "");
    setDataInicio(searchParams.get("data") || "");
    setOrdem(searchParams.get("ordem") || "relevancia");
  }, [searchParams]);

  useEffect(() => {
    async function carregarCampos() {
      setLoading(true);
      const { data } = await supabase
        .from("campos")
        .select("*")
        .eq("ativo", true)
        .order("created_at", { ascending: false });

      setTodosCampos(data || []);
      setLoading(false);
    }
    carregarCampos();
  }, []);

  const distritosOpcoes: OptionItem[] = [
    { value: "", label: isEn ? "All Districts" : "Todos os Distritos" },
    ...[
      "Aveiro",
      "Beja",
      "Braga",
      "Bragança",
      "Castelo Branco",
      "Coimbra",
      "Évora",
      "Faro",
      "Guarda",
      "Leiria",
      "Lisboa",
      "Portalegre",
      "Porto",
      "Santarém",
      "Setúbal",
      "Viana do Castelo",
      "Vila Real",
      "Viseu",
    ].map((d) => ({ value: d, label: d })),
  ];

  const categoriasOpcoes: OptionItem[] = [
    { value: "", label: isEn ? "All Categories" : "Todas as Categorias" },
    { value: "Desporto", label: isEn ? "Sports & Football" : "Desporto & Futebol" },
    { value: "Surf", label: "Surf & Náutica" },
    { value: "Aventura & Natureza", label: isEn ? "Adventure & Nature" : "Aventura & Natureza" },
    { value: "Tecnologia & Ciência", label: isEn ? "Tech & Science" : "Tecnologia & Ciência" },
    { value: "Artes & Criatividade", label: isEn ? "Arts & Creativity" : "Artes & Criatividade" },
    { value: "Línguas", label: isEn ? "Languages" : "Línguas" },
  ];

  const idadesOpcoes: OptionItem[] = [
    { value: "", label: isEn ? "All Ages" : "Todas as Idades" },
    { value: "3-6", label: isEn ? "3 to 6 years" : "3 a 6 anos" },
    { value: "7-12", label: isEn ? "7 to 12 years" : "7 a 12 anos" },
    { value: "13-18", label: isEn ? "13 to 18 years" : "13 a 18 anos" },
  ];

  const ordemOpcoes: OptionItem[] = [
    { value: "relevancia", label: isEn ? "Featured" : "Relevância" },
    { value: "preco_asc", label: isEn ? "Price: Low to High" : "Preço: Mais Baixo" },
    { value: "preco_desc", label: isEn ? "Price: High to Low" : "Preço: Mais Alto" },
  ];

  const obterPrecoMinimo = (campo: any) => {
    let p = Number(campo.preco) || 0;
    let pacotesArray = [];
    if (typeof campo.pacotes === "string") {
      try {
        pacotesArray = JSON.parse(campo.pacotes);
      } catch {
        pacotesArray = [];
      }
    } else if (Array.isArray(campo.pacotes)) {
      pacotesArray = campo.pacotes;
    }
    if (!p && pacotesArray.length > 0) {
      const precos = pacotesArray
        .flatMap((pac: any) =>
          Array.isArray(pac.variantes)
            ? pac.variantes.map((v: any) => Number(v.preco) || 0)
            : []
        )
        .filter((val: number) => val > 0);
      if (precos.length > 0) p = Math.min(...precos);
    }
    return p;
  };

  const resultados = todosCampos
    .filter((c: any) => {
      if (q) {
        const qNorm = normalize(q);
        const texto = normalize(
          `${c.nome || ""} ${c.nome_en || ""} ${c.categoria || ""} ${
            c.Distrito || ""
          } ${c.local || ""} ${c.descricao || ""}`
        );
        if (!texto.includes(qNorm)) return false;
      }

      if (distrito) {
        const locCampo = normalize(`${c.Distrito || ""} ${c.local || ""}`);
        if (!locCampo.includes(normalize(distrito))) return false;
      }

      if (categoria) {
        const catNorm = normalize(categoria);
        const catCampo = normalize(
          `${c.categoria || ""} ${c.categoria_en || ""}`
        );
        if (
          !catCampo.includes(catNorm) &&
          !(catNorm.includes("desporto") && catCampo.includes("futebol")) &&
          !(catNorm.includes("linguas") && catCampo.includes("ingles"))
        ) {
          return false;
        }
      }

      if (idade) {
        const matchRange = idade.match(/(\d+)\D+(\d+)/);
        const matchCampo = (c.idade || "").match(/(\d+)\D+(\d+)/);
        if (matchRange && matchCampo) {
          const minFiltro = parseInt(matchRange[1], 10);
          const maxFiltro = parseInt(matchRange[2], 10);
          const minCampo = parseInt(matchCampo[1], 10);
          const maxCampo = parseInt(matchCampo[2], 10);
          if (maxCampo < minFiltro || minCampo > maxFiltro) return false;
        }
      }

      if (dataInicio) {
        const dataText = JSON.stringify(c.pacotes || "") + (c.datas || "");
        if (dataText.length > 5 && !dataText.includes(dataInicio.slice(0, 7))) {
          return false;
        }
      }

      return true;
    })
    .sort((a, b) => {
      if (ordem === "preco_asc") return obterPrecoMinimo(a) - obterPrecoMinimo(b);
      if (ordem === "preco_desc") return obterPrecoMinimo(b) - obterPrecoMinimo(a);
      return 0;
    });

  const limparFiltros = () => {
    setQ("");
    setDistrito("");
    setCategoria("");
    setIdade("");
    setDataInicio("");
    setOrdem("relevancia");
    router.push(`/${lang}/pesquisa`);
  };

  const temFiltros = q || distrito || categoria || idade || dataInicio;

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-[#F8FAFC] font-sans text-[#0F172A] pb-24">
      {/* HEADER DA PESQUISA */}
      <section className="bg-[#2563EB] text-white pt-14 pb-20 px-4 md:px-8 border-b-8 border-[#F59E0B] relative overflow-hidden">
        <div className="absolute -top-20 -right-20 w-80 h-80 bg-[#F59E0B] rounded-full opacity-90 pointer-events-none hidden lg:block" />
        <div className="max-w-[1280px] mx-auto flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-end justify-between gap-6 relative z-10">
          <div>
            <span className="inline-block bg-[#F59E0B] text-white text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-md mb-3">
              {isEn ? "Directory & Booking" : "Diretório Oficial HelloCamp"}
            </span>
            <h1 className="text-3xl md:text-5xl font-black tracking-tight text-white m-0">
              {isEn ? "Explore Holiday Camps" : "Explorar Campos de Férias"}
            </h1>
            <p className="text-blue-100 text-sm md:text-base font-medium mt-2 mb-0">
              {isEn
                ? "Compare verified programs, dates, and prices in one place."
                : "Compare programas verificados, datas e preços num só lugar."}
            </p>
          </div>

          <Link
            href={`/${lang}/mapa`}
            className="bg-[#F59E0B] hover:bg-amber-600 text-white px-6 py-4 rounded-md text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest no-underline transition-all shadow-lg flex items-center justify-center gap-2 w-fit"
          >
            <span>🗺️</span>{" "}
            {isEn ? "Open Interactive Map" : "Ver no Mapa Interativo"}
          </Link>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* BARRA DE FILTROS COM DROPDOWNS 100% CUSTOMIZADOS */}
      <section className="max-w-[1280px] mx-auto px-4 md:px-8 -mt-10 relative z-30 mb-10">
        <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-md shadow-xl border border-slate-200 space-y-4">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-12 gap-4">
            <div className="md:col-span-4">
              <label className="block text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400 mb-1">
                {isEn ? "Keyword" : "Pesquisa Livre"}
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={q}
                onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)}
                placeholder={
                  isEn
                    ? "Surf, Football, Robotics, Lisbon..."
                    : "Surf, Futebol, Robótica, Lisboa..."
                }
                className="w-full bg-[#F8FAFC] border border-slate-200 rounded-md px-4 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-[#0F172A] outline-none focus:border-[#2563EB] focus:bg-white shadow-sm"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="md:col-span-3">
              <CustomDropdown
                label={isEn ? "District" : "Distrito"}
                value={distrito}
                options={distritosOpcoes}
                onChange={setDistrito}
                placeholder={isEn ? "All Districts" : "Todos os Distritos"}
              />
            </div>

            <div className="md:col-span-3">
              <CustomDropdown
                label={isEn ? "Category" : "Categoria"}
                value={categoria}
                options={categoriasOpcoes}
                onChange={setCategoria}
                placeholder={isEn ? "All Categories" : "Todas as Categorias"}
              />
            </div>

            <div className="md:col-span-2">
              <CustomDropdown
                label={isEn ? "Age Group" : "Faixa Etária"}
                value={idade}
                options={idadesOpcoes}
                onChange={setIdade}
                placeholder={isEn ? "All Ages" : "Todas as Idades"}
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4 pt-4 border-t border-slate-100">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <span className="bg-[#2563EB] text-white text-xs font-black px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-md">
                {resultados.length}{" "}
                {isEn ? "programs found" : "programas encontrados"}
              </span>
              {temFiltros && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={limparFiltros}
                  className="bg-[#F43F5E] hover:bg-rose-600 text-white text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest px-3.5 py-2 rounded-md border-0 cursor-pointer"
                >
                  ✕ {isEn ? "Clear Filters" : "Limpar Filtros"}
                </button>
              )}
            </div>

            <div className="w-60">
              <CustomDropdown
                label={isEn ? "Sort by" : "Ordenar por"}
                value={ordem}
                options={ordemOpcoes}
                onChange={setOrdem}
                placeholder={isEn ? "Featured" : "Relevância"}
              />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* GRELHA DE RESULTADOS */}
      <section className="max-w-[1280px] mx-auto px-4 md:px-8">
        {loading ? (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
            {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6].map((n) => (
              <div
                key={n}
                className="bg-white rounded-md border border-slate-200 h-96 animate-pulse"
              />
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : resultados.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="bg-white rounded-md border border-slate-200 p-12 text-center shadow-sm max-w-xl mx-auto">
            <span className="text-5xl block mb-4">🏕️</span>
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-black text-[#0F172A] mb-2">
              {isEn
                ? "No camps match your exact filters"
                : "Nenhum campo encontrado com estes filtros"}
            </h2>
            <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm font-medium mb-6">
              {isEn
                ? "Try clearing some filters or exploring all active programs."
                : "Experimente limpar os filtros selecionados para ver todos os programas ativos."}
            </p>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={limparFiltros}
              className="bg-[#2563EB] hover:bg-blue-700 text-white font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-xs px-8 py-4 rounded-md border-0 cursor-pointer shadow-sm"
            >
              {isEn ? "Show All Camps" : "Ver Todos os Campos"}
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
            {resultados.map((campo: any) => {
              const nome = isEn && campo.nome_en ? campo.nome_en : campo.nome;
              const cat =
                isEn && campo.categoria_en
                  ? campo.categoria_en
                  : campo.categoria;
              const local =
                isEn && campo.local_en
                  ? campo.local_en
                  : campo.Distrito || campo.local || "Portugal";
              const preco = obterPrecoMinimo(campo);
              const imgUrl = campo.imagem?.startsWith("http")
                ? campo.imagem
                : "/og-image.jpg";

              return (
                <div
                  key={campo.id}
                  className="group flex flex-col bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md shadow-sm hover:shadow-xl hover:border-[#2563EB] transition-all duration-300 hover:-translate-y-1 overflow-hidden relative"
                >
                  <Link
                    href={`/${lang}/campo/${campo.id}`}
                    className="absolute inset-0 z-10"
                  >
                    <span className="sr-only">Ver {nome}</span>
                  </Link>

                  <div className="absolute top-4 right-4 z-20">
                    <BotaoFavorito campoId={campo.id} />
                  </div>

                  <div className="relative h-60 w-full bg-slate-100 overflow-hidden">
                    <img
                      src={imgUrl}
                      alt={nome}
                      loading="lazy"
                      className="w-full h-full object-cover transition-transform duration-700 group-hover:scale-105"
                    />
                    <div className="absolute top-4 left-4 bg-[#2563EB] text-white px-3.5 py-1.5 text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest rounded-md shadow-sm">
                      {cat}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex flex-col p-6 flex-1 pointer-events-none">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-2">
                      <span>📍 {local}</span>
                      {campo.idade && <span>• 👶 {campo.idade}</span>}
                    </div>

                    <h3 className="text-xl font-black text-[#0F172A] leading-tight mb-4 line-clamp-2">
                      {nome}
                    </h3>

                    <div className="mt-auto pt-4 flex items-center justify-between border-t border-slate-100">
                      <div>
                        <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400 block">
                          {isEn ? "From" : "Desde"}
                        </span>
                        <span className="text-2xl font-black text-[#2563EB]">
                          {preco > 0 ? `${preco}€` : "Sob Consulta"}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <span className="bg-[#0F172A] group-hover:bg-[#2563EB] text-white px-5 py-2.5 rounded-md text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest transition-colors shadow-sm">
                        {isEn ? "View Dates" : "Ver Detalhes"}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}

export default function PesquisaPage({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ lang: string }>;
}) {
  const { lang } = use(params);
  return (
    <Suspense
      fallback={
        <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#F8FAFC] flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400">
          A carregar campos...
        </div>
      }
    >
      <PesquisaConteudo lang={lang} />
    </Suspense>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="app/[lang]/(public)/checkout/[id]/page.tsx">
"use client";

import React, { useEffect, useState, use } from "react";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import Link from "next/link";

export default function CheckoutPage({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ lang: string; id: string }>;
}) {
  const { lang, id } = use(params);
  const router = useRouter();
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const isEn = lang === "en";

  const [campo, setCampo] = useState<any>(null);
  const [organizador, setOrganizador] = useState<any>(null);
  const [criancas, setCriancas] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [reservasExistentes, setReservasExistentes] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [user, setUser] = useState<any>(null);
  const [isGuest, setIsGuest] = useState(true);
  const [processingState, setProcessingState] = useState(false);

  // Estados do Formulário de Encarregado e Participantes
  const [guestData, setGuestData] = useState({
    nome: "",
    email: "",
    telefone: "",
    nif: "",
    contacto_emergencia: "",
  });
  const [selecoesCriancas, setSelecoesCriancas] = useState<string[]>([""]);
  const [respostasCustomizadas, setRespostasCustomizadas] = useState<
    Record<number, Record<string, string>>
  >({});
  const [concordaTermos, setConcordaTermos] = useState(false);

  // Dropdowns Customizados (Zero <select> nativos)
  const [openDropdownKey, setOpenDropdownKey] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Modal de Adicionar Novo Participante
  const [showModal, setShowModal] = useState(false);
  const [savingChild, setSavingChild] = useState(false);
  const [indexToAssign, setIndexToAssign] = useState(0);
  const [newChild, setNewChild] = useState({
    nome: "",
    nif: "",
    data_nascimento: "",
    sexo: "Masculino",
    restricoes_alimentares: "",
    doencas_cronicas: "",
  });

  // Parâmetros vindos da CaixaReserva via URL
  const quantidade = Math.max(
    1,
    parseInt(searchParams.get("quantidade_criancas") || "1", 10)
  );
  const extSeguro = searchParams.get("ext_seguro") === "true";
  const extTransporte = searchParams.get("ext_transporte") === "true";
  const descontoUrlValor = Number(searchParams.get("desconto_valor")) || 0;
  const descontoUrlNome = searchParams.get("desconto_nome") || "";

  let turnoSelecionado: any = null;
  try {
    const rawTurno = searchParams.get("turno");
    if (rawTurno) turnoSelecionado = JSON.parse(rawTurno);
  } catch {}

  let alojamentoSelecionadoUrl: any = null;
  try {
    const rawAloj = searchParams.get("alojamento");
    if (rawAloj) alojamentoSelecionadoUrl = JSON.parse(rawAloj);
  } catch {}

  let opcoesParticipantesUrl: Record<number, Record<string, string>> = {};
  try {
    const rawOpcoes = searchParams.get("opcoes_participantes");
    if (rawOpcoes) opcoesParticipantesUrl = JSON.parse(rawOpcoes);
  } catch {}

  useEffect(() => {
    setSelecoesCriancas(Array(quantidade).fill(""));
  }, [quantidade]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const carregarCheckout = async () => {
      const { data: campoData } = await supabase
        .from("campos")
        .select("*")
        .eq("id", id)
        .single();

      if (!campoData) {
        router.push(`/${lang}/pesquisa`);
        return;
      }
      setCampo(campoData);

      if (campoData.organizador_id) {
        const { data: orgData } = await supabase
          .from("perfis")
          .select("id, email, empresa_nome, stripe_account_id")
          .eq("id", campoData.organizador_id)
          .maybeSingle();
        setOrganizador(orgData);
      }

      const {
        data: { session },
      } = await supabase.auth.getSession();

      if (session?.user) {
        setUser(session.user);
        setIsGuest(false);

        const [{ data: perfilPai }, { data: criancasDb }, { data: resDb }] =
          await Promise.all([
            supabase
              .from("perfis")
              .select("*")
              .eq("id", session.user.id)
              .maybeSingle(),
            supabase
              .from("criancas")
              .select("*")
              .eq("cliente_id", session.user.id)
              .order("created_at", { ascending: false }),
            supabase
              .from("reservas")
              .select("crianca_id, turno_nome, status_pagamento")
              .eq("campo_id", id)
              .in("status_pagamento", ["Pago", "Sinal Pago"]),
          ]);

        if (perfilPai) {
          setGuestData({
            nome: perfilPai.nome_completo || "",
            email: perfilPai.email || session.user.email || "",
            telefone: perfilPai.telefone || "",
            nif: perfilPai.nif || "",
            contacto_emergencia: perfilPai.contacto_emergencia || "",
          });
        }
        const listaCriancas = criancasDb || [];
        setCriancas(listaCriancas);
        setReservasExistentes(resDb || []);

        if (listaCriancas.length > 0 && quantidade === 1) {
          setSelecoesCriancas([listaCriancas[0].id]);
        }
      } else {
        setIsGuest(true);
      }

      setLoading(false);
    };

    carregarCheckout();
  }, [id, lang, router, quantidade]);

  const calcularIdadeNum = (dataNasc: string) => {
    if (!dataNasc) return 10;
    const hoje = new Date();
    const nasc = new Date(dataNasc);
    let idade = hoje.getFullYear() - nasc.getFullYear();
    const m = hoje.getMonth() - nasc.getMonth();
    if (m < 0 || (m === 0 && hoje.getDate() < nasc.getDate())) idade--;
    return idade;
  };

  const openNewChildModal = (index: number) => {
    setNewChild({
      nome: "",
      nif: "",
      data_nascimento: "",
      sexo: "Masculino",
      restricoes_alimentares: "",
      doencas_cronicas: "",
    });
    setIndexToAssign(index);
    setShowModal(true);
  };

  const handleSaveNewChild = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setSavingChild(true);
    try {
      if (isGuest) {
        const guestChild = {
          ...newChild,
          id: `guest_${Date.now()}`,
          isGuestChild: true,
        };
        setCriancas((prev) => [...prev, guestChild]);
        setSelecoesCriancas((prev) => {
          const novas = [...prev];
          novas[indexToAssign] = guestChild.id;
          return novas;
        });
      } else {
        const { data: created, error } = await supabase
          .from("criancas")
          .insert([{ ...newChild, cliente_id: user.id }])
          .select()
          .single();
        if (error) throw error;
        setCriancas((prev) => [...prev, created]);
        setSelecoesCriancas((prev) => {
          const novas = [...prev];
          novas[indexToAssign] = created.id;
          return novas;
        });
      }
      setShowModal(false);
    } catch (err: any) {
      alert(err.message);
    } finally {
      setSavingChild(false);
    }
  };

  const handleUpdateLocalCrianca = (
    idDaCrianca: string,
    campoTabela: string,
    valor: string
  ) => {
    setCriancas((prev) =>
      prev.map((c) =>
        c.id === idDaCrianca ? { ...c, [campoTabela]: valor } : c
      )
    );
  };

  const handleSaveDBCrianca = async (
    idDaCrianca: string,
    campoTabela: string,
    valor: string
  ) => {
    if (isGuest || idDaCrianca.startsWith("guest_")) return;
    await supabase
      .from("criancas")
      .update({ [campoTabela]: valor })
      .eq("id", idDaCrianca);
  };

  if (loading || !campo) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 flex flex-col items-center justify-center font-bold text-slate-400 text-xs uppercase tracking-widest animate-pulse">
        <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full border-4 border-slate-200 border-t-[#0E3A68] animate-spin mb-4" />
        {isEn
          ? "Preparing secure checkout..."
          : "A preparar a sua reserva segura..."}
      </div>
    );
  }

  const isExterna = campo.modalidade_reserva === "link_externo";
  const perguntasDeTexto = (campo?.perguntas_customizadas || []).filter(
    (p: string) => p.startsWith("TEXTO|") || !p.startsWith("ESCOLHA|")
  );

  // =========================================================================
  // CÁLCULOS FINANCEIROS COMPLETOS (PROGRAMA + ALOJAMENTO + TAXAS - DESCONTO)
  // =========================================================================
  const multiplicador = Number(turnoSelecionado?.multiplicador) || 1;
  const totalDiasExtras = Number(turnoSelecionado?.quantidade) || 1;
  const precoBaseUnitario =
    (Number(turnoSelecionado?.preco) || Number(campo.preco) || 0) *
    multiplicador;

  const custoAlojamentoUnit = Number(alojamentoSelecionadoUrl?.custoUnit) || 0;

  const custoSeguroUnit = extSeguro
    ? campo.tipo_extra_seguro === "diario"
      ? Number(campo.extra_seguro || 0) * totalDiasExtras
      : Number(campo.extra_seguro || 0)
    : 0;

  const custoTransporteUnit = extTransporte
    ? campo.tipo_extra_transporte === "diario"
      ? Number(campo.extra_transporte || 0) * totalDiasExtras
      : Number(campo.extra_transporte || 0)
    : 0;

  const taxaMatriculaUnit = Number(campo.taxa_matricula) || 0;
  const taxaMaterialUnit = Number(campo.taxa_material) || 0;

  const precoFinalTotal = Math.max(
    0,
    (precoBaseUnitario +
      custoAlojamentoUnit +
      custoSeguroUnit +
      custoTransporteUnit +
      taxaMatriculaUnit +
      taxaMaterialUnit) *
      quantidade -
      descontoUrlValor
  );

  const isPagamentoFracionado = campo.tipo_pagamento === "50_sinal";
  const valorACobrarAgora = isPagamentoFracionado
    ? precoFinalTotal / 2
    : precoFinalTotal;
  const valorPendente = isPagamentoFracionado
    ? precoFinalTotal - valorACobrarAgora
    : 0;

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!concordaTermos) {
      alert(
        isEn
          ? "You must accept the Terms and Conditions to proceed."
          : "Tem de aceitar os Termos e Condições para avançar."
      );
      return;
    }

    if (!isExterna && selecoesCriancas.some((c) => c === "")) {
      alert(
        isEn
          ? "Please select or create a participant for each spot."
          : "Por favor, selecione ou adicione um participante para cada vaga."
      );
      return;
    }

    if (
      (isGuest || isExterna) &&
      (!guestData.nome.trim() ||
        !guestData.email.trim() ||
        !guestData.telefone.trim())
    ) {
      alert(
        isEn
          ? "Please fill in your contact details."
          : "Por favor, preencha os seus dados de contacto."
      );
      return;
    }

    setProcessingState(true);

    try {
      if (isExterna) {
        await supabase.from("leads_externas").insert([
          {
            campo_id: campo.id,
            organizador_id: campo.organizador_id,
            nome_cliente: guestData.nome,
            email_cliente: guestData.email,
            telefone_cliente: guestData.telefone,
            turno_interesse: turnoSelecionado?.nome || "Geral",
            preco_estimado: precoFinalTotal,
          },
        ]);

        await fetch("/api/notificar-reserva", {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            lead: {
              nome: guestData.nome,
              email: guestData.email,
              telefone: guestData.telefone,
            },
            campoNome: campo.nome,
            parceiroEmail: organizador?.email || "info@hellocamp.pt",
            lang,
          }),
        }).catch(() => {});

        window.location.href = campo.link_externo_reserva;
      } else {
        const criancasParaEnviar = selecoesCriancas.map((cId, index) => {
          const c = criancas.find((cr) => cr.id === cId);
          return {
            ...c,
            escolhas_multiplas_url: opcoesParticipantesUrl[index + 1] || {},
          };
        });

        const payloadCheckout = {
          totalAmount: precoFinalTotal,
          userEmail: isGuest ? guestData.email : user.email,
          lang: lang,
          campoNome: campo.nome,
          stripeAccountId: organizador?.stripe_account_id,
          tipoPagamento: campo?.tipo_pagamento,
          campoId: campo.id,
          isGuest: isGuest,
          guestData: isGuest ? guestData : null,
          criancas: criancasParaEnviar,
          reservaSpecs: {
            turno_nome: turnoSelecionado?.nome || "Programa Base",
            extras_escolhidos: {
              ext_seguro: extSeguro,
              ext_transporte: extTransporte,
              extAlojamento: alojamentoSelecionadoUrl?.nome || null,
              dias_inscritos: totalDiasExtras,
              dias_soltos: turnoSelecionado?.dias_soltos || [],
              data_inicio: turnoSelecionado?.data_inicio || null,
            },
            respostas_customizadas: respostasCustomizadas,
            opcoes_participantes_url: opcoesParticipantesUrl,
            quantidade_reservas: quantidade,
            valor_por_reserva: precoFinalTotal / quantidade,
          },
        };

        const res = await fetch("/api/stripe-checkout", {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify(payloadCheckout),
        });

        const data = await res.json();
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data.error || "Erro ao iniciar checkout.");
        if (data.url) window.location.href = data.url;
      }
    } catch (error: any) {
      alert(error.message);
      setProcessingState(false);
    }
  };

  const labelClass =
    "block text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-500 mb-2 ml-1";
  const inputClass =
    "w-full p-4 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 outline-none focus:border-[#0E3A68] focus:bg-white transition-colors";

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 py-10 font-sans">
      {processingState && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-white/95 z-[100] flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
          <div className="w-16 h-16 border-4 border-slate-100 border-t-[#0E3A68] rounded-full animate-spin mb-6" />
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-900 mb-2">
            {isEn ? "Securing your spot..." : "A processar a sua inscrição..."}
          </h2>
          <p className="text-slate-500 font-medium text-sm">
            {isEn
              ? "Please wait while we redirect you to secure payment."
              : "Por favor, aguarde o redirecionamento para o pagamento seguro Stripe."}
          </p>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* MODAL ADICIONAR NOVO PARTICIPANTE */}
      {showModal && !isExterna && (
        <div
          className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/70 backdrop-blur-sm z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4"
          onClick={() => setShowModal(false)}
        >
          <div
            className="bg-white w-full max-w-lg rounded-[2rem] p-8 shadow-2xl relative"
            onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
          >
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setShowModal(false)}
              className="absolute top-5 right-5 w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-slate-100 text-slate-500 font-black cursor-pointer"
            >
              &times;
            </button>
            <h3 className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-900 mb-6">
              {isEn ? "Add Participant" : "Novo Participante"}
            </h3>

            <form onSubmit={handleSaveNewChild} className="space-y-4">
              <div>
                <label className={labelClass}>
                  {isEn ? "Full Name *" : "Nome Completo *"}
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required
                  value={newChild.nome}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setNewChild({ ...newChild, nome: e.target.value })
                  }
                  className={inputClass}
                  placeholder="Ex: Martim Sousa"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div>
                  <label className={labelClass}>
                    {isEn ? "Date of Birth *" : "Data Nasc. *"}
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="date"
                    required
                    value={newChild.data_nascimento}
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      setNewChild({
                        ...newChild,
                        data_nascimento: e.target.value,
                      })
                    }
                    className={inputClass}
                  />
                </div>
                <div className="relative">
                  <label className={labelClass}>
                    {isEn ? "Gender *" : "Sexo *"}
                  </label>
                  <div
                    onClick={() =>
                      setOpenDropdownKey(
                        openDropdownKey === "modal_sexo" ? null : "modal_sexo"
                      )
                    }
                    className={`${inputClass} cursor-pointer flex justify-between items-center`}
                  >
                    <span>{newChild.sexo}</span>
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">▼</span>
                  </div>
                  {openDropdownKey === "modal_sexo" && (
                    <div className="absolute top-[calc(100%+4px)] left-0 w-full bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl shadow-xl z-50 overflow-hidden">
                      {["Masculino", "Feminino", "Prefiro não dizer"].map(
                        (s) => (
                          <div
                            key={s}
                            onClick={() => {
                              setNewChild({ ...newChild, sexo: s });
                              setOpenDropdownKey(null);
                            }}
                            className="p-3 text-xs font-bold hover:bg-blue-50 cursor-pointer"
                          >
                            {s}
                          </div>
                        )
                      )}
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className={labelClass}>
                  {isEn
                    ? "Allergies / Medical Restrictions"
                    : "Alergias / Restrições Médicas"}
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={newChild.restricoes_alimentares}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setNewChild({
                      ...newChild,
                      restricoes_alimentares: e.target.value,
                    })
                  }
                  className={inputClass}
                  placeholder={isEn ? "None" : "Nenhuma (Opcional)"}
                />
              </div>

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={savingChild}
                className="w-full py-4 bg-[#0E3A68] text-white font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-xs rounded-xl shadow-lg cursor-pointer"
              >
                {savingChild
                  ? "..."
                  : isEn
                  ? "Save Participant"
                  : "Guardar Participante"}
              </button>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="max-w-[1200px] mx-auto px-4 md:px-6 flex flex-col lg:flex-row gap-8 items-start">
        {/* COLUNA ESQUERDA */}
        <div className="flex-[1_1_60%] w-full space-y-8">
          {!isExterna && isGuest && (
            <div className="bg-amber-50 p-6 rounded-[2rem] border border-amber-200 flex justify-between items-center flex-wrap gap-4">
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-base font-black text-amber-900 m-0 mb-1">
                  {isEn ? "Checking out as Guest" : "A reservar como Convidado"}
                </h3>
                <p className="text-xs font-bold text-amber-700 m-0">
                  {isEn
                    ? "Already have a HelloCamp account?"
                    : "Já tem conta na HelloCamp?"}
                </p>
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  sessionStorage.setItem(
                    "redirect_after_login",
                    window.location.href
                  );
                  router.push(`/${lang}/login`);
                }}
                className="px-6 py-3 bg-amber-600 text-white font-black uppercase tracking-widest rounded-xl text-[10px] cursor-pointer"
              >
                {isEn ? "Login to speed up" : "Fazer Login"}
              </button>
            </div>
          )}

          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-8">
            {(isGuest || isExterna) && (
              <section className="bg-white p-6 md:p-10 rounded-[2rem] border border-slate-200 shadow-sm">
                <h2 className="text-xl font-black text-slate-900 m-0 mb-6 pb-4 border-b border-slate-100">
                  {isEn
                    ? "Parent / Guardian Contact Details"
                    : "Dados do Encarregado de Educação"}
                </h2>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-5">
                  <div className="sm:col-span-2">
                    <label className={labelClass}>
                      {isEn ? "Full Name *" : "Nome Completo *"}
                    </label>
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      required
                      value={guestData.nome}
                      onChange={(e) =>
                        setGuestData({ ...guestData, nome: e.target.value })
                      }
                      className={inputClass}
                    />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <label className={labelClass}>E-mail *</label>
                    <input
                      type="email"
                      required
                      value={guestData.email}
                      onChange={(e) =>
                        setGuestData({ ...guestData, email: e.target.value })
                      }
                      className={inputClass}
                    />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <label className={labelClass}>
                      {isEn ? "Phone *" : "Telemóvel *"}
                    </label>
                    <input
                      type="tel"
                      required
                      value={guestData.telefone}
                      onChange={(e) =>
                        setGuestData({ ...guestData, telefone: e.target.value })
                      }
                      className={inputClass}
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>
              </section>
            )}

            {!isExterna && (
              <section className="bg-white p-6 md:p-10 rounded-[2rem] border border-slate-200 shadow-sm">
                <h2 className="text-xl font-black text-slate-900 m-0 mb-6 pb-4 border-b border-slate-100">
                  {isEn ? "Participants" : "Participantes"}
                </h2>

                {Array.from({ length: quantidade }).map((_, i) => {
                  const childId = selecoesCriancas[i];
                  const childInfo = criancas.find((c) => c.id === childId);
                  const participantNumber = i + 1;
                  const escolhasUrlLocais =
                    opcoesParticipantesUrl[participantNumber];
                  const ddKey = `child_select_${i}`;

                  return (
                    <div
                      key={i}
                      className={
                        i !== quantidade - 1
                          ? "mb-10 pb-10 border-b border-slate-100"
                          : ""
                      }
                    >
                      <label className="block text-xs font-black text-[#0E3A68] mb-3 uppercase tracking-widest">
                        {isEn
                          ? `Participant ${participantNumber}`
                          : `Participante ${participantNumber}`}
                      </label>

                      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3 items-stretch sm:items-center">
                        <div className="relative flex-1">
                          <div
                            onClick={() =>
                              setOpenDropdownKey(
                                openDropdownKey === ddKey ? null : ddKey
                              )
                            }
                            className={`${inputClass} cursor-pointer flex justify-between items-center`}
                          >
                            <span>
                              {childInfo
                                ? `👦 ${childInfo.nome}`
                                : isEn
                                ? "Select a participant..."
                                : "Escolher participante..."}
                            </span>
                            <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">▼</span>
                          </div>
                          {openDropdownKey === ddKey && (
                            <div className="absolute top-[calc(100%+6px)] left-0 w-full bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl shadow-xl z-50 overflow-hidden">
                              {criancas.length === 0 ? (
                                <div className="p-3.5 text-xs text-slate-400 font-bold">
                                  Clique em &quot;+ Adicionar Novo&quot; ao lado.
                                </div>
                              ) : (
                                criancas.map((c) => {
                                  const idade = calcularIdadeNum(
                                    c.data_nascimento
                                  );
                                  const jaSelec =
                                    selecoesCriancas.includes(c.id) &&
                                    selecoesCriancas[i] !== c.id;
                                  return (
                                    <div
                                      key={c.id}
                                      onClick={() => {
                                        if (jaSelec) return;
                                        const novas = [...selecoesCriancas];
                                        novas[i] = c.id;
                                        setSelecoesCriancas(novas);
                                        setOpenDropdownKey(null);
                                      }}
                                      className={`p-3.5 text-xs font-bold border-b border-slate-50 last:border-0 ${
                                        jaSelec
                                          ? "opacity-40 cursor-not-allowed"
                                          : "hover:bg-blue-50 cursor-pointer text-slate-800"
                                      }`}
                                    >
                                      👦 {c.nome} ({idade} anos)
                                    </div>
                                  );
                                })
                              )}
                            </div>
                          )}
                        </div>

                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => openNewChildModal(i)}
                          className="px-6 py-4 bg-blue-50 hover:bg-blue-100 text-[#0E3A68] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-xs rounded-xl border border-blue-200 cursor-pointer"
                        >
                          + {isEn ? "New Child" : "Adicionar Novo"}
                        </button>
                      </div>

                      {childInfo && (
                        <div className="mt-5 bg-slate-50 p-6 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                          <div>
                            <label className={labelClass}>
                              {isEn ? "Allergies" : "Alergias Alimentares"}
                            </label>
                            <input
                              type="text"
                              value={childInfo.restricoes_alimentares || ""}
                              onChange={(e) =>
                                handleUpdateLocalCrianca(
                                  childId,
                                  "restricoes_alimentares",
                                  e.target.value
                                )
                              }
                              onBlur={(e) =>
                                handleSaveDBCrianca(
                                  childId,
                                  "restricoes_alimentares",
                                  e.target.value
                                )
                              }
                              placeholder="Nenhuma"
                              className="w-full p-3 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold"
                            />
                          </div>
                          <div>
                            <label className={labelClass}>
                              {isEn
                                ? "Medical Conditions / Medication"
                                : "Condições Médicas / Medicação"}
                            </label>
                            <input
                              type="text"
                              value={childInfo.doencas_cronicas || ""}
                              onChange={(e) =>
                                handleUpdateLocalCrianca(
                                  childId,
                                  "doencas_cronicas",
                                  e.target.value
                                )
                              }
                              onBlur={(e) =>
                                handleSaveDBCrianca(
                                  childId,
                                  "doencas_cronicas",
                                  e.target.value
                                )
                              }
                              placeholder="Nenhuma"
                              className="w-full p-3 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold"
                            />
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      )}

                      {childInfo &&
                        escolhasUrlLocais &&
                        Object.keys(escolhasUrlLocais).length > 0 && (
                          <div className="mt-4 bg-emerald-50 p-4 rounded-2xl border border-emerald-200 grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                            {Object.entries(escolhasUrlLocais).map(
                              ([perg, resp]: any) => (
                                <div
                                  key={perg}
                                  className="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl border border-emerald-100"
                                >
                                  <span className="block text-[9px] font-black uppercase text-slate-400">
                                    {perg}
                                  </span>
                                  <span className="text-xs font-black text-emerald-800">
                                    {resp}
                                  </span>
                                </div>
                              )
                            )}
                          </div>
                        )}

                      {childInfo && perguntasDeTexto.length > 0 && (
                        <div className="mt-4 bg-blue-50 p-5 rounded-2xl border border-blue-200 space-y-4">
                          {perguntasDeTexto.map(
                            (pOriginal: string, pIdx: number) => {
                              const pTituloPt = pOriginal.startsWith("TEXTO|")
                                ? pOriginal.split("|")[1]
                                : pOriginal;
                              return (
                                <div key={pIdx}>
                                  <label className="block text-[10px] font-black uppercase text-[#0E3A68] mb-1.5">
                                    {pTituloPt} *
                                  </label>
                                  <input
                                    type="text"
                                    required
                                    value={
                                      respostasCustomizadas[i]?.[pTituloPt] ||
                                      ""
                                    }
                                    onChange={(e) =>
                                      setRespostasCustomizadas((prev) => ({
                                        ...prev,
                                        [i]: {
                                          ...(prev[i] || {}),
                                          [pTituloPt]: e.target.value,
                                        },
                                      }))
                                    }
                                    className="w-full p-3.5 bg-white border border-blue-200 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold"
                                  />
                                </div>
                              );
                            }
                          )}
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </section>
            )}

            <section className="bg-white p-6 md:p-10 rounded-[2rem] border border-slate-200 shadow-sm">
              <label className="flex items-start gap-4 cursor-pointer">
                <input
                  type="checkbox"
                  required
                  checked={concordaTermos}
                  onChange={(e) => setConcordaTermos(e.target.checked)}
                  className="mt-1 w-5 h-5 accent-[#0E3A68] cursor-pointer"
                />
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">
                  {isEn ? (
                    <>
                      I accept HelloCamp&apos;s{" "}
                      <Link
                        className="text-[#0E3A68] underline"
                        href={`/${lang}/termos`}
                        target="_blank"
                      >
                        Terms & Conditions
                      </Link>{" "}
                      and the camp&apos;s cancellation policy.
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      Declaro que sou maior de idade e aceito os{" "}
                      <Link
                        className="text-[#0E3A68] underline"
                        href={`/${lang}/termos`}
                        target="_blank"
                      >
                        Termos e Condições
                      </Link>{" "}
                      e a política de cancelamento do programa.
                    </>
                  )}
                </span>
              </label>

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={processingState || !concordaTermos}
                className="w-full mt-6 py-5 px-6 text-sm uppercase tracking-widest font-black rounded-2xl bg-[#e2a41d] hover:bg-[#d4991b] disabled:bg-slate-200 text-slate-900 shadow-xl cursor-pointer transition-all"
              >
                {isExterna
                  ? isEn
                    ? "Proceed to Official External Form ↗"
                    : "Avançar para Formulário Oficial ↗"
                  : isEn
                  ? `Confirm & Pay ${valorACobrarAgora.toFixed(2)}€`
                  : `Confirmar e Pagar ${valorACobrarAgora.toFixed(2)}€`}
              </button>
            </section>
          </form>
        </div>

        {/* SIDEBAR DIREITA - RESUMO */}
        <aside className="flex-[1_1_35%] w-full lg:sticky lg:top-24">
          <div className="bg-white p-6 md:p-8 rounded-[2rem] shadow-sm border border-slate-200">
            <h3 className="text-base font-black mb-6 border-b border-slate-100 pb-4 uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-900">
              {isEn ? "Booking Summary" : "Resumo da Inscrição"}
            </h3>

            <div className="space-y-4 mb-6 text-xs">
              <div>
                <span className="block text-[10px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase mb-1">
                  {isEn ? "Program" : "Programa"}
                </span>
                <p className="text-sm font-black text-slate-900 m-0">
                  {turnoSelecionado?.nome || campo?.nome}
                </p>
              </div>

              <div className="flex justify-between items-center pt-3 border-t border-slate-100">
                <span className="font-bold text-slate-500">
                  {isEn
                    ? `Base Price (x${quantidade})`
                    : `Preço Base (x${quantidade})`}
                </span>
                <span className="font-black text-slate-900">
                  {(precoBaseUnitario * quantidade).toFixed(2)}€
                </span>
              </div>

              {custoAlojamentoUnit > 0 && (
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
                  <span className="font-bold text-slate-500">
                    🛏️ {alojamentoSelecionadoUrl?.nome || "Alojamento"}
                  </span>
                  <span className="font-black text-slate-900">
                    +{(custoAlojamentoUnit * quantidade).toFixed(2)}€
                  </span>
                </div>
              )}

              {extSeguro && (
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
                  <span className="font-bold text-slate-500">🛡️ Seguro</span>
                  <span className="font-black text-slate-900">
                    +{(custoSeguroUnit * quantidade).toFixed(2)}€
                  </span>
                </div>
              )}

              {extTransporte && (
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
                  <span className="font-bold text-slate-500">
                    🚌 Transporte
                  </span>
                  <span className="font-black text-slate-900">
                    +{(custoTransporteUnit * quantidade).toFixed(2)}€
                  </span>
                </div>
              )}

              {taxaMatriculaUnit > 0 && (
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
                  <span className="font-bold text-slate-500">
                    📝 Taxa Matrícula
                  </span>
                  <span className="font-black text-slate-900">
                    +{(taxaMatriculaUnit * quantidade).toFixed(2)}€
                  </span>
                </div>
              )}

              {taxaMaterialUnit > 0 && (
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
                  <span className="font-bold text-slate-500">
                    🎒 Taxa Material
                  </span>
                  <span className="font-black text-slate-900">
                    +{(taxaMaterialUnit * quantidade).toFixed(2)}€
                  </span>
                </div>
              )}

              {descontoUrlValor > 0 && (
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-[#167524] font-black bg-emerald-50 p-2.5 rounded-xl">
                  <span>✨ {descontoUrlNome || "Desconto"}</span>
                  <span>-{descontoUrlValor.toFixed(2)}€</span>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            <div className="p-5 rounded-2xl bg-[#0E3A68] text-white flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
              <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest">
                {isEn ? "Total Value" : "Valor Total"}
              </span>
              <span className="text-2xl font-black">
                {precoFinalTotal.toFixed(2)}€
              </span>
            </div>

            {!isExterna && (
              <div className="flex justify-between items-center bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 p-4 rounded-2xl">
                <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-[#167524]">
                  {isEn ? "To Pay Now" : "A Pagar Agora"}
                </span>
                <span className="text-xl font-black text-[#167524]">
                  {valorACobrarAgora.toFixed(2)}€
                </span>
              </div>
            )}

            {isPagamentoFracionado && !isExterna && (
              <p className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 text-center mt-3 mb-0">
                Restantes {valorPendente.toFixed(2)}€ pagos até 1 semana antes
                no Portal de Cliente.
              </p>
            )}
          </div>
        </aside>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="app/[lang]/(public)/institucional/page.tsx">
"use client";

import React, { useEffect, useState, useRef, use } from "react";
