import Link from "next/link";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";

const FALLBACK_IMG =
  "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1540479859555-17af45c78602?auto=format&fit=crop&q=80&w=1000";

function limparHtml(html: string) {
  if (!html) return "";
  return html.replace(/<[^>]*>?/gm, " ").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
}

export default function InstitucionalIndexPage({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ lang: string }>;
}) {
  const { lang } = use(params);
  const isEn = lang === "en";

  const [iniciativas, setIniciativas] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [distritoFiltro, setDistritoFiltro] = useState("Todos");
  const [pesquisa, setPesquisa] = useState("");
  const [dropdownAberto, setDropdownAberto] = useState(false);
  const dropdownRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const fechar = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (
        dropdownRef.current &&
        !dropdownRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)
      ) {
        setDropdownAberto(false);
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener("mousedown", fechar);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("mousedown", fechar);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    async function carregarIniciativas() {
      setLoading(true);
      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/institucional");
        if (res.ok) {
          const json = await res.json();
          const listaApi = Array.isArray(json) ? json : json.data;
          if (Array.isArray(listaApi) && listaApi.length > 0) {
            setIniciativas(listaApi);
            setLoading(false);
            return;
          }
        }
      } catch (e) {
        // Fallback direto para Supabase
      }

      const { data } = await supabase
        .from("institucional_iniciativas")
        .select("*")
        .order("created_at", { ascending: false });

      setIniciativas(data || []);
      setLoading(false);
    }

    carregarIniciativas();
  }, []);

  const distritosDisponiveis = [
    "Todos",
    ...Array.from(
      new Set(
        iniciativas
          .map((item) => (item.distrito || "").trim())
          .filter(Boolean)
      )
    ).sort(),
  ];

  const iniciativasFiltradas = iniciativas.filter((item) => {
    if (distritoFiltro !== "Todos" && item.distrito !== distritoFiltro) {
      return false;
    }
    if (pesquisa.trim()) {
      const termo = pesquisa.toLowerCase().trim();
      const texto = `${item.titulo || ""} ${item.entidade_organizadora || ""} ${
        item.entidade_nome || ""
      } ${item.concelho || ""} ${item.distrito || ""} ${
        item.descricao_html || ""
      }`.toLowerCase();
      if (!texto.includes(termo)) return false;
    }
    return true;
  });

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-[#F8FAFC] font-sans text-[#0F172A] pb-24">
      {/* HERO AUTARQUIAS */}
      <section className="bg-[#16A34A] text-white pt-20 pb-28 px-4 md:px-8 border-b-8 border-[#F59E0B] relative overflow-hidden">
        <div className="absolute -top-20 -right-20 w-80 h-80 bg-[#F59E0B] rounded-full opacity-90 pointer-events-none hidden lg:block" />
        <div className="max-w-[1280px] mx-auto relative z-10">
          <span className="inline-block bg-[#0F172A] text-white text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-md mb-4">
            🏛️{" "}
            {isEn
              ? "Municipalities & Parishes"
              : "Câmaras Municipais & Juntas de Freguesia"}
          </span>
          <h1 className="text-3xl md:text-6xl font-black tracking-tight text-white mb-4 max-w-3xl leading-tight">
            {isEn
              ? "Public & Municipal Holiday Programs"
              : "Campos de Férias das Autarquias e Juntas"}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-emerald-50 text-base md:text-xl font-medium max-w-2xl leading-relaxed">
            {isEn
              ? "Consult official municipal youth programs, dates, and registration links across Portugal."
              : "Consulte num só portal os programas municipais de férias desportivas e culturais, idades, datas e links oficiais de cada Câmara Municipal e Junta de Freguesia."}
          </p>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* BARRA DE PESQUISA E DROPDOWN CUSTOMIZADO DE DISTRITO */}
      <section className="max-w-[1280px] mx-auto px-4 md:px-8 -mt-10 relative z-30 mb-12">
        <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-md shadow-xl border border-slate-200 flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-4 items-center">
          <div className="w-full md:flex-1">
            <label className="block text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400 mb-1">
              {isEn
                ? "Search Municipality or Program"
                : "Pesquisar Câmara, Junta ou Concelho"}
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={pesquisa}
              onChange={(e) => setPesquisa(e.target.value)}
              placeholder={
                isEn
                  ? "e.g., Cascais, Sintra, Lisboa, Porto..."
                  : "Ex: Cascais, Sintra, Lisboa, Porto, Oeiras..."
              }
              className="w-full bg-[#F8FAFC] border border-slate-200 rounded-md px-4 py-3.5 text-sm font-bold text-[#0F172A] outline-none focus:border-[#16A34A] focus:bg-white"
            />
          </div>

          {/* DROPDOWN CUSTOMIZADO (SEM SELECT NATIVO) */}
          <div ref={dropdownRef} className="w-full md:w-72 relative">
            <label className="block text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400 mb-1">
              {isEn ? "Filter by District" : "Filtrar por Distrito"}
            </label>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setDropdownAberto((prev) => !prev)}
              className="w-full bg-[#F8FAFC] border border-slate-200 hover:border-[#16A34A] rounded-md px-4 py-3.5 text-sm font-bold text-[#0F172A] flex items-center justify-between cursor-pointer"
            >
              <span className="truncate">
                {distritoFiltro === "Todos"
                  ? isEn
                    ? "All Districts"
                    : "Todos os Distritos"
                  : distritoFiltro}
              </span>
              <svg
                className={`w-4 h-4 text-slate-500 transition-transform ${
                  dropdownAberto ? "rotate-180 text-[#16A34A]" : ""
                }`}
                fill="none"
                viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                stroke="currentColor"
                strokeWidth="2.5"
              >
                <path
                  strokeLinecap="round"
                  strokeLinejoin="round"
                  d="M19 9l-7 7-7-7"
                />
              </svg>
            </button>

            {dropdownAberto && (
              <div className="absolute z-50 left-0 right-0 mt-1.5 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md shadow-2xl max-h-64 overflow-y-auto py-1">
                {distritosDisponiveis.map((d) => (
                  <button
                    key={d}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      setDistritoFiltro(d);
                      setDropdownAberto(false);
                    }}
                    className={`w-full text-left px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-between cursor-pointer border-0 ${
                      distritoFiltro === d
                        ? "bg-[#16A34A] text-white font-black"
                        : "bg-white text-[#0F172A] hover:bg-slate-100"
                    }`}
                  >
                    <span>
                      {d === "Todos"
                        ? isEn
                          ? "All Districts"
                          : "Todos os Distritos"
                        : d}
                    </span>
                    {distritoFiltro === d && <span>✓</span>}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {(distritoFiltro !== "Todos" || pesquisa) && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setDistritoFiltro("Todos");
                setPesquisa("");
              }}
              className="w-full md:w-auto mt-5 bg-[#F43F5E] hover:bg-rose-600 text-white text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest px-5 py-3.5 rounded-md border-0 cursor-pointer"
            >
              ✕ {isEn ? "Clear" : "Limpar"}
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* LISTAGEM DE INICIATIVAS AUTÁRQUICAS (LÊ IMAGEM_CAPA_URL E LOGOTIPO_URL REAIS) */}
      <section className="max-w-[1280px] mx-auto px-4 md:px-8">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-8">
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-black text-[#0F172A] m-0">
            {isEn ? "Municipal Initiatives" : "Iniciativas Municipais Ativas"}
          </h2>
          <span className="bg-[#16A34A] text-white text-xs font-black px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-md">
            {iniciativasFiltradas.length} {isEn ? "Programs" : "Programas"}
          </span>
        </div>

        {loading ? (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
            {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6].map((n) => (
              <div
                key={n}
                className="bg-white rounded-md border border-slate-200 h-96 animate-pulse p-6"
              />
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
            {iniciativasFiltradas.map((item) => {
              const titulo =
                item.titulo ||
                item.entidade_organizadora ||
                item.entidade_nome ||
                "Programa Municipal";
              const entidade =
                item.entidade_organizadora ||
                item.entidade_nome ||
                item.concelho ||
                "Autarquia";
              const resumo =
                limparHtml(item.descricao_html) ||
                item.resumo ||
                "Programa municipal de ocupação de tempos livres e férias escolares.";

              // Lê a coluna real `imagem_capa_url` definida na base de dados!
              const fotoDefinida =
                item.imagem_capa_url ||
                item.imagem_url ||
                item.imagem ||
                item.foto_url ||
                FALLBACK_IMG;

              const idadesTexto =
                item.idade_min_global && item.idade_max_global
                  ? `${item.idade_min_global} aos ${item.idade_max_global} anos`
                  : item.faixa_etaria || "6 aos 16 anos";

              return (
                <div
                  key={item.id}
                  className="group flex flex-col bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md shadow-sm hover:shadow-xl hover:border-[#16A34A] transition-all overflow-hidden"
                >
                  <div className="relative h-56 w-full bg-slate-100 overflow-hidden">
                    <img
                      src={fotoDefinida}
                      alt={titulo}
                      loading="lazy"
                      onError={(e) => {
                        e.currentTarget.src = FALLBACK_IMG;
                      }}
                      className="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform duration-500"
                    />
                    <div className="absolute top-3 left-3 bg-[#16A34A] text-white px-3 py-1 rounded-md text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest shadow-sm">
                      🏛️ {item.distrito || "Portugal"}
                    </div>

                    {item.logotipo_url && (
                      <div className="absolute bottom-3 left-3 w-11 h-11 bg-white rounded-md p-1.5 shadow-md border border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden">
                        <img
                          src={item.logotipo_url}
                          alt={entidade}
                          className="w-full h-full object-contain"
                        />
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  <div className="p-6 flex flex-col flex-1">
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-[#2563EB] mb-1">
                      {entidade} {item.concelho ? `• ${item.concelho}` : ""}
                    </span>
                    <h3 className="text-lg font-black text-[#0F172A] leading-snug mb-3 line-clamp-2">
                      {titulo}
                    </h3>
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 font-medium leading-relaxed line-clamp-3 mb-4">
                      {resumo}
                    </p>

                    <div className="mt-auto pt-4 border-t border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                      <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 truncate">
                        👶 {idadesTexto}
                      </span>
                      <Link
                        href={`/${lang}/institucional/${item.id}`}
                        className="bg-[#16A34A] hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white px-4 py-2.5 rounded-md text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest no-underline transition-colors shadow-sm"
                      >
                        {isEn ? "View Details →" : "Ver Programa →"}
                      </Link>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="app/[lang]/(public)/login/page.tsx">
"use client";

import React, { useState, use, Suspense } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";

function LoginForm({ lang }: { lang: string }) {
  const isEn = lang === "en";
  const router = useRouter();
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const redirectTo =
    searchParams.get("redirect") || searchParams.get("redirectTo") || "";

  const [modo, setModo] = useState<"password" | "magic">("password");
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [mostrarPass, setMostrarPass] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [erro, setErro] = useState("");
  const [magicEnviado, setMagicEnviado] = useState(false);
  const [escolhaSuperAdmin, setEscolhaSuperAdmin] = useState(false);

  const redirecionarAposLogin = async (userId: string, userMetaRole?: string) => {
    if (redirectTo) {
      const cleanRedirect = redirectTo.startsWith("/")
        ? redirectTo
        : `/${lang}/${redirectTo}`;
      router.push(cleanRedirect);
      return;
    }

    const savedRedirect =
      typeof window !== "undefined"
        ? sessionStorage.getItem("redirect_after_login")
        : null;
    if (savedRedirect) {
      sessionStorage.removeItem("redirect_after_login");
      router.push(savedRedirect);
      return;
    }

    const { data: perfil } = await supabase
      .from("perfis")
      .select("role, is_superadmin")
      .eq("id", userId)
      .maybeSingle();

    if (perfil?.is_superadmin) {
      setEscolhaSuperAdmin(true);
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    const role = perfil?.role || userMetaRole || "cliente";

    if (role === "organizador" || role === "parceiro" || role === "admin") {
      router.push(`/${lang}/admin/dashboard`);
    } else if (role === "monitor") {
      router.push(`/${lang}/monitores/portal/perfil`);
    } else {
      router.push(`/${lang}/cliente/dashboard`);
    }
  };

  const handleLoginPassword = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    setErro("");

    const cleanEmail = email.trim().toLowerCase();
    const cleanPassword = password.trim();

    try {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({
        email: cleanEmail,
        password: cleanPassword,
      });

      if (error || !data?.user) {
        setErro(
          isEn
            ? `Invalid email or password. Please check your credentials.`
            : `E-mail ou palavra-passe incorretos. Por favor, verifique os seus dados.`
        );
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      await redirecionarAposLogin(
        data.user.id,
        data.user.user_metadata?.role
      );
    } catch (err: any) {
      setErro(
        err?.message ||
          (isEn ? "Error signing in." : "Ocorreu um erro ao iniciar sessão.")
      );
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleMagicLink = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    setErro("");

    const cleanEmail = email.trim().toLowerCase();

    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/auth/acesso", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          action: "magic_link",
          email: cleanEmail,
          lang,
        }),
      });

      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error(
          data.error || "Não foi possível enviar o link mágico."
        );
      }

      setMagicEnviado(true);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setErro(err.message);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-white rounded-md shadow-2xl border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden">
      {/* BARRA DE CORES OFICIAIS HELLO CAMP */}
      <div className="w-full h-2 flex">
        <div className="w-1/4 h-full bg-[#F59E0B]" />
        <div className="w-1/4 h-full bg-[#2563EB]" />
        <div className="w-1/4 h-full bg-[#16A34A]" />
        <div className="w-1/4 h-full bg-[#F43F5E]" />
      </div>

      <div className="p-8 md:p-10">
        <div className="text-center mb-8">
          <Link
            href={`/${lang}`}
            className="text-3xl font-black tracking-tight inline-flex items-center no-underline mb-3"
          >
            <span className="text-[#0F172A]">Hello</span>
            <span className="text-[#F59E0B]">Camp</span>
          </Link>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-black text-[#0F172A] m-0">
            {escolhaSuperAdmin
              ? isEn
                ? "Select Workspace"
                : "Escolha o Painel de Acesso"
              : isEn
              ? "Welcome back"
              : "Iniciar Sessão"}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 mt-1 mb-0">
            {escolhaSuperAdmin
              ? isEn
                ? "Your account has SuperAdmin and Partner privileges"
                : "A sua conta possui permissões de SuperAdmin HQ e de Parceiro Organizador"
              : isEn
              ? "Access your Family, Partner, or Monitor Portal"
              : "Aceda à sua Área de Família, Parceiro ou Monitor"}
          </p>
        </div>

        {escolhaSuperAdmin ? (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            <Link
              href={`/${lang}/superadmin/parceiros`}
              className="w-full bg-[#0F172A] hover:bg-slate-800 text-white p-5 rounded-md no-underline flex items-center justify-between shadow-md transition-colors border-l-4 border-[#F59E0B]"
            >
              <div>
                <span className="bg-[#F59E0B] text-white text-[9px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md inline-block mb-1">
                  SuperAdmin HQ
                </span>
                <div className="text-sm font-black text-white">
                  🛡️ Gestão Global da Plataforma (SuperAdmin)
                </div>
              </div>
              <span className="text-xl font-black text-white">&rarr;</span>
            </Link>

            <Link
              href={`/${lang}/admin/dashboard`}
              className="w-full bg-[#2563EB] hover:bg-blue-700 text-white p-5 rounded-md no-underline flex items-center justify-between shadow-md transition-colors border-l-4 border-[#16A34A]"
            >
              <div>
                <span className="bg-[#16A34A] text-white text-[9px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md inline-block mb-1">
                  Portal B2B
                </span>
                <div className="text-sm font-black text-white">
                  🏕️ Painel de Parceiro Organizador (Meus Campos)
                </div>
              </div>
              <span className="text-xl font-black text-white">&rarr;</span>
            </Link>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 p-1 bg-[#F8FAFC] rounded-md border border-slate-200 mb-6">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setModo("password");
                  setErro("");
                }}
                className={`py-2.5 rounded-md text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider cursor-pointer border-0 transition-all ${
                  modo === "password"
                    ? "bg-[#2563EB] text-white shadow-sm"
                    : "bg-transparent text-slate-600 hover:text-[#0F172A]"
                }`}
              >
                🔑 {isEn ? "With Password" : "Com Password"}
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setModo("magic");
                  setErro("");
                }}
                className={`py-2.5 rounded-md text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider cursor-pointer border-0 transition-all ${
                  modo === "magic"
                    ? "bg-[#F59E0B] text-white shadow-sm"
                    : "bg-transparent text-slate-600 hover:text-[#0F172A]"
                }`}
              >
                ✨ {isEn ? "Magic Link" : "Link por E-mail"}
              </button>
            </div>

            {erro && (
              <div className="mb-6 p-4 bg-rose-50 border-2 border-[#F43F5E] text-[#F43F5E] rounded-md text-xs font-black leading-relaxed">
                ⚠️ {erro}
              </div>
            )}

            {modo === "password" ? (
              <form onSubmit={handleLoginPassword} className="space-y-5">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-500 mb-2">
                    {isEn ? "Email Address" : "E-mail"}
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="email"
                    required
                    value={email}
                    onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="nome@email.com"
                    className="w-full bg-[#F8FAFC] border border-slate-300 rounded-md px-4 py-3.5 text-sm font-bold text-[#0F172A] outline-none focus:border-[#2563EB] focus:bg-white transition-colors"
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
                    <label className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-500">
                      {isEn ? "Password" : "Palavra-passe"}
                    </label>
                    <Link
                      href={`/${lang}/recuperar-password`}
                      className="text-[11px] font-black text-[#2563EB] hover:underline no-underline"
                    >
                      {isEn ? "Forgot password?" : "Esqueceu a password?"}
                    </Link>
                  </div>
                  <div className="relative">
                    <input
                      type={mostrarPass ? "text" : "password"}
                      required
                      value={password}
                      onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="••••••••"
                      className="w-full bg-[#F8FAFC] border border-slate-300 rounded-md pl-4 pr-16 py-3.5 text-sm font-bold text-[#0F172A] outline-none focus:border-[#2563EB] focus:bg-white transition-colors"
                    />
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setMostrarPass(!mostrarPass)}
                      className="absolute right-2.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 bg-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-300 text-[#0F172A] text-[10px] font-black uppercase px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-md cursor-pointer border-0"
                    >
                      {mostrarPass
                        ? isEn
                          ? "Hide"
                          : "Ocultar"
                        : isEn
                        ? "Show"
                        : "Ver"}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={loading}
                  className="w-full bg-[#2563EB] hover:bg-blue-700 text-white font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-xs py-4 rounded-md shadow-md transition-colors cursor-pointer border-0 disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  {loading
                    ? isEn
                      ? "Signing in..."
                      : "A entrar..."
                    : isEn
                    ? "Sign In →"
                    : "Entrar na Conta →"}
                </button>
              </form>
            ) : magicEnviado ? (
              <div className="bg-emerald-50 border-2 border-[#16A34A] p-6 rounded-md text-center">
                <span className="text-3xl block mb-2">📬</span>
                <h3 className="text-base font-black text-[#0F172A] mb-1">
                  {isEn ? "Check your inbox!" : "Verifique o seu e-mail!"}
                </h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 font-medium m-0">
                  {isEn
                    ? `We sent an instant login link via Resend to ${email}. Click it to enter automatically.`
                    : `Enviámos um link de acesso direto (via Resend) para ${email}. Basta clicar no botão do e-mail para entrar imediatamente!`}
                </p>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <form onSubmit={handleMagicLink} className="space-y-5">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-500 mb-2">
                    {isEn ? "Your Email Address" : "O Seu E-mail"}
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="email"
                    required
                    value={email}
                    onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="nome@email.com"
                    className="w-full bg-[#F8FAFC] border border-slate-300 rounded-md px-4 py-3.5 text-sm font-bold text-[#0F172A] outline-none focus:border-[#F59E0B] focus:bg-white transition-colors"
                  />
                </div>

                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={loading}
                  className="w-full bg-[#F59E0B] hover:bg-amber-600 text-white font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-xs py-4 rounded-md shadow-md transition-colors cursor-pointer border-0 disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  {loading
                    ? isEn
                      ? "Sending Link..."
                      : "A enviar link..."
                    : isEn
                    ? "Send Instant Login Link →"
                    : "Receber Link de Acesso no E-mail →"}
                </button>
              </form>
            )}

            <div className="mt-8 pt-6 border-t border-slate-100 text-center space-y-3">
              <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 m-0">
                {isEn ? "Don't have an account yet?" : "Ainda não tem conta?"}{" "}
                <Link
                  href={`/${lang}/registo`}
                  className="text-[#2563EB] font-black hover:underline no-underline"
                >
                  {isEn ? "Create Free Account" : "Criar Conta Grátis"}
                </Link>
              </p>
            </div>
          </>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function LoginPage({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ lang: string }>;
}) {
  const { lang } = use(params);
  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center p-4 md:p-12 bg-[#F8FAFC] font-sans">
      <Suspense
        fallback={
          <div className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400">
            A carregar...
          </div>
        }
      >
        <LoginForm lang={lang} />
      </Suspense>
    </main>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="app/[lang]/(public)/mapa/MapaMapLibre.tsx">
"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useMemo } from "react";
import * as maplibregl from "maplibre-gl";
import "maplibre-gl/dist/maplibre-gl.css";

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// WORKER DO MAPLIBRE
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
  try {
    // @ts-ignore
    if (typeof (maplibregl as any).setWorkerUrl === "function") {
      (maplibregl as any).setWorkerUrl(
        "https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/maplibre-gl@5.0.1/dist/maplibre-gl-csp-worker.js"
      );
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn("[MAPA] setWorkerUrl falhou:", e);
  }
}

interface Campo {
  id: string | number;
  nome: string;
  imagem?: string;
  categoria?: string;
  preco?: number;
  latitude: number;
  longitude: number;
}

interface MapaMapLibreProps {
  campos: any[];
  lang: string;
}

const FALLBACK_IMG =
  "data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg' viewBox='0 0 400 200'%3E%3Crect fill='%23f1f5f9' width='400' height='200'/%3E%3Ctext x='50%25' y='50%25' font-family='sans-serif' font-size='16' font-weight='bold' fill='%2394a3b8' text-anchor='middle' dy='.3em'%3EHelloCamp%3C/text%3E%3C/svg%3E";

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// ESTILO ESRI WORLD STREET MAP (Inglês, Grátis, Sem API Key)
// Design Premium e Zoom profundo até ao nível da rua.
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
const esriRasterStyle: maplibregl.StyleSpecification = {
  version: 8,
  sources: {
    'esri-tiles': {
      type: 'raster',
      tiles: [
        'https://server.arcgisonline.com/ArcGIS/rest/services/World_Street_Map/MapServer/tile/{z}/{y}/{x}'
      ],
      tileSize: 256,
      attribution: '&copy; <a href="https://www.esri.com/" target="_blank">Esri</a>, NAVTEQ, Garmin'
    }
  },
  layers: [
    {
      id: 'esri-tiles-layer',
      type: 'raster',
      source: 'esri-tiles',
      minzoom: 0,
      maxzoom: 19
    }
  ]
};

export default function MapaMapLibre({ campos, lang }: MapaMapLibreProps) {
  const mapContainer = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  const map = useRef<maplibregl.Map | null>(null);
  const isEn = lang === "en";

  const camposValidos = useMemo<Campo[]>(() => {
    const lista = campos || [];
    const contagemCoords: Record<string, number> = {};

    return lista
      .map((c): Campo | null => {
        const latBase = Number(c.latitude);
        const lngBase = Number(c.longitude);
        if (
          c.latitude == null || c.longitude == null ||
          c.latitude === "" || c.longitude === "" ||
          isNaN(latBase) || isNaN(lngBase) ||
          latBase === 0 || lngBase === 0
        ) return null;

        // Se dois campos tiverem exatamente a mesma coordenada, afasta-os ligeiramente em círculo (~35m)
        const chave = `${latBase.toFixed(4)},${lngBase.toFixed(4)}`;
        const ocorrencia = contagemCoords[chave] || 0;
        contagemCoords[chave] = ocorrencia + 1;

        let lat = latBase;
        let lng = lngBase;
        if (ocorrencia > 0) {
          const angulo = (ocorrencia * 2 * Math.PI) / 6;
          const raio = 0.00035 * Math.ceil(ocorrencia / 6);
          lat = latBase + Math.sin(angulo) * raio;
          lng = lngBase + Math.cos(angulo) * raio;
        }

        return {
          id: c.id,
          nome: isEn && c.nome_en ? c.nome_en : c.nome,
          imagem: c.imagem,
          categoria: isEn && c.categoria_en ? c.categoria_en : c.categoria,
          preco: Number(c.preco) || 0,
          latitude: lat,
          longitude: lng,
        };
      })
      .filter((c): c is Campo => c !== null);
  }, [campos, isEn]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!mapContainer.current || map.current) return;

    const instance = new maplibregl.Map({
      container: mapContainer.current,
      style: esriRasterStyle,
      center: [-8.2245, 39.3999],
      zoom: 5,
      minZoom: 3,
      maxZoom: 19,
      attributionControl: { compact: true },
    });

    instance.dragRotate.disable();
    instance.touchZoomRotate.disableRotation();
    instance.keyboard.disableRotation();

    map.current = instance;

    instance.addControl(
      new maplibregl.NavigationControl({ showCompass: false }),
      "top-right"
    );

    const resizeObserver = new ResizeObserver(() => instance.resize());
    if (mapContainer.current) resizeObserver.observe(mapContainer.current);
    const raf = requestAnimationFrame(() => instance.resize());

    return () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
      resizeObserver.disconnect();
      instance.remove();
      map.current = null;
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const instance = map.current;
    if (!instance) return;

    const setupMarkers = () => {
      const existing = (instance as any)._hcMarkers as maplibregl.Marker[] | undefined;
      if (existing) existing.forEach((m) => m.remove());
      (instance as any)._hcMarkers = [];

      if (camposValidos.length === 0) return;

      const newMarkers: maplibregl.Marker[] = [];

      camposValidos.forEach((c) => {
        // IMPORTANTE: 'el' é o contentor gerido pelo MapLibre (tem de ser position: absolute; top: 0; left: 0;)
        const el = document.createElement("div");
        el.className = "hc-marker-root";
        
        // Z-Index dinâmico no elemento raiz para garantir que o hover fica por cima dos outros pinos
        el.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => { el.style.zIndex = "999"; });
        el.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => { el.style.zIndex = ""; });

        // O '.hc-marker-container' com position: relative vai DENTRO de 'el' para não estragar a posição geográfica!
        el.innerHTML = `
          <div class="hc-marker-container">
            <div class="hc-pin-svg">
              <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="36" height="36" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
                <path d="M12 2C8.13 2 5 5.13 5 9c0 5.25 7 13 7 13s7-7.75 7-13c0-3.87-3.13-7-7-7zm0 9.5c-1.38 0-2.5-1.12-2.5-2.5s1.12-2.5 2.5-2.5 2.5 1.12 2.5 2.5-1.12 2.5-2.5 2.5z" fill="#e2a41d" stroke="#ffffff" stroke-width="1.5"/>
              </svg>
            </div>

            <!-- Ponte invisível para o rato não perder o hover entre o pino e o card -->
            <div class="hc-hover-bridge"></div>

            <!-- O Card do Campo (Pop-up via CSS Hover) -->
            <div class="hc-hover-popup">
              <div class="hc-popup-img-wrap">
                <img src="${c.imagem || FALLBACK_IMG}" alt="${escapeHtml(c.nome)}" class="hc-popup-img" loading="lazy" onerror="this.src='${FALLBACK_IMG}'"/>
                ${c.categoria ? `<span class="hc-popup-badge">${escapeHtml(c.categoria)}</span>` : ""}
              </div>
              <div class="hc-popup-body">
                <h3 class="hc-popup-title">${escapeHtml(c.nome)}</h3>
                <div class="hc-popup-footer">
                  ${c.preco != null && c.preco > 0 ? `
                    <div>
                      <span class="hc-popup-from">${isEn ? 'From' : 'Desde'}</span>
                      <p class="hc-popup-price">${c.preco}€</p>
                    </div>
                  ` : '<div></div>'}
                  <a href="/${lang}/campo/${c.id}" class="hc-popup-cta">
                    ${isEn ? "View Dates" : "Ver Datas"}
                  </a>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        `;

        // offset: [0, 3] compensa a margem interna inferior do viewBox 24x24 do SVG para cravar o bico na coordenada
        const marker = new maplibregl.Marker({ element: el, anchor: "bottom", offset: [0, 3] })
          .setLngLat([c.longitude, c.latitude])
          .addTo(instance);

        newMarkers.push(marker);
      });

      (instance as any)._hcMarkers = newMarkers;

      if (camposValidos.length === 1) {
        instance.flyTo({ center: [camposValidos[0].longitude, camposValidos[0].latitude], zoom: 11, duration: 800 });
      } else {
        const bounds = new maplibregl.LngLatBounds();
        camposValidos.forEach((c) => bounds.extend([c.longitude, c.latitude]));
        instance.fitBounds(bounds, { padding: 80, duration: 800, maxZoom: 12 });
      }
    };

    setupMarkers();
    instance.once("load", setupMarkers);
  }, [camposValidos, lang, isEn]);

  return (
    <>
      <style>{`
        /* Garante que o marcador raiz do MapLibre NUNCA entra em fluxo normal de blocos */
        .maplibregl-marker.hc-marker-root {
          position: absolute !important;
          top: 0 !important;
          left: 0 !important;
          width: 36px;
          height: 36px;
          pointer-events: auto;
        }

        /* O Contentor Interno do Pino */
        .hc-marker-container { 
          position: relative; 
          width: 36px; height: 36px; 
          display: flex; justify-content: center; align-items: flex-end; 
          cursor: pointer;
        }

        /* O SVG do Pino em si */
        .hc-pin-svg {
          display: flex;
          transform-origin: bottom center;
          transition: transform 0.2s cubic-bezier(0.34, 1.56, 0.64, 1);
          filter: drop-shadow(0px 4px 6px rgba(226, 164, 29, 0.4));
        }

        .hc-marker-container:hover .hc-pin-svg {
          transform: scale(1.15) translateY(-2px);
        }

        /* Ponte invisível para o Hover não quebrar */
        .hc-hover-bridge {
          position: absolute;
          bottom: 30px;
          left: 50%;
          transform: translateX(-50%);
          width: 60px;
          height: 20px;
          background: transparent;
        }

        /* O Card (Popup) que aparece por cima */
        .hc-hover-popup {
          position: absolute;
          bottom: 44px; /* Fica logo acima do pino */
          left: 50%;
          transform: translateX(-50%) translateY(10px);
          width: 260px;
          background: white;
          border-radius: 16px;
          box-shadow: 0 10px 30px -5px rgba(0,0,0,0.2);
          opacity: 0;
          visibility: hidden;
          transition: all 0.2s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1);
          pointer-events: none; /* Para não bloquear cliques até estar visível */
          overflow: hidden;
          border: 1px solid #f1f5f9;
        }

        /* A Magia do Hover: Mostrar o Card */
        .hc-marker-container:hover .hc-hover-popup {
          opacity: 1;
          visibility: visible;
          transform: translateX(-50%) translateY(0);
          pointer-events: auto; /* Permite clicar no botão "Ver Datas" */
        }

        /* Design do Interior do Card */
        .hc-popup-img-wrap { position: relative; width: 100%; height: 140px; background: #f8fafc; }
        .hc-popup-img { width: 100%; height: 100%; object-fit: cover; display: block; margin: 0; }
        .hc-popup-badge {
          position: absolute; top: 12px; left: 12px;
          background: rgba(255,255,255,0.95); backdrop-filter: blur(4px);
          color: #0f172a; font-size: 9px; font-weight: 900;
          text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.08em;
          padding: 6px 10px; border-radius: 8px; box-shadow: 0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.05);
        }
        
        .hc-popup-body { padding: 16px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 12px; }
        
        .hc-popup-title {
          margin: 0; font-size: 15px; font-weight: 900; color: #0f172a; line-height: 1.3;
          display: -webkit-box; -webkit-line-clamp: 2; -webkit-box-orient: vertical; overflow: hidden; text-align: left;
        }
        
        .hc-popup-footer {
          display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: flex-end;
          margin-top: 4px; padding-top: 12px; border-top: 1px solid #f1f5f9;
        }

        .hc-popup-from {
          display: block; font-size: 9px; font-weight: 800; color: #94a3b8; 
          text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.05em; margin-bottom: 2px;
        }

        .hc-popup-price { margin: 0; font-size: 20px; font-weight: 900; color: #0f172a; text-align: left; line-height: 1;}
        
        .hc-popup-cta {
          background: #0f172a; color: white;
          font-size: 11px; font-weight: 900; padding: 10px 16px;
          border-radius: 10px; text-decoration: none;
          text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.05em; transition: background 0.15s ease;
        }
        
        .hc-popup-cta:hover { background: #e2a41d; }
      `}</style>
      <div ref={mapContainer} className="w-full h-full bg-[#f1f5f9]" style={{ minHeight: "400px" }} />
    </>
  );
}

function escapeHtml(str: string): string {
  if (!str) return "";
  return String(str)
    .replace(/&/g, "&amp;")
    .replace(/</g, "&lt;")
    .replace(/>/g, "&gt;")
    .replace(/"/g, "&quot;")
    .replace(/'/g, "&#039;");
}

<file path="app/[lang]/(public)/components/header.tsx">
"use client";

import React, { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter, usePathname } from "next/navigation";
import AuthButton from "./AuthButton";
import LanguageSwitcher from "./LanguageSwitcher";

export interface HeaderProps {
  dict?: any;
  lang: string;
}

export default function Header({ dict, lang }: HeaderProps) {
  const isEn = lang === "en";
  const router = useRouter();
  const pathname = usePathname();

  const [menuAberto, setMenuAberto] = useState(false);
  const [searchOpen, setSearchOpen] = useState(false);
  const [termoPesquisa, setTermoPesquisa] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if ((e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey) && e.key.toLowerCase() === "k") {
        e.preventDefault();
        setSearchOpen((prev) => !prev);
      }
      if (e.key === "Escape") {
        setSearchOpen(false);
        setMenuAberto(false);
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
  }, []);

  const handleQuickSearch = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setSearchOpen(false);
    setMenuAberto(false);
    if (termoPesquisa.trim()) {
      router.push(
        `/${lang}/pesquisa?q=${encodeURIComponent(termoPesquisa.trim())}`
      );
    } else {
      router.push(`/${lang}/pesquisa`);
    }
  };

  const navLinks = [
    {
      href: `/${lang}/pesquisa`,
      label: isEn ? "Explore" : "Explorar",
      active: pathname?.includes("/pesquisa"),
    },
    {
      href: `/${lang}/institucional`,
      label: isEn ? "Municipalities" : "Autarquias",
      active: pathname?.includes("/institucional"),
    },
    {
      href: `/${lang}/monitores`,
      label: isEn ? "Monitors" : "Monitores",
      active: pathname?.includes("/monitores"),
    },
    {
      href: `/${lang}/guia-pais`,
      label: isEn ? "Parents Guide" : "Guia de Pais",
      active: pathname?.includes("/guia-pais"),
    },
    {
      href: `/${lang}/blog`,
      label: "Blog",
      active: pathname?.includes("/blog"),
    },
  ];

  return (
    <>
      <header className="sticky top-0 z-50 w-full bg-white/95 backdrop-blur-md border-b border-slate-200/80 font-sans">
        <div className="max-w-[1280px] mx-auto px-4 md:px-8 h-16 md:h-[74px] flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
          <Link
            href={`/${lang}`}
            className="text-2xl md:text-[26px] font-black tracking-tight flex items-center no-underline flex-shrink-0"
          >
            <span className="text-[#0F172A]">Hello</span>
            <span className="text-[#F59E0B]">Camp</span>
          </Link>

          <nav className="hidden lg:flex items-center gap-1 xl:gap-2">
            {navLinks.map((item) => (
              <Link
                key={item.href}
                href={item.href}
                className={`px-3.5 py-2 rounded-md text-[13px] font-bold whitespace-nowrap no-underline transition-all ${
                  item.active
                    ? "bg-[#2563EB] text-white font-black shadow-sm"
                    : "text-slate-600 hover:text-[#0F172A] hover:bg-slate-100"
                }`}
              >
                {item.label}
              </Link>
            ))}
          </nav>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 md:gap-3.5 flex-shrink-0">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setSearchOpen(true)}
              className="w-9 h-9 rounded-md bg-[#F8FAFC] hover:bg-slate-200/80 text-slate-600 flex items-center justify-center transition-colors border border-slate-200 cursor-pointer"
              title={isEn ? "Quick Search (⌘K)" : "Pesquisa Rápida (⌘K)"}
              aria-label="Pesquisar"
            >
              <svg
                className="w-4 h-4"
                viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                fill="none"
                stroke="currentColor"
                strokeWidth="2.5"
              >
                <circle cx="11" cy="11" r="8"></circle>
                <line x1="21" y1="21" x2="16.65" y2="16.65"></line>
              </svg>
            </button>

            <Link
              href={`/${lang}/parceiro`}
              className="hidden md:inline-flex items-center text-[11px] font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-white bg-[#F59E0B] hover:bg-amber-600 px-4 py-2 rounded-md transition-all no-underline whitespace-nowrap shadow-sm"
            >
              {isEn ? "Partners" : "Parceiros"}
            </Link>

            <div className="hidden sm:block h-4 w-px bg-slate-200" />

            <div className="hidden sm:block">
              <LanguageSwitcher lang={lang} />
            </div>

            <AuthButton dict={dict} lang={lang} />

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setMenuAberto(!menuAberto)}
              className="lg:hidden p-2 rounded-md text-[#0F172A] hover:bg-slate-100 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              aria-label="Menu"
            >
              {menuAberto ? (
                <svg
                  className="w-6 h-6"
                  fill="none"
                  stroke="currentColor"
                  viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                  strokeWidth="2.5"
                >
                  <path
                    strokeLinecap="round"
                    strokeLinejoin="round"
                    d="M6 18L18 6M6 6l12 12"
                  />
                </svg>
              ) : (
                <svg
                  className="w-6 h-6"
                  fill="none"
                  stroke="currentColor"
                  viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                  strokeWidth="2.5"
                >
                  <path
                    strokeLinecap="round"
                    strokeLinejoin="round"
                    d="M4 6h16M4 12h16M4 18h16"
                  />
                </svg>
              )}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {menuAberto && (
          <div className="lg:hidden bg-white border-t border-slate-200 px-6 py-6 shadow-xl flex flex-col gap-3">
            <form onSubmit={handleQuickSearch} className="relative w-full mb-2">
              <input
                type="text"
                value={termoPesquisa}
                onChange={(e) => setTermoPesquisa(e.target.value)}
                placeholder={
                  isEn
                    ? "Search camps, sports, cities..."
                    : "Pesquisar campos, desportos, locais..."
                }
                className="w-full bg-[#F8FAFC] border border-slate-200 rounded-md px-4 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-[#0F172A] outline-none focus:border-[#2563EB]"
              />
              <button
                type="submit"
                className="absolute right-3 top-2.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-[#2563EB] bg-transparent border-0 cursor-pointer"
              >
                🔍
              </button>
            </form>

            {navLinks.map((item) => (
              <Link
                key={item.href}
                href={item.href}
                onClick={() => setMenuAberto(false)}
                className="text-sm font-black text-[#0F172A] py-2.5 border-b border-slate-100 no-underline"
              >
                {item.label}
              </Link>
            ))}

            <Link
              href={`/${lang}/parceiro`}
              onClick={() => setMenuAberto(false)}
              className="mt-2 w-full text-center bg-[#F59E0B] text-white font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-xs py-3.5 rounded-md no-underline shadow-md"
            >
              {isEn ? "For Organizers (B2B)" : "Área de Parceiros (B2B)"}
            </Link>

            <div className="pt-4 flex items-center justify-between border-t border-slate-100">
              <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest">
                {isEn ? "Language" : "Idioma"}
              </span>
              <LanguageSwitcher lang={lang} />
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </header>

      {searchOpen && (
        <div
          className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-[#0F172A]/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-start justify-center pt-24 px-4"
          onClick={() => setSearchOpen(false)}
        >
          <div
            className="bg-white w-full max-w-xl rounded-md shadow-2xl border border-slate-200 p-6"
            onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
              <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400">
                {isEn ? "Quick Search" : "Pesquisa Rápida"}
              </span>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setSearchOpen(false)}
                className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 cursor-pointer bg-transparent border-0"
              >
                ESC ✕
              </button>
            </div>
            <form onSubmit={handleQuickSearch} className="flex gap-2">
              <input
                type="text"
                autoFocus
                value={termoPesquisa}
                onChange={(e) => setTermoPesquisa(e.target.value)}
                placeholder={
                  isEn
                    ? "Type a sport, city, or camp name..."
                    : "Escreva um desporto, cidade ou nome do campo..."
                }
                className="flex-1 bg-[#F8FAFC] border border-slate-200 rounded-md px-4 py-3.5 text-base font-bold text-[#0F172A] outline-none focus:border-[#2563EB] focus:bg-white"
              />
              <button
                type="submit"
                className="bg-[#2563EB] hover:bg-blue-700 text-white font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-xs px-6 py-3.5 rounded-md transition-colors cursor-pointer border-0"
              >
                {isEn ? "Search" : "Ir"}
              </button>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

<file path="app/[lang]/(public)/mapa/page.tsx">
"use client";

import React, { useEffect, useState, useRef, use } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";
import MapaWrapper from "./MapaWrapper";

export default function PaginaMapa({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ lang: string }>;
}) {
  const { lang } = use(params);
  const isEn = lang === "en";

  const [campos, setCampos] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [categoriaFiltro, setCategoriaFiltro] = useState("Todas");
  const [distritoFiltro, setDistritoFiltro] = useState("Todos");
  const [dropCatOpen, setDropCatOpen] = useState(false);
  const [dropDistOpen, setDropDistOpen] = useState(false);

  const catRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const distRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const closeDrops = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (catRef.current && !catRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
        setDropCatOpen(false);
      }
      if (distRef.current && !distRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
        setDropDistOpen(false);
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener("mousedown", closeDrops);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("mousedown", closeDrops);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    async function fetchCampos() {
      setLoading(true);
      const { data } = await supabase
        .from("campos")
        .select("*")
        .eq("ativo", true);
      setCampos(data || []);
      setLoading(false);
    }
    fetchCampos();
  }, []);

  const categorias = [
    "Todas",
    ...Array.from(
      new Set(
        campos
          .map((c) => (isEn && c.categoria_en ? c.categoria_en : c.categoria))
          .filter(Boolean)
      )
    ),
  ];

  const distritos = [
    "Todos",
    ...Array.from(
      new Set(
        campos
          .map((c) => c.Distrito || c.local || c.local_en)
          .filter(Boolean)
      )
    ).sort(),
  ];

  const camposFiltrados = campos.filter((c) => {
    const catReal = isEn && c.categoria_en ? c.categoria_en : c.categoria;
    if (categoriaFiltro !== "Todas" && catReal !== categoriaFiltro) {
      return false;
    }
    const loc = c.Distrito || c.local;
    if (distritoFiltro !== "Todos" && loc !== distritoFiltro) {
      return false;
    }
    return true;
  });

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-[#F8FAFC] font-sans text-[#0F172A] pb-16 flex flex-col">
      {/* BARRA DE TOPO DO MAPA */}
      <section className="bg-[#2563EB] text-white py-8 px-4 md:px-8 border-b-4 border-[#F59E0B]">
        <div className="max-w-[1400px] mx-auto flex flex-col lg:flex-row lg:items-center justify-between gap-6">
          <div>
            <span className="inline-block bg-[#F59E0B] text-white text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest px-3 py-1 rounded-md mb-2">
              🗺️ {isEn ? "Geolocation Explorer" : "Exploração Geográfica"}
            </span>
            <h1 className="text-2xl md:text-4xl font-black tracking-tight text-white m-0">
              {isEn
                ? "Interactive Map of Holiday Camps"
                : "Mapa Interativo de Campos de Férias"}
            </h1>
          </div>

          {/* FILTROS COM DROPDOWNS 100% CUSTOMIZADOS */}
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
            {/* Dropdown Categoria */}
            <div ref={catRef} className="relative w-52">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setDropCatOpen((p) => !p)}
                className="w-full bg-white text-[#0F172A] px-4 py-3 rounded-md text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center justify-between border-0 cursor-pointer shadow-md"
              >
                <span className="truncate">
                  {categoriaFiltro === "Todas"
                    ? isEn
                      ? "All Categories"
                      : "Todas as Categorias"
                    : categoriaFiltro}
                </span>
                <span>▾</span>
              </button>
              {dropCatOpen && (
                <div className="absolute z-50 left-0 right-0 mt-1.5 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md shadow-2xl max-h-60 overflow-y-auto py-1">
                  {categorias.map((cat) => (
                    <button
                      key={cat}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => {
                        setCategoriaFiltro(cat);
                        setDropCatOpen(false);
                      }}
                      className={`w-full text-left px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-between cursor-pointer border-0 ${
                        categoriaFiltro === cat
                          ? "bg-[#2563EB] text-white font-black"
                          : "bg-white text-[#0F172A] hover:bg-slate-100"
                      }`}
                    >
                      <span className="truncate">{cat}</span>
                      {categoriaFiltro === cat && <span>✓</span>}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Dropdown Distrito */}
            <div ref={distRef} className="relative w-52">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setDropDistOpen((p) => !p)}
                className="w-full bg-white text-[#0F172A] px-4 py-3 rounded-md text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center justify-between border-0 cursor-pointer shadow-md"
              >
                <span className="truncate">
                  {distritoFiltro === "Todos"
                    ? isEn
                      ? "All Districts"
                      : "Todos os Distritos"
                    : distritoFiltro}
                </span>
                <span>▾</span>
              </button>
              {dropDistOpen && (
                <div className="absolute z-50 left-0 right-0 mt-1.5 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md shadow-2xl max-h-60 overflow-y-auto py-1">
                  {distritos.map((d) => (
                    <button
                      key={d}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => {
                        setDistritoFiltro(d);
                        setDropDistOpen(false);
                      }}
                      className={`w-full text-left px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-between cursor-pointer border-0 ${
                        distritoFiltro === d
                          ? "bg-[#2563EB] text-white font-black"
                          : "bg-white text-[#0F172A] hover:bg-slate-100"
                      }`}
                    >
                      <span className="truncate">{d}</span>
                      {distritoFiltro === d && <span>✓</span>}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            <Link
              href={`/${lang}/pesquisa`}
              className="bg-[#F59E0B] hover:bg-amber-600 text-white px-5 py-3 rounded-md text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest no-underline shadow-md"
            >
              {isEn ? "List View →" : "Ver em Lista →"}
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* CORPO: LISTA LATERAL + MAPA INTERATIVO (USANDO MapaWrapper) */}
      <section className="max-w-[1400px] mx-auto px-4 md:px-8 pt-6 grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-6 flex-1 w-full">
        <div className="lg:col-span-4 space-y-4 max-h-[680px] overflow-y-auto pr-1">
          <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-md border border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between shadow-sm">
            <span className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-[#0F172A]">
              {camposFiltrados.length}{" "}
              {isEn ? "Camps on Map" : "Campos no Mapa"}
            </span>
            {(categoriaFiltro !== "Todas" || distritoFiltro !== "Todos") && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setCategoriaFiltro("Todas");
                  setDistritoFiltro("Todos");
                }}
                className="bg-[#F43F5E] text-white text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest px-3 py-1 rounded-md border-0 cursor-pointer shadow-sm"
              >
                ✕ {isEn ? "Reset" : "Limpar"}
              </button>
            )}
          </div>

          {loading ? (
            <div className="space-y-3">
              {[1, 2, 3].map((i) => (
                <div
                  key={i}
                  className="bg-white p-4 rounded-md h-28 animate-pulse border border-slate-200"
                />
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : camposFiltrados.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="bg-white p-8 rounded-md text-center border border-slate-200">
              <p className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-500 m-0">
                {isEn
                  ? "No camps found for this filter."
                  : "Nenhum campo encontrado com estes filtros."}
              </p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            camposFiltrados.map((c) => {
              const nome = isEn && c.nome_en ? c.nome_en : c.nome;
              const local = c.Distrito || c.local || "Portugal";
              const img = c.imagem?.startsWith("http")
                ? c.imagem
                : "/og-image.jpg";

              return (
                <Link
                  key={c.id}
                  href={`/${lang}/campo/${c.id}`}
                  className="flex gap-4 bg-white p-3.5 rounded-md border border-slate-200 hover:border-[#2563EB] shadow-sm transition-all no-underline group"
                >
                  <img
                    src={img}
                    alt={nome}
                    className="w-24 h-24 rounded-md object-cover flex-shrink-0 bg-slate-100"
                  />
                  <div className="flex flex-col justify-between flex-1 min-w-0">
                    <div>
                      <span className="inline-block bg-[#2563EB] text-white text-[9px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md mb-1">
                        {isEn && c.categoria_en ? c.categoria_en : c.categoria}
                      </span>
                      <h3 className="text-sm font-black text-[#0F172A] group-hover:text-[#2563EB] truncate m-0">
                        {nome}
                      </h3>
                      <p className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-400 m-0 mt-0.5">
                        📍 {local}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                    <span className="bg-[#F59E0B] text-white text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest px-3 py-1 rounded-md w-fit shadow-sm">
                      {isEn ? "View Camp →" : "Ver Campo →"}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </Link>
              );
            })
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="lg:col-span-8 bg-white rounded-md border-2 border-slate-200 shadow-xl overflow-hidden min-h-[540px] lg:h-[680px]">
          <MapaWrapper campos={camposFiltrados || []} lang={lang} />
        </div>
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}

<file path="app/[lang]/(public)/page.tsx">
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Metadata } from "next";
import { getDictionary } from "@/lib/getDictionary";
import BotaoFavorito from "./components/BotaoFavorito";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

export async function generateMetadata({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ lang: string }>;
}): Promise<Metadata> {
  const { lang } = await params;
  const isEn = lang === "en";

  return {
    title: isEn
      ? "HelloCamp | Find the Best Summer Camps & Youth Programs"
      : "HelloCamp | Os Melhores Campos de Férias",
    description: isEn
      ? "Discover and book unforgettable holiday camps, sports programs, and creative workshops for children and teens."
      : "Explora, aprende e cresce num só lugar. Encontra os melhores campos de férias, colónias desportivas e atividades para os teus filhos.",
  };
}

export default async function HomePage({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ lang: string }>;
}) {
  const { lang } = await params;
  const isEn = lang === "en";
  const dict = await getDictionary(lang as "pt" | "en");

  const todayISO = new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0];

  const { data: todosCamposAtivos } = await supabase
    .from("campos")
    .select(
      "id, nome, nome_en, categoria, categoria_en, Distrito, local, local_en, pais, pais_en, preco, imagem, pacotes, ativo, status_aprovacao"
    )
    .eq("ativo", true);

  const camposValidos = todosCamposAtivos || [];

  const distritosComCampos = new Set(
    camposValidos
      .map((c: any) => (c.Distrito || c.local || "").toLowerCase().trim())
      .filter(Boolean)
  );

  const paisesComCampos = new Set(
    camposValidos
      .map((c: any) => (c.pais || "portugal").toLowerCase().trim())
      .filter(Boolean)
  );

                if (!precoVisivel && pacotesArray.length > 0) {
                  const todosPrecos = pacotesArray.flatMap(

                  );
                  if (todosPrecos.length > 0)

      )}

      {/* PAÍSES (APENAS COM CAMPOS ATIVOS) */}
      {paisesAtivosCards.length > 0 && (

          <div className="max-w-[1280px] mx-auto px-4 md:px-8">

                return (

                      </p>

            </div>

        </section>
      )}

      {/* BLOCO SEO PROGRAMÁTICO */}
      <section className="bg-white py-16 border-t border-slate-200/80">
        <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto px-4 md:px-6">
